50 periya maliyiṭattu muḻakkameṉṟu māṉkūṭṭam añcumpaṭi mika uṟinta ālāṅkaṭṭi kāṟṟōṭu kalantu citaṟutalāl, karumpu vaḷarum kaḻaṉiyuṭiya nāṭukaḷ celvam poḻiyum. (ivvakic) ciṟappaminta ulakikkāttu civanta kiḻakkut ticiyākap pāyum kāviri, aṉṟiyum pūmiyiṟ parakkum āṉporuni, kuṭavaṉāṟu ākiyamūṉṟuñ cērak kūṭiya kūṭṭam aṉiyi. peruma! kolkaḷiṟākiya valiya vil (umiḻum ampukaḷākiya) nīrttuḷiyic cinta, periyapaḻiya ulakil vēlākiya mīṉ pirakācikka, paranta paṇiyākiya muḻavōciyik kēṭṭa (tampakiyi veruvi, uṉṉuṭaṉ naṭpākiya) vēntarkku añcattakka puṉalākiya tārppaṭi araṇākum. maliyiṭattavum, kaṭaliṭattavum piṟa iṭattavumākiya araṇaminta pōrikkaṭantu, kōpitta vīrar malinta pakkattē uḷḷa nāṭṭi akappaṭuttalāl, nalla pukaḻaminta akaṉṟa iṭatti viṭṭōṭiya pakivariṉ kōpamaṟumpaṭi tārppaṭiyi nirappiṉi. ātaliṉ, yāṉ niṉṉi oṉṟu kēṭkiṉṟēṉ; cāntu pularntapiṉ toyyil eḻuti (pala) vaki vaṉappuṟṟa mārpāṟ piṇippuṇṭa, (nī) tēṉī olikkum melliya kūntaluṭiya makaḷiriṉ melliya aṇiyil vativi; vatintu kolkiṉṟa varuttaṅkaḷinta mārpik kavarkiṉṟa muyakkattālē irāppoḻutip payaṉkoṇṭa melliya uṟakkam koḷvi; pakiyiṉ veppattāl pācaṟiyil iruntu varunti aritiṟ peṟṟa ciṟutuyil poruntāta, vāttiyaṅkaḷi ṉiṭiyē caṅku muḻaṅkukiṉṟa celvattiyuṭiya niṉkaṇkaḷ, viriya evvaḷavu kālañ cellumō?
aintām patikam
vaṭavar añcum vāṉtōyum veṟṟikkoṭiyuṭiya kuṭanāṭṭār aracaṉākiya neṭuñcēralātaṟku cōḻaṉ1 maṇakkiḷḷi īṉṟa makaṉ. pattiṉik kaṭavuḷukkuk kaṟkoḷḷavēṇṭi, kāṟṟaṭikkiṉṟa kāṭṭuk kūṭāka ampip pōṟceṉṟu, āriya aracari vīḻtti, pala aruvikaḷ viḻukiṉṟa kaṅkiyil mūḻki, pacukkaḷikkaṉṟōṭu koṇṭu, vil ampu corital māṟāta iṭumpilil taṅki, pulikaḷippōṉṟa vīrarik koṉṟu, neytal pūttuḷḷa viyalūri aḻittu, akkari aṭintu koṭukūri veṉṟu paḻiyaṉ kākkum karuñciṉiyuṭiya vēmpiṉ aṭiyi veṭṭi, pirakācikkiṉṟa āparaṇaṅkaḷik kaḷinta peṇ kaḷiṉ palakariyakūntalāṟṟiritta kayiṟṟāl yāṉiyi vaṇṭiyiṟpūṭṭi; (nēri) vāyilil taṅki, cōḻarkuṭiyiluḷḷa oṉpatu iḷavaracarik koṉṟu, niliyākiya pōriṉ āṟṟali aṟuttuk keṭutaṟkariya tāṉiyōṭu, kaṭal piṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭṭuvaṉik kūttiṉ vikaṟpaṅkaḷi aṟinta kācaṟu ceyyuṭ paraṇar pattup pāṭṭup pāṭiṉār. pāṭip peṟṟaparicil: umpaṟkāṭṭu vāriyiyum, taṉ makaṉ kuṭṭuvaṉ cēraliyum koṭut tāṉakkō kaṭalpiṟakkōṭṭiya ceṅkuṭvaṉ aimpattiyāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ. piṟakkōṭṭutal-piṉṉukkuccellum paṭi ōṭṭutal.


āṟām pattu
51 nī, acikiṉṟa akaṉṟa nīrpparappuk kalaṅkumpaṭi kāṟṟu aṭikka, viḷaṅkum periya tiri iṭipōl muḻaṅkum kaṭaṟkaric cōli yuṭiya (uṉ nakarukku) mēṟkup puṟattuc ceṉṟu, kūvaliṟpaṭiyum vicālitta tāḷuṭiya nāri, kuvinta pūṅkottukaḷuṭiya kōṅkiṉ kiḷi kaḷiṟ ṟaṅkuvatum, vaṇṭukaḷ taṅki uṟivatum, aṭuppaṅkoṭi neruṅkik kariyil aṭikiṉṟatum, naṇṭu āṭiyavaṭuvi kāṟṟu nuṇṇiya maṇali aṭittumāṟṟuvatumākiya valiya kariya paṉi kaḷuṭiya poḻililtaṅki, oppaṉiyāṟ polivu peṟṟu, ulāvi acintu āṭukiṉṟa melliyacāyaluṭiya viṟaliyariṉ teyvamēṟiya vikārattāl ēṟpaṭṭa nuṭakkam pōl tōṉṟi, iṭaṅkaḷ tōṟum ariya maṇiyuṭiya pāmpu tiriyum imayamaliyum, caṅku kumuṟum paṉikkaṭalum1 kiḻakkum mēṟkum kaṭalum ākiya iṭattē uḷḷa aracarum piṟarum cēvittaṟkup poruntiṉa pantarivēyntāṟpōl muṟukkaviḻnta vaḷaviya neytaṟ pūvāṟṟoṭutta mālikaḷi īramākiya tēṉ nāṟum paṭi tūkki, cuṭarnutalum, melliya cāyalum, vāḷ nakiyum, pirakācik kiṉṟa paṟkaḷum, amiḻtam poḻintu civanta vāyum, acikiṉṟa naṭiyumuṭiya viṟaliyar pāṭal miḻaṟṟa puṟattu viṉiyiṉmiyiṉ viṉōtattilē kūṭi uṟintaṉi. nī avviṉōtattilē nīṭi uṟital aṟiyātu ‘pirakācikkiṉṟa vēluṭiya aṇṇal melliyaṉ pōlum’ eṉa niṉ perumi uṇarātōr eṇṇuvarō? nītāṉ kōpamuṭiya aravōṭokkum pakivarik kaṭuka aḻikka vēṇṭum nilimiyil, maḻi tavaḻumperuṅ kuṉṟattu av varaviviṉi aḻikkum uru mēṟṟiṉi oppi (avvāṟu viriyac ceyyum nilimikkaṇ niṉak kēṟpa) niṉ paṭivaḻi vāḻnarum kālāṭkaḷ mēṟ cella etirkkanēriṉ yāṉikaḷatu kōṭukaḷi veṭṭum vāḷuṭiya valiyarkaḷāyiruppar. avvāṟu nī aḻiyātu māṟupāṭāṟṟip porutaḻikkumvaḻi nī puṉinta vīramporuntiya veḷḷiya paṉi māli utiram teṟittu niṟam peyartaliṉ ataṉip paruntu kavartaṟku virumpi vaṭṭamiṭum; vaṭṭamiṭa, niṉmuṉṉar olikkum periya kaṇṇuṭiya taṇṇumiyiṉ kaṇṇi niṉṉetirniṉṟu māṟṟār eyta ampu kiḻittalāl; taṇṇumi koṭṭum iḷiñar taṇṇumi koṭṭā toḻivar; taṭuttaṟkariya cīṟṟamuṭiya periya kūṟṟam vali virittāṟ pōla kaḷattil etirtta māṟṟār paṭiyi yellām oṉṟākak kollak karuti nōkkiṉ nōkkiṉiyuṭiyi. neṭunta kāy! ceruvakattu nī avvāṟu kaṭiyun taṉmiyuṭiyi.
52 ariya paṇṭaṅkaḷik koṇṭu varuvōr nīriṭattē mitakkaviṭṭa periya āravāramuṭiya vaṅkam ticikaḷiṟ ṟirivatupōl, koṭi aci yumpaṭi niṟkum kolkiṉṟa yāṉikaḷ neruṅkic cella, teḷḷiya ōciyuṭiya maṇikaḷ aciyum neṭiya tērip pakippulattē parappi, mipūciya mikap periya paricikaḷiyum, kavacattiyum matiyātu vāḷ vēl mutaliyavaṟṟit tāṅkip pōrukku muṉṉaṇiyiṟ cellum koṭiya kaṇṇuṭiya vīrar, pakivar ōyviṉṟip pōr ceytaliṉ vīra cuvarkkam aṭivar. ivvāṟu palarpaṭa nallapōrveṉṟa iṭiyō ṭotta vicālitta niṉki irappōrkkuk kavitalallatu irattaṟkuk kavital aṟiyā eṉakkēḷviyuṟṟōm. acikiṉṟa māliyum, paranta tēmalum, kuḷirnta imiyum, maḻikkaṇṇum pēraḻaku muṭiyaḷ niṉtēvi. cuṭarviṭukiṉṟatum, celvam viḷaṅku kiṉṟatumākiya pāṇṭil viḷakku eriya, muḻā olikkun tuṇaṅkik kūttukku, mukkāramiṭum valiya ēṟṟippōlat tuṇiyākat taḻuvit taṉṉic cēvikkum makaḷiroṭu kikōttu nī kuraviyāṭic ceṟitalikkaṇṭu, niṉtēvi varuntuvaḷ. varuntippirakācikkiṉṟa pūvitaḻi otta ciṟiya aṭiyiṭattaṉa vākiya kiṇkiṇikaḷ ciṟiya paraṭṭi alippa, kolkiṉṟa puṉaliluḷḷa taḷirpōla naṭuṅki niṉṟu niṉṉi eṟiya ōṅkiya ciṟiya ceṅku vaḷiyittā eṉa nī irappavum īyāḷāy ataṉikkoṇṭu, nī emakku yār? eṉak kūṟip peyarvāḷ avvāṟu irantu nī peṟātu, avaḷic caṭutiyiṟ kōpitta nōkkattōṭu, ati avaḷ pāṉiṉṟum pirittuk koḷḷamāṭṭāyāyiṉi. avvāṟu ataṉik koḷḷamāṭṭāta nī, akaṉṟa periya vāṉil pakaṟpoḻutiṟ celvōric cuṭum katirviṭum kōpamuṭiya ñāyiṟṟippōṉṟa niṟamuṭiya vāṉṟōy veṇ kuṭi vēntar tam eyilip pakuttuk kōṭalyāṅku valliyāyiṉāy. niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻvatāka.
53 nī veṉṟu (eṅkaḷukku) āparaṇaṅkaḷit tarutaṟku, virumpiya iṭattē taṅkiyapōtu (tōṟṟa) vēntar ‘emakkum pōtiyaḷavu koṭuttaṉi’ eṉakkūṟic cōrāta varuvāyuṭiya nāṭukaḷit tiṟiyākak koṭuppar. ataṉip peṟṟa nī avarkku aruḷpurivi. ciṉam poruntiya taliva! uyarnta malikaḷuṭiya ulakil kaṭalāṟcūḻappaṭṭa paḻiya pukaḻaminta niṉ mūtūriṭattē celkuviyāyiṉ (cellum neṟiyiṉ) vaḷinta vaḻi yāṟ celvāyāka appaṭic cellutaṟkuk kāraṇam yāteṉil, kaṇiyamaram puṟattē cērkkappaṭṭatum irumputtakaṭukaḷāṟ piṇikkap paṭṭatumākiya kūṭṭamākiya maraṅkaḷi niṟuttic ceyyappaṭṭa niliyiṉ vāyiṟ katavukaḷik kāṇiṉ, matampāyum cuvaṭuṭiyatum, valiya kiyit tūkki vēṅkiyiveṉṟatumākiya niṉkaḷiṟu poṟiyuṭiya pukar neṟṟiyiluyarnta kiyic curuṭṭit tōṭṭiyi vilakki, uyarnta veṟṟik 1koṭi aciyattāṅka māṭṭātu. tāṅkavēṇṭuvatēl civanta puḷḷikaḷiṭṭa cilampōṭu aḻakiya taḻikaḷ tūṅkum ēntira amip puṭiya ampukkaṭṭukaḷ tūṅkumvāyilum kolit toḻiluṭiya valiyamutali tiriyum āḻnta akaḻum, vāṉatti muṭṭum vaḷinta aḻakiya matilumuṭiya, poruntāta pakivar muṉikeṭap porutu niṉṉāṟ koṇṭu piṟarkkuk koṭukkappaṭṭa aracar eyil mukattu (avyāṉikaḷic celuttuvatallatu) niṉmuṉṉōr ōmpiya eyil mukattuc celuttutal etaṟku?
54 periya pācaṟiyiṉ naṭuvil kaṟaṅkum olikkiṉṟa kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya muraciṉ ōci pācaṟi eṅkum paṭarntu paṭiyi ēvi olippa, taṇṭā yutattiyuṭiya pukaḻaminta valiyōr iḷiyarkaḷi aṇivakuppukku ēvit tāmum ēval ceyyum pakivaratu2 yāṉappaṭiyik kāṇiṉ nillātatāṉiyuṭiya talimiyi uṭiyōy! niṉṉi vaḷḷiya ṉeṉṟu yāvarum kūṟutalāṉē niṉṉik kāṇavantēṉ. yāṉ vaḷḷiya taṉi nī muṭippāyākavēṇṭum. niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻvatāka. vīṅkiya kaṇuvuṭiya perutta mūṅkil pōṉṟa periya tōḷum, uyarnta aḻakiya kuḷirnta kaṇṇum, pūntoḻilaminta tukiluṭuttiya ariyum, tēṉāṟukiṉṟa kūntalum, pirakācikkiṉṟa āparaṇamumuṭiya viṟaliyar niṉ vīrattip pāṭavum, iravalar varuttam nīṅkavum nāḷtōṟum ciṟanta nalla āparaṇaṅkaḷi varivillātu vīcuvi. nī attaṉmiyi ākalāṉ, eṉpōlum iravalaratu ākkattiṉ poruṭṭuc ciṟitu kālamum ivvulakattiṉiṉṟu uyar nili ulakattiṟ cellātē ivviru nilamaruṅkilē neṭuṅkālam nilipeṟuvāyāka.
55 perumi taṅkiyavaḷatu kaṇava! pulavarkaḷatu vēntē! niṉṉi virumpi vantēṉ; kolkiṉṟa pōri virumpum koṟṟava! iṉiya ōciyuṭiya tiri olikkum kaṭaliṭattu vanta nalla paṇṭaṅkaḷākiya celvam kiṭak kum paṇṭacālikaḷil tāḻi kamaḻum kariyiyum, periya tuṟiyi yum, taṇṇiya kaṭaṟkarit tōṭṭaṅkaḷiyu muṭiya nalla nāṭṭiṉ araca! arittukkaritta miyāl taṉṉiṟpukka cevvūṉtōṉṟāta avari tuvari mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ cuviyiyum, veṇṇiṟa ūṉiyum cōṟṟukkuppatil uṇṇum maṟavar kavacamē.! kuṭanāṭṭavar kōvē! koṭit tēraṇṇāl.! iravalar varārāyiṉum tērip pōkki avari atil varappaṇṇi avarkku uṇavu nalkum, viruppam mikunta vārttiyiyum, pukaḻiyumuṭiya tōṉṟal! peruma! tiṉantiṉam akaṉṟa iṭattiluḷḷār tārppaṭi aḻintu alaṟa nīṇṭamali aṭukku kaḷiyuṭiya nāṭukaḷik kikkoṇṭu porutu ciṉantaṇinta pōr aṟikūvum āṇmiyum, etirttavarkaḷic cititta oḷḷiya vāḷum uṭiya uṟcākam poruntiya kuricil! niṉ vāḻnāḷ, pala āṇṭukaḷāka vēṇṭiya kālattuppeytu ulakik kākkum mukili ottu, eḵkiṉa pañcupōla veḷuttup poṅki mēleḻuntu vāṉic cērnta (veṇ) mukili ovvātu oḻivatāka.
56 veṟṟi muracam āravārippa uyarttiyavāḷum, ilaṅkum pūṇum, poṉṉuḻiñiyum uṭiyaṉāy, aṟiyāmi mikutiyāṟ pakittup pōkkeḻunta māṟṟu vēntar tamatumeymaṟanta veṟṟic celvam māyntu oḻiyum pōrkkaḷattu āṭuṅkō, viḻākkoṇṭāṭum periya nakariṭattu kūttar muḻaviṉmuṉ āṭavallāṉallaṉ. avaṉ kaṇṇi vāḻvatāka.
57 iḷiya putalvar aḷitta vaḷam poruntiya cilampum, aṭaṅkiya koḷ kiyum, āṉṟa aṟivum, tōṉṟiya nalla pukaḻum uṭiya oṇṇutaṉ makaḷiratu aḻuta kaṇṇiṉum iravalar varuttatti añcum kāttaṟ ṟoḻili etirkoḷvōṉi, 1cilavaḷikaḷi aṇinta viṟali, pāṇarkiyiluḷḷa iciyaminta pālip pēriyāḻiṉ narampit tirutti taḻiñcip pāṭalkaḷi iṉṉiciyāṟpāṭi varakkaṇṭōm. melliya vaḻiyiṟ ciṟiya aṭikaḷi otukkic celvōmō. ap pātukāppi etir koḷvōṉākiya tuṇaṅkiyāṭiya valampaṭu kōmāṉ (ippōtutāṉ avvetir kōṭṭiṟkēṟpa) varuvāyiṉi yuṭiya kuṟivupaṭāta valiyuṭiya maṟavar valicāyaperiya paṉimāli yoṭu periya kaḻal civappak kurutipaṉikkum pulāl niṟinta kaḷat tōṉāyirukkiṉṟāṉ; kāṇpāyāka.
58 veḷḷiyapaṉan tōṭṭiṉmēl aḻakiya kuvaḷippūkkaḷi aṇin tōrum vāḷiṉvāy uṇṭākkiya māṭcimippaṭṭa variyākiya taḻumpu uṭiyarumākiya maṟavar, kolipuriyum paṭikkalaṅkaḷit tāṅkuvar. tāṅki ‘iṉṟu iṉitu uṇṭaṉamāyiṉ nāḷi maṇṇiṉāṟ ceyta vaḷinta matilik kaṭantu uṭpukuntallatuuṇṇamāṭṭō meṉṟu (tāṅkaḷ cūṭiya) poṟ kaṇṇikkēṟṟa viṉipuriyum vīrar perumakaṉē! poy pēcutal aṟiyāta pirakācikkiṉṟa nāvum, eyili eṟiyum valiya villum, ampum, kavacamum viḷaṅkukiṉṟa uyarnta aḻakiya mārpumuṭiya cāṉṟōṉākiya vāṉavarampaṉi, kāṉakattu, kāṟṟāṭiyiṉ ōci pōl ciḷvaṇṭukaḷ iruntu olikkum porukkuṭiya ariyum ciṟiya iliyumuṭiya periya vēla maraṅkaḷ vaḷarum pulliya pulaṅkaḷi vittum valiya kiyuṭiya viṉiñar tam kalappi ceṉṟa koḻuvaḻi maruṅkil acikiṉṟa katiruṭiya maṇikaḷip peṟum akaṉṟa iṭa muṭiya nāṭṭiyuṭiyāṉ eṉṟu colluvārkaḷ. (avaṉ avvāṟu celvakkuṟiyilaṉātalāl, attarattiṟ kēṟpa namakku vēṇṭuvaṉa tarutaliṟ kuṟivuṭiyaṉallaṉ; vantamikkēṟpa) viṟaliyīr āṭalikkuṟiyaṟac celuttumiṉ; paricilarāyuḷḷīr nīyirum arum kavikaḷip pāṭik kivarappaṇṇumiṉ.
59 pakaṟpoḻutu nīḷātu iravu nīṇṭu mācit taṉmi peruki, mākkaḷ kuḷirāl uḷvaḷiyum mātattil, kuḷirnta curavaḻiyiṟ cellum pāṇaṉ uvappavum, pulliya iruḷ akalavum, ñāyiṟu pal katir parappik kiḻakkut ticiyiṟ ṟōṉṟiyatupōla, iravalaratu ciṟu kuṭikaḷ celvattāṟ peruka ulakamtāṅkiya mēlāṉa kalviyuṭiya villōr meymmaṟi! celvarkkuc celva! cērntārkkaraṇam! palvēṟu vakip paṭṭa akaṉṟa iṭattil kuvintu kiṭakkum, maliyiṭattavum kaṭaliṭattavu mākiya paṇṭaṅkaḷip pakuttu (iravalarkku) aḷikkum muṟiyil muṭṭuṟātu, aṟam purintoḻukum viruppāṟ ciṟanta valiya paṇi otta niṉ tōḷuk kēṟpa matiyātu, etirttu valiyiṟ kuṟiyuṟṟa pakivar paṇintu perumi ciṟanta āparaṇaṅkaḷit tiṟi iṭuvarāyiṉ niṉ ciṉam cellat taṇiyumō? niṉ kaṇṇi vāḻvatāka.
60 vaṇṭukaḷ puṟattē mūcavum tīñcuvi māṟātatum, arampōḻamāṭṭāta tum, marattiṟ paḻuttatumākiya aḻakiya cēṟu aminta muṭṭipōṉṟu paḻutta paḻaṅkaḷ vaḻi cellummākkaṭkuc cellutalit taṭukkum. (vaḻiccelvōr appaḻaṅkaḷi āyvatil nērattip pōkkuvar eṉṟavāṟu). ivvāṟu māṟāta viḷivuṭiya āṟāta putuvaruvāyuṭiya avaṉ, ampu toṭutteytaliṉ maṭinta villik kaḷinta maṟavar,1 poṅku tuḷikaḷuṭiya tiri maṅkuṟ poḻutoṭu mayaṅka varum kaṭalūti paṉikkiṉṟa uṇṇā naṟavil (naṟavu eṉṉumūril) melliya luṭiya peṇkaḷiṉ kūṭṭattiṉaṉ. iṉittāṉi, koliviṉi mēviṟṟu ākalāl, tāṉ pōrttoḻili mēvalaṉ. pāṇmakaḷē! nām avaṉik kāṇutaṟkuc celvēmō; celliṉ kuṟivuṟātu (namakku vēṇṭiyavaṟṟi) vīcuvāṉ.
āṟām patikam
kuṭakkō neṭuñcēralātaṟku, vēḷāvikkōmāṉ tēvi īṉṟa makaṉ taṇṭāraṇiyattār kavarntu ceṉṟa kāṭṭāṭṭit tōṇṭiyiṟ koṇarntu koṭuppittup, pārppārkkukkapiliyōṭu kuṭanāṭṭi luḷḷa ōrūri īntu, vāṉavarampaṉ eṉṉum peyar iṉitu vaḻaṅkap peṟṟu, maṟṟiya vīrarip pōriṭattē oṭukki, aracari oṭṭi kuḻantiyik kāppāṟṟun tāypōlak kuṭikaḷip pātukāttu, viruppañ ciṟanta nalla neñcuṭiya āṭu kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉi, ceyyuṭ pāṭum aṭaṅkiya koḷkiyuṭiya kākkipāṭiṉiyār nac ceḷḷiyār pattup pāṭṭup pāṭiṉār.
pāṭip peṟṟa paricil: kalaṉaṇika veṉṟu avarkku oṉpatukāp poṉ ṉum nūṟāyiram kāṇamum koṭuttut taṉ pakkattuk koṇṭāṉakkō. āṭu kōṭpāṭṭuc cēralātaṉ muppat teṭṭiyāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ.


ēḻām pattu
61 puṇpaṭṭa vāypōlap paḻuttu viḻunta palāppaḻaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟu mōṭu kiṉṟatēṉ nāṟum nāṭum, veṟṟiyumuṭiyavaṉum, cittirattippōṉṟu alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa pāviyippōṉṟa nallavaḷatu kaṇavaṉum, poṉ pōṉṟa pūvum ciṟiya iliyum pulliya aṭiyumuṭiya uṉṉatti uṭiyavaṉum, pakivarkku valiyaṉumāṉākiya ematu talivaṉum, pūcip pularntacāntuṭiyavaṉum, pularāta īkiyuṭiyavaṉum, malarnta mārpuṭiyavaṉumākiya periya koṭiyiyuṭiya pāri, muracam maṇṇiṭattē kiṭantu kāyavumiravalarvaruntavum, tirumpivarāta toliviṭattē ceṉṟāṉ. oḷḷiyavāḷum, valiya kaḷiṟum, cantiraṉ pōṉṟu veḷḷiya vēlumuṭiya num vīrar pōrceytu puṇpaṭṭa mikutiyāl pulāl nāṟukiṉṟatumākiya pācaṟiyiṉkaṇ, pāṭiṉi muḻaviṉ tāḷattiṟ kēṟpak kiyi acikkiṉṟa viḻāvi otta niṉ vinōta makiḻcci yiṉkaṇ, nī īvāyāka eṉṟu colli irakkaveṉṟu vantu cila pukaḻntu collukiṉṟēṉumallēṉ; allatu uṇmi yallātaṉa vaṟṟip pukaḻntu collukiṉṟēṉumallēṉ. 1ītaṟku iraṅkātumīyun tōṟumakiḻātum koṭukkiṉṟa periya koṭiyāḷi eṉṟu ulakam kūṟum appāri kuṇaṅkaḷākiya nalla pukaḻi niṉpāl taravantēṉ.
62 pala aṇikaḷip puṉintu celkiṉṟa pala yāṉik kūṭṭattōṭum, mukil eṉṟu añcumpaṭiyāṉa mikap periya paricikaḷōṭum, kūriya kattarikkōlāl piṭari mayir maṭṭañceytakutirikaḷōṭum, matiliṉ pakkaṅkaḷ aḻiyumpaṭi vaḷittu vantu puṟattē taṅkiyatāṉi koḷuviya paciya poṟikaḷic cintum pirakācikkiṉṟa aḻal, (ulakam kaṭal koṇṭu kiṭanta ukāntakālattu akkaṭal nīrellām vaṟṟa eṟittaṟkut tōṉṟum) putumiyōṭu kūṭi (annīr vaṟṟumpaṭi) pollā mayakkamum pēroliyum ceytu tirikiṉṟa vaṭavittīyiṉ vaṇṇattik 2koḷḷutaṟkuk kāraṇamāy niṉṟa mikka valimiyum, muṟṟakkaṟṟa paṭikkalappayiṟciyumuṭiya veṟṟivēntē! puṉal ali mōtukiṉṟa akaḻiṭattu malipōṉṟa matili araṇākavuṭiya accantarum vicālittakiyuṭiya niṉpakivar (tammik) kākkumpaṭi kūṟip paṇintu tiṟitaruvarāyiṉ, matuvaruntuvōrum, valiyakiyuṭiya varum aruviyiṭattuḷḷa āmpalic cūṭiya taliyiyuṭiyōru mākiya toḻilāḷar, pulluṭiya periyaveḷiyilpalamāṭṭu niri kaḷip parappi mēyaviṭṭu vaḷam poruntiya vayalil viḷintatum, vikkōluṭaṉarintuparantukiṭappatum, kaḷattiṟkaṭā viṭutaṟṟoḻi laṟṟatumākiya poliyi kāñci marattiṉ kīḻ poṟukkic cērttu, āṭu kiṉṟa ciṟakuṭiya tēṉīkkaḷ ōṭṭum niṉ akaṉṟa iṭattiyuṭiya nāṭu pukaḻ amintatu.
63 nī pārppārkkallatu paṇitali aṟiyāy. paṇiyāta uḷḷattōṭu aḻaku poruntiya naṇpiṉarukkallatu kaṇ añcutal aṟiyāy. vaḷinta villāṟ porukiṉṟa uṉatu maṇaṅkamaḻkiṉṟa mārpi (niṉṉōṭu poruvāriṉmiyiṉ) niṉmakaḷir pōkattuk kallatu malar vittali aṟiyāy. ulakam māṟupaṭum kālamāyiṉum coṉṉa coṟpiḻittal aṟiyāy. (ivi niṉ iyalpu.), iviyēyaṉṟi ciṟiya iliyuṭiya uḻiñi māli cūṭik koḷḷi atikappaṭamāṟṟāratu tamiḻppaṭi kaḷi yellām iṭi veḷiyillāmaṟ koṉṟuparappi, maliyi nilikulikkum iṭiyēṟṟippōl cīṟi, orumittu vaḷittuc cōḻa pāṇṭiyariruvariyu mōṭṭiya vāṭpōrilmēmpaṭukiṉṟa tāṉiyi yuṭiya vellukiṉṟa pōriyuṭiyōy! muṉpiṟar pāl veṟṟi peṟṟu niṉakku aḻinta māṟṟār niṉṉōṭu pakimāṟi, iṉṟumutal niṉṉālē paṭikkappaṭṭāṟ pōl meṉṟu tāḻvukūṟa, ataṟku ēṟpa nīyum, niṉperumiyum kaṇṇōṭṭamumākiya num nukaṅ koṇṭu iṉṉum veṟṟi mikuntaṉi. (niṉkuṇaṅkaḷ ivvāṟākiya ataṉāṉē) celvakkōvē! cērar maruka! vāḻvāyāka! kāṟṟiṉāl eḻum tirikaḷ muḻaṅkum ōciyuṭiya (kaṭali) elliyāka vuṭiya periya iṭatti yuṭiya ulakam ceyta naṉmi uṇṭeṉil, pala kēṭillāta aṭi aṭuttal aṟiyāta pala āmpalākiya (eṇṇuḷḷa) āyiraveḷḷa ūḻi vāḻka.
64 veṟṟi muracuṭiyōrum (kūriya) vāyuṭiya vāṭpōril veṟṟiyuṭi yōrum poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷi aṇintōrumākiya vēntar ulakiṟ palarāvar. avarāṟpeṟum payaṉ eṉ? aṟam kūṟip pirakācamaṭinta nāvum, uyarttic collappaṭum vēḷviyi muṭitta kalviyumuṭiya antaṇar ariya āparaṇaṅkaḷip peṟṟaṉar. nīr paṭukiyiṉāl mikac cēṟāṭiyatāl kaḷiṟu niṟṟaṟku veṟukkum muṟṟamuṭiyatum āṇṭu vāḻvārkkallatu piṟarkkup pukutaṟkariya oḻuṅkuṭiyatumākiya māḷikiyiṉ puṟattuk kūttari (nīṅkaḷ kāṇiṉum) virivil, kūriyakat tarik kōlāl maṭṭañ ceyyappaṭṭa piṭarimayiruṭiya kutirikaḷi, avi iḻukkum vaṇṭikaḷuṭaṉ āparaṇaṅkaḷāl alaṅkarittu īmiṉeṉṟu (aṅkuḷḷārkku)k kaṭṭaḷi ceytu niṉ ūriṭattu nīṅkāta īyumkōṭpāṭuṭiya ṉāyiruppi. ātalāl, kariyaparantakaḻiyiṭattu malarnta neytaliṉ itaḻpōṉṟa aḻakiya tōṟṟattippōl uyarnta mukiliṉum periya payaṉ poḻivāyāka. aṇṇal! tōṉṟāl! paci yuṭiya cuṟṟattiṉ varuttaṅkaḷinta pukaḻaminta pācaṟiyiṭattē, ākāyattil pala mīṉkaḷiṉ oḷikeṭa ñāyiṟu tōṉṟiyāṅku paki variṉ māṟupāṭṭi aḻitta niṉ valiya tāḷkaḷi vāḻttum poruṭṭu niṉṉik kāṇavantēṉ.
65 vīḻntukiṭakkum piṇaṅkaḷi iṭaṟutalāl irattak kaṟi paṭṭa kuḷampum vēkamumuṭiya nalla kutirikaḷukku virintataliyāṭṭamaṇintu, etirttu malintapakivar vīram keṭumpaṭi porutavarum, eppoḻutum niliyāmiyi uḷḷattiṟ koṇṭirukkum (atāvatu eppoḻutum pōril uyir viṭa āyattamāyirukkum) peruma! vil vīrarkaḷatu kavacamē! cērntōrkkuc celva! āparaṇaṅkaḷa aṇinta perutta alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa iḷa muliyum, malarnta nōkkum, mūṅkilpōṉṟu paṇittu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa iṟiyuṭiya perutta tōḷum, aḻakiya kaṭavuḷarum āḷkiṉṟa kaṟpum, tūrattē naṟumaṇam vīcum nalla neṟṟiyum iḷamiyumuṭiya āparaṇaṅkaḷaṇintavaḷatu kaṇava! pāṇar puravala! paricilar celvam! mārpiṭattu puṇkaḷ viḷaṅkutalāl pukaḻcāṉṟa mārpa; niṉ ōlakka iruppiṉ ciṟappiyellām, iṉiyanaram puṭiya pāli yāḻi mīṭṭalil vallōṉ, pālippaṇkaḷi ellām orōvoṉ ṟākap peyarttu vācikkumāṟupōla oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu ovvāta iṉpatti uṇṭavarkkuk koṭukkum, (nilattic) cēṟu ceykiṉṟa māripōlaḷikkum viḻāviṉ taṉmi poruntiya kaḷḷuvarkkaṅkaḷiṉ makiḻcciyāl iṉitu kaṇṭēm.
66 vaḷinta kariya kōṭuṭiya iṉiya ōcipaḻutta perumiyuṭiya periya yāḻiṭattil pālippaṇṇi vācittup (paricil peṟum) niṉi vuṭaṉ cellum pāṭippaṭinta vāyuṭiya iravala! pakivaratu valiyit tāṅkiya avarāṟ kulittaṟkariya oḻuṅkuṭiya paṭivakuppiṉiyum, paruva maḻikkālattu maliyiṉ koṭumuṭikaḷip pōṉṟa parici kaḷōṭu mēlē uyarntu virintilaṅkukiṉṟa vēlkaḷiyum uṭiya maṉṉar, pūmālikaḷ acintāṟ pōṉṟa acikiṉṟa vāṭpōriṭattup paṭuvar. ammaṟavar aṇiyum paṉantōṭuṭaṉ veṟṟimātuṟiyum vākiyiṉ pañcu pōṉṟa malariyum viravittoṭutta māliyip pōṉṟu pūtta mulliyic cūḻntutiriyum vaṇṭu kāṭṭiṭattiluḷḷa piṭaviṉ māli pōṉṟa pūṅkottil taṅkum. (ivvakiyāṉa kāṭṭil) aḻakiya paḷiṅkip parappiṉatu pōṉṟa civanta parukkik kaṟkaḷuṭiya uyarnta nilattē katirviṭṭilaṅkukiṉṟa tirumaṇi kiṭikkum akaṉṟa iṭattiyuṭiya nāṭṭukkuriyōṉ, iṭiyiṉ ōciyuṭiya muracattōṭu vañciṉaṅkūṟi vēli uṭiya (pakivar) kūṭṭattiṉar pōr citiyum paṭi veṭṭik koṉṟu puṟaṅkoṭuttōṭac ceyta piṇam kuvinta kaḷattup, pakivar, tiṟiyākat tantayāṉikaḷōṭu marakkālāl aḷak kappaṭum nellākiya uṇaviyum pikaḷiṟ pōṭa muṭiyāta aḷavu (iravalarukku nalkuvāṉ) eṉa ellārum collu vārkaḷ. ātalāl (iravala) nī avaṉ pāl ēku.
67 pantar eṉṉum peyaruṭiya periya pukaḻaminta mūtūrkku (āṟṟum) kaṭaṉaṟinta marapiluḷḷa (yāḻmīṭṭal mattaḷaṅ koṭṭal) mutaliyavaṟṟil valla kiyiyuṭiya pāṇa! (paricil vaḻaṅkuvārmītu) pukaḻcci uri kaḷikkūṟum cuṟṟattōṭu nī, kollukiṉṟa paṭikkalaṅkaḷ tōṉṟavum veṟṟikkoṭi aciyavum, pirakācikkiṉṟa kompukaḷōṭu valampuric caṅku ārppavum, pala yāṉik kūṭṭaṅkaḷ iṭam peyarntu tiriyavum, pōr ceyyumiṭattiṟku iṭampōtāta pirakācikkiṉṟa niṇam niṟinta parappil kūṭṭamākiya ciṟakuṭiya eruvip paṟavikaḷ irattaṅkuṭikka vum, tali aṟuppuṇṭu eñciya āṇmi niṟinta uṭaṟ kuṟikaḷōṭu uruvillāta pēy makaḷ kaṇṭārkku varuttañ ceyyavum, nāṭu uṭaṉ naṭuṅkumpaṭi palapōrveṉṟu, koṉṟiyiṉ nāṟukiṉṟa pūṅkottu kaḷuṭaṉ veḷḷiya (paṉantōṭi viravittoṭutta) variyuṭiya māli yaṇintavarum, vāḷmukattil veṭṭiya māṭcimippaṭṭa uṭampi uṭiyavarum, vaḷinta periya taliyum nēriya kompum periya uṭalumuṭiya erutupōṉṟa tāḻnta valiya maṭiyar irumpuk kampiyiṟ kōttup porikkum iṟicciyippōla uṭampu citintu vaṭuk kaḷālē pūciṉa cāntiṉ aḻaku maṟiyum vīrarkaḷumākiya avarkaḷatu perumakaṉum, teyvattiṉ viruppattukkuriyateṉa aṟintum malarnta kāntaḷi tavaṟātu ūtiya vēkamākap paṟakkum vaṇṭu, paṟikaḷ (pōriṉmiyāl muḻakkātu) paṇṇaḻintu kiṭakkum perumicāṉṟa uyarnta pāṟikaḷuṭiya nērimalip porunaṉumākiya celvak kōmāṉip pāṭic celvāyāka. celliṉ teḷḷiya kaṭal muttōṭu koṭu maṇam eṉṉum ūri veṉṟupeṟṟa nalla āparaṇaṅkaḷiyum, peṟukuvi.
68 maṇṇiṉāṟ kaṭṭiya uyarnta vaḷinta matiluṭiya nakariṭattuc cittiratti otta (tamatu illiṉ) neṭiya cuvaril, (talivaṉ viṉi vaḻippirintuvarāmaṟ) kaḻinta nāṭkaḷ palavaṟṟiyum nittiriyiṉmi yiṉ paciya āparaṇaṅkaḷ nekiḻumpaṭikīṟikkuṟittalāl civantaviralkaḷ civappēṟiya, uḷḷiṭumaṇikaḷiyuṭiya cilampaṇinta teyvamātar pōṉṟu aḻakiya peṇkaḷippiṇikkum maṇamkamaḻkiṉṟamārpa! (niṉtāḷ niḻalōr) kāṟṟu aṭittaliṉ kaṭal olittal pōla māṟṟār nilattiṟ koṇṭa pācaṟiyiṉ naṭuvē, kaṭiya oliyi eḻuppum taḻaṅkum kuraluṭiya muracam akaṉṟa periyavāṉil atira, accantarum varikaḷ iṭappaṭṭa (pakivar) matili eṟintallatu uṇṇēm eṉṟukūṟi oṉṟiṉ piṉ oṉṟākap pala nāṭkaḷ kaḻiyā niṟka uṟcākam uṭiyarāy, uṭampi vāṭṭum varuttamuṭiya pakivartaṅkum araṇittāmkip paṟṟiṉaṉṟi, iṉiya kaḷ viṟṟakku aṭiyāḷamākak koṭikaṭṭap paṭṭa vītiyiṟpukuntu ariya kaḷḷiṉ vilikkup paki vēntaṉ ūrukiṉṟa yāṉiyiṉ veṇkōṭṭik koṭuttapiṉ (uṇṭu) makiḻntu tuṉpam iṭiviravātu iṉpamākavē celkiṉṟa vāḻkkiyiyum eppoḻutu muṭiya, vaṭa pūmiyākiya uttara kuruviṟ palanāḷ taṅkap peṟuvarō? (peṟār). (avar avvāṟu uttarakuruviṟ peṟum iṉpattippeṟi ṉaṉṟi, niṉ mārpāṟ piṇikkappaṭṭa ariviyarum iṉiyanaki tiṉamum poruntiya pala nāṭkaḷip peṟuvatētu).
69 mali ottayāṉiyum vāṉi muṭṭum veṟṟik koṭiyum, mali mītuḷḷa aruvi pōl muṟi muṟiyē aciyavum, kaṭal pōṉṟa tāṉiyum, koṭiyakuraluṭiya muracamum, kāṟṟaṭikkum kaṭal pōl muḻaṅkavum, (pakivaratu) ārāyntu eṭutta veṟṟi vīrarōṭu, veṭṭik kūr maḻuṅkiya vāyuṭiya vāḷiyuṭiya vīrariyum, ili pōṉṟa vēliyuṭiya vīrariyum, terinteṭutta pāykiṉṟa kutirip paṭiyiyum, piṇaṅkaḷ vīḻntu kuviyumpaṭi, koṉṟu (keṭṭup pōṉa appakivaratu) kuṭikaḷi an nāṭṭil vāḻumpaṭi aruḷ ceyta veṟṟi vēntē! ilaṅkukiṉṟa cuṭar viṭum cakkarattiyuṭiya niṉ muṉṉōr, (niccayamāka) niṉṉip pōl acivillāta koḷkiyuṭiyar ātaliṉ, (poṉṉulakamaṉṟi) maṉṟaṅkaḷuṭiya immaṇṇulakam muḻuvatum payaṉip poḻintāṟ pōṉṟum cūriyaṉ ciṉantaṇintāṟ pōṉṟum tōṉṟa (mātam mummāri peyyavum), veḷḷi maḻikku uṭalāṉa maṟṟi nāḷ kōḷkaḷukkuc celkiṉṟa nalla nāṭkaḷilē niṟkavum vicumpiṭattē uḷḷamaḻi, ivvulakiṉiyāṉē purappē ṉeṉṟu ēṟa viṭṭuk koṇṭu niṉṟāṟ pōla niṟpavum, nāluvēṟu ticikaḷum pakiyiṉṟi viḷaṅkavum (niṉpukaḻellām uḷavāka) aciviṉṟi āṇṭōrāvar.
70 kaḷiṟṟic celuttiyatāḷum, kutiriyi varuttiya pātamum, (māṟṟār) pōrit tolitta vēlum, maliyi alitta (valiya) tōḷum, villi alittavaliyum uṭiyavarum, vaṇṭu icipāṭāta kuḷirnta iḷampaṉiyiṉ kuvinta kūriya veḷḷiya kuruttōṭu iṉiya cuṉi nīril malarnta malarit toṭutta māliyi aṇintavarum, matattiṉāl cerukkiyuṭikiṉṟa nilimiyiṉiyumuṭiya (pakivaratu) pōri veṉṟu vīrattikkeṭuttu kaṭiya ciṉamuṭiya aracariṉ ceruk koḻittavarum, nakikkāyiṉum poykūṟāta vāymiyum pakivar puṟañ coṟkēḷāta kuṟṟantīrnta pukaḻuṭiyarumākiya, periya vīrakkaḻa laṇinta vīrar peruma! meṉmikkuṇaṅkaḷ ciṟantu periya maṭam nili peṟṟa kaṟpup poruntiya naṟumaṇaṅkamaḻum cuṭarviṭum neṟṟiyuṭiya kuṟṟan tīrntavaḷatu kaṇava! pūṇkaḷ pirakācikkiṉṟa mārpa! niṉṉi viṭṭu nīṅkāta koḷkiyuṭiya cuṟṟam niṉṉic cuṟṟiyirukka vēḷvi yil kaṭavuḷukku āvuti aḷittāy. puttēḷir nāṭṭut tēvarkaḷi iṉpuṟuttiṉi. vaṇaṅkiya cāyalum (pakivari) vaṇaṅkāta āṇmi yumuṭiya iḷiya valiya putalva! niṉ mutiyōrippēṇi avar kaḷukku iṟukkum pitirkkaṭaṉkaḷic ceyta vellum pōruṭiya taliva! cikaraṅkaḷ tōṟum niṟintu māṇṭōrkaḷuṭiya ulakamuṅ kēṭkumpaṭi iḻumeṉ ōciyōṭu iḻikiṉṟa aruviyuṭiya nīṇṭa ayirimalipōla niṉvāḻnāḷ nīṅkātākuka.
ippāṭaliṉ mūlattil (23vari) māṭōr eṉṟiruppati māṇṭō reṉṟum, iḷantuṇip putalvariṉ mutiyarppēṇi (21vari) eṉṟirup pati iḷantuṇip putalvaniṉ tuṇivarppēṇi eṉṟum poruḷ koṇṭēm. ‘riṉ’ eṉpatu niṉ eṉṟirukka vēṇṭum pōl terikiṟatu.
ēḻām patikam
vīrattōṭu pakivari akappaṭuttiyavarum periya nuṇṇiya kalvi yuṭiyavarumākiya antuvaṉākiya cēraṉukku oru tanti īṉṟamaka ḷākiya peruntēviyiṭam piṟantamakaṉ, aracaṉik koṉṟu pakivari ōṭṭi, accantaruntāṉiyik koṇṭu ceṉṟu pala pōr veṉṟu, pukaḻtalaminta periya vēḷvi vēṭṭakāli maṟṟumuḷḷa aṟattuṟi kaḷiyuñ ceytu muṭittu, tirumālit taṉatu neñcil uṟiya vittu nel viḷivu nīṅkāta ōkantūrittirumālukku aḷittu, purōkitaṉi lum tāṉ aṟaneṟiyaṟintu ceḻitta uḷḷattōṭu kuṟṟam nīṅkiya celva muṭiya kaṭuṅ kōvāḻiyātaṉik kapilar pattup pāṭṭup pāṭiṉār.
pāṭip peṟṟa paricil; ciṟu koṭi eṉa nūṟāyiraṅ kāṇaṅ koṭuttu naṟṟāveṉuṅ kuṉṟēṟi niṉṟu taṉ kaṇṇiṟ kaṇṭa nāṭellām kāṭṭik koṭuttāṉakkō. ivaṉ irupattiyāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ.

eṭṭām pattu
71 iṭiyarukkut talivaṉāṉa kaḻuvuḷuṭaṉ kōpittu eḻuntu, urōñci ūri erikkum neruppup parakkavum, (neṟ) pōri erittu maṇakkum pukiticikaḷi maṟikkavum, matiliṉ vāyil tōṉṟātu ampukaḷic celuttum vīrar āḻnta akaḻuṭiya matiliṉ aṭiyil kuṟukiya tāḷuḷḷa ñāyil kiṭakkum, kāvaluṭiya matiliṉ katavik kippaṟṟiṉi; kip paṟṟi ēṟukaḷōṭu kaṉṟukaḷuṭiya pacuk kūṭṭattikkavarntu pulāl nāṟum villuṭiya iḷiya vīrarukkup paṅkiṭṭaḷikka, av iḷiyar annirikaḷit tamatu aḻakiyakikaḷāṟ piṟarkkaḷippar. (ivvāṟu kaḻuvuḷiṉ) talimaṭaṅkutalum mattik kayiṟṟāl iḻuttuk kaṭiyum kaḻinta poḻuti niṉintu iraṅkum iṭiyar periya payattiṉāl (kaḻuvuḷi viṭṭu) niṉakkuttiṟi koṭāta vēṟu kuṟumpar nāṭṭilē avar nāṭu pāḻākumpaṭi ceṉṟu taṅkuvar. velvataṟku ariya pōriṉ ariya nilimiyit tāṅkiya pukar neṟṟiyuṭiya kaḷiṟṟuyāṉiyōṭu ariya āparaṇaṅkaḷi muṉkoṭāta avar putitākat tēṭiya poru ḷōṭu muṉṉē tēṭiya poruḷum aṟṟatu eṉa mey naṭuṅka mikku niṉṉi aṇaṅkeṉak karutip palapaṭap paravuvar. pēy tāṉpaṟṟiṉāru yiri veḷavātu taṉakku avar paliyiṭṭuḻi appali koṇṭu peyaru māṟu pōla nīyum avaruyiri veḷavātu tiṟikoṇṭu peyarāniṉ ṟāy. (nī ceytatu itu māttira malla) nīṅkāta putu varuvāyuṭiya akaṉṟa iṭamuṭiya vayaliṉ nīrilē vaḷarum āmpali neytalōṭu arintu vayaliṭattiṟ peṇkaḷ viḷiyāṭum kaṭā viṭum periyakaḷattil periya kaṭākkaḷ utirtta, melliya cōṟṟik koṭukkum cennelli marakkālāl aḷantu piyiṭattiṟ pōṭṭuk kuvittāṟ pōṉṟu, kaṭitākak koṭṭutaluṭiya kuḷaviyiṉam tiraṇṭu taṅkum kūṭukaḷik kiṇṭiya ciṟuvarip pōlap, perumāṉē! varuntiṉārkaḷ; iṉimēl uḷḷattil aṟivuṭiyōriyum mūṭariyum avaravar aṟiviṉit terintu eṇṇi avaravariṭattiṟ ceyyum aruḷaṟintu aruḷāyāyiṉ neṭuntakāy! ivaṇ vāḻvāryār? niṉvāḻnāḷ vāḻvatāka.
72 niṉṉōṭu kōpittu etirttōr tam aṟiyāmiyiṉ mikutiyāl niṉ kuṭiyiluḷḷa muṉṉōrākiya aracar ulakatti āḷutaṟku aṟivu vali yuṟuttiyavarum kāriyaṅkaḷi naṉṟāyaṟinta uḷḷattiyuṭiyarumākiya cāṉṟōri otta niṉcūḻccip paṇpi aṟiyār. nī tāṉ cūḻcci uṭiyayi aṉṟi, ciṉam poruntiya kuricil! niṉṉi etirt tōrkkup pōr ceyyumiṭattu, ellā uyirum iṟantupaṭum ūḻikkāla muṭivil nilattiṉ pāram nīṅkap parantu, nīr maṇṭi varukiṉṟa valiya tirikaḷ uṭiyatum, palluyiriyum oruṅku tāṉ kolluṅ karuttuṭiyatumāka kōpitteḻukiṉṟatumākiya veḷḷam elliyillāta ticikaḷil iruḷ cērap parakkiyālē, ataṉi māykkavēṇṭi ātittar paṉṉiruvarun tōṟṟiya vaṭavittī aṉiyi; ataṉi aṟikiṉṟaṉarātalāl tampaki mikutiyāl añcārāy avar paṭi eṭukkat tuṇital allatu palarum tammuṭaṉ oruṅku kūṭikkākkiṉum tam nāṭṭik kākka māṭṭār.
73 aṟivuṭiyōr eṇṇiṉum mūṭar eṇṇiṉum piṟarkku nī uvami yāvatallatu niṉakkup piṟar uvamiyākāta oppaṟṟa periya vēntē! ciṟanta virinta iṭattiyuṭiya viḷinta vayalkaḷiluḷḷa nāriyi oppum makaḷir paciya āparaṇaṅkaḷik kaḷiyātu oṉṟukkup pakkattē oṉṟāy pala kūṭṭaṅkaḷāka niṉṟu kuravi āṭuvar. (ivvakic ciṟap paminta) kāviri nīrāl niṟinta vayalkaḷ paranta vaṉappuṭiya pukār nakariyuṭiya celva! pāṇṭi nāṭṭavar meymmaṟi! mūṅkil parantu vaḷarum maḻi tavaḻkiṉṟa neṭiya cikaramuṭiya kolli malipporuna! koṭiyaciyum tēruṭiya cēra! niṉ celvamum āṇmiyum koṭi yum ulakattu makkaḷiṉ aḷavik kaṭantaṉa. niṉṉōṭu māṟupaṭuvatu numakku uṟutiyaṉṟeṉa yāṉ paṉṉāṭ colliyum (niṉ pakivar) tēṟiṟṟilar; tēṟārāyiṉum ulakattu matippuṭiya cāṉṟōr collat tām tēṟuvārō veṉak karutiṉ, avar coṉṉaviṭattum avarkaḷ mati maruṇṭatu kāṇāniṉṟēṉ. ātalāl niṉperumiyi avarkaṭku eppaṭi urippēṉeṉa yāṉ varuntā niṉṟēṉ. itu eṉṉiṭattuḷḷa kuṟi. itaṉi aṟintu nī avarpāl aruḷa vēṇṭukiṉṟēṉ.
74 ivvulakattavarkaḷukkuc ceyyavēṇṭiya ariya kaṭamikaḷic ceytu muṭitta pōril veṟṟiyuṭiya mutalva! yākañ ceytaṟkuriya viti kaḷik kēṭṭu ataṟkuriya virataṅkaḷi viṭātu tēvarkaḷ uvakkumpaṭi vēḷvi vēṭṭaṉi 1eṉṟum, cāyntu karumaṇal ottut tāḻntakariya kūntal mutaliyavaṟṟāl vēṟupaṭṭa niṉ tēvi (maṉiviyar palaruḷ paṭṭattut tēvi) nalla aḻakiya maṇikaḷ aṇinta vaṭivaminta tōḷum, kariya curuṇṭa mayirum pirakācikkiṉṟa neṟṟiyumuṭiyavaḷ. (māṉiṉ ilakkaṇaṅkaḷi) aṟintōr mali aṭintum ciṟu kuṉṟukaḷi nāṭiyum ceṉṟu koṇarnta ciṟiya paciya puḷḷikaḷum marak kiḷikaḷi otta kavarpaṭṭa maruppu muṭiya puḷḷimāṉiṉ iṟicciyi nīkkik kuṟṟaṅkaḷip pōkki vaṭṭamāy veṭṭiya pirakācikkiṉṟa eñciya tōliṉ viḷimpilē, koṭumaṇam eṉṉum ūriṟ kiṭitta toḻil māṭcimippaṭṭa nalla āparaṇaṅkaḷiyum, pattar eṉṉum ūrār (tiṟiyāka aḷanta) palarālum pukaḻappaṭum muttiyum viḷimpilē vittukkaṭṭik kūriya irumpūciyiṉāl (toḻil val lōṉ) tōluṭceyyum toḻilellām ceytu (titta) tōliṉi vicumpiṟ paṟakkum paruntu kavarutaṟkup pārkkac cūṭiṉamiyāl, karuvil mātam nirampip periya aṟivip peṟṟu, amitiyum talimi yum uṭpaṭṭa piṟavumākiya āḷukik kaminta vīram poruntiya putalvaṉip peṟṟaṉi eṉṟum, yāṉ maruṇṭēṉallēṉ. niṉṉi nalvaḻiyil oḻukuvittu niṉṟa narimūtāḷaṉi (purōkitaṉi) niṉtava oḻukkattāṉē, koṭiyum perumiyum celvamum cantatiyum teyvamum ākiya palavum tavamuṭiyōrk keṉak kūṟi illaṟa oḻuk kiṉi oḻittut tuṟavoḻukkattilē cella oḻukuvittaṉi; av vāṟu ceyvitta niṉ pēroḻukkattiṉiyum pēraṟiviṉiyum terintu nāṉ maruṇṭēṉ.
75 poṟiya! (=cēra) nī periya puliyik koṉṟu kaḷiṟṟi aṭum (etirttaṟ) kariya kōpamuṭiya ciṅkam aṉiyi! ataṉāl, pala vēṟpaṭiyiyum poṉmāliyā lalaṅkaritta yāṉiyiyum aḻakiya tēriyumuṭiya vēntarum vēḷirum piṟarum niṉ aṭikkīḻppaṇivar; paṇintu, nelliṉkaṇṇē neruṅki vaḷarntamiyāl aṟukkappaṭṭa karumpu iṭiyaṟātu pāku aḷippatum, varuvārik kaṇakkiṭa muṭiyāta tumākiya vaḷam poruntiya tamatu maruta nilaṅkaḷuṭiya nāṭṭi (āḷāniṟpar). (muṉpaṇintataṟkēṟpa) piṉ niṉvaḻi oḻukārāyiṉ ariya paṟiyikkoṭṭum toḻilāḷar pulliya pōr ceytu kaḷḷuviṟkum vītiyil akkaḷḷukku viliyākak koṭukkum veḷvaraku vittutaṟku uḻuta koḷḷuṭiya karampiyākiya valiyapār nilattilē keṭṭup pōyiruntu (āṇṭu viḷinta) veḷvaraku, uṇpataṉṟittām paṇ ṭuṇṇum cennel uṇavu uṇṇak kiṭiyātapaṭi vaṟumippaṭuvar! avar tannāṭṭi āḷa eppaṭi muṭiyum?
76 mukil paṭintu karumikoṇṭu kuḷirnta tuḷikaḷic coritalāl pala vitikaḷi uḻutu vittum (toḻilāṟ periyarāyiṉum kulattālum oḻukkattālum) ciṟiya ērāḷar, uḻutu viḷivittukkoḷḷalaṉṟi, kuḷirnta tuṟiyiṭattuḷḷa pakaṉṟip pūvāṟṟoṭutta māliyi veḷutta āṭipōṟ cūṭikkoṇṭuniṉṟu uḻuta iṭaṅkaḷtōṟum oḷiyuṭiya tirumaṇikaḷi eṭukkum nāṭṭuk kuriyōṉāvaṉ (nam vēntaṉ). avaṉ, maṉṉaratu kaḷiṟuṭiya pōr citiyumpaṭi uyarttiya, pirakācikkiṉṟa vāḷum, muracumuḻaṅkum paṭiyumuṭiya maṉṉar kūṭic ceṟinta nilimiyi aḻittu, tuṟiyiṭattiṟ pōyp periya kaṭali nīntiya marakkalatti aḻivuṟātu pātukākkum paṇṭavāṇikarip pōlap periya paṭivakuppiluḷḷavarkaḷiṉ puṇṇinīkki, avaratu valiya tuyarik kaḷintu pōriṭattu viṉiyiliruttalē vinōta mākak koṇṭu, irantavarkkīntu, piṉṉumirappavarkaḷukku mākkaḷi variyātu koṭukkum mācitaṟirukki (=mā+citaṟu+irukki)yik kaṇṭu pōka vantēṉ.
77 vaḻiyiṭattē cellum putiya makkāḷ! ciṉanta pōriyuṭiya poṟi yaṉ, eṉṉa? perumpaṭiyaṉō eṉṟaṉirāyiṉ, avaṉ, tāṉiyiṭat tuḷḷa pakikeṭṭōṭa aracarik kaḷattilē uṭal oḻintukiṭakkak koṉṟu veṉṟu tōḷōccit tuṇaṅkiyāṭiya vīrar ippoḻutu ivaṉ kaḷattiṟ paṭṭukkiṭakka appiṇaṅkaḷ mēluruṇṭa cilluṭiya paṇ aminta tērum, māvum, mākkaḷum eṇṇaṟku arumiyiṉ eṇṇiṟṟilaṉ; kaṭṭuttaṟiyil nillātu kuttukkōl palavaṟṟi muṟittu, uyarappaṟakkum paruntiṉ nilattē tiriyum niḻaliccāṭit tūrattē paralniṟinta mēṭṭil nēriya paṭiyuṭiya koṅkaratu ā parantāṟ pōṟcelavuṭiya yāṉikaḷi avaṉṟāṉiyiṟ kāṇpāy.
78 cila vaḷikaḷiyuṭiya viṟali! nī celkuviyāyiṉ, kiḷi kaṭikiṉṟa makaḷir (camīpattiluḷḷa maruta nilattē ceṉṟu) neytalōṭu virinta itaḻuṭiya tāmariyi arintu, piṉ kiḷi kaṭikiṉṟavarkaḷāy pala vaḷa muṭiya mulli nilap puṉaṅkaṭōṟum (kiḷi kaṭi pāṭali) nuvalvatum, pala payaṉkaḷuṭiya kāṭuṭiya ūril velkiṉṟa pōriyuṭiya vīrar vīram kākkum viṟpaṭi taṅkum kāṭuṭiya takaṭūri aḻittu, (ciṉamikuti yāl māṟṟār paṭiyiṉi nērē pārātu) eṭuttum paṭuttum vaḷittum) pēypōlap piṟaḻanōkkiya pala vāttiyaṅkaḷuṭiya avarōṭu uṟṟa kaṭiyapaki oḻiyac ceṉṟu koṭiya muṉiyi aḻittapōtu, kutiri yōṭu āparantaṉṉa yāṉiyiyuṭiyōṉ kuṉṟu, uvvelliyil aruvi, veṟṟimuracippōl olittup pirakācattuṭaṉ viḻum atuvākum. cilvaḷi viṟali!
nī celkuviyāyiṉ, yāṉi uṭiyōṉatu kuṉṟu uvvelliyil uḷḷa aruvipāykiṉṟa atuvākum eṉa viṟaliyi āṟṟup paṭuttiyatu icceyyuḷ eṉka.
79 māli aṇinta mārpa! pōriṭattu uyirkaḷik kāttaluṭiyi, iravalariṉ naṭuvē koṭiyip pōṟṟuvi. periyōrip pōṟṟic ciṟiyōri aḷippi; niṉ ellāk kuṇaṅkaḷum aḷattaṟkariya; nī avvāṟoḻukutaliṉ palaticikaḷilumparantanalliciyi iṉikkaṉa vilum piṟar naccutaṟkaṟiyār; ataṉi aṟiyāmiyiṉ, pirakācikkiṉṟa civanta nāvāl niṉṉi vaṇaṅkātavarkaḷi aḻitta āṇmiyiyum, vaḷiyaṇinta peṇkaḷiṉ tōḷiṭattuk kuḻinta māliyiyu maṇintamārpa! niṉṉi vaḻipaṭṭiruttalaṉṟit tam nilattiliruntu niṉṉōṭu pakittu, (piṉpu pōrkkaḷattu niṉ valiyik kaṇṭu iṉi niṉvaḻi oḻukuvēm eṉaccollit tām) ēṟiya yāṉiyiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅki vil nāṇ aṟuppuṇṭu niṉ ceṅkōlvaḻi oḻukāta vel kiṉṟa pōriyuṭiya vēntar muraciṉi uṭittu, avar aracayāṉi kataṟumpaṭi kompiyaṟuttu nī iṇakkiya aḻakiya kaṭṭilmēl iruntu, tumpi cūṭiya vīrariṉ uṭampu acintu varukiṉṟa niṟamuṭiya irat tatti aṉṟip pali koḷḷāta añcun taṉmiyuṭiya koṟṟavi uṟikiṉṟa ayirimalip peruma! niṉpukaḻkēṭilavāka.
80 ulakamuḻumiyum nilipeṟṟa nallapukaḻuṭiya toliyāta kalvi yuṭiyōy, ampuṭiya valiya vīrarākiya uyarntōr, niṉpakiva ruṭiya periya maruppuṭiyakaḷiṟṟiyāṉi malipōṟ kūṭṭamāy niṟkavum, eṭuttumuḻakkiya veṟṟi muracam kārkāla maḻiyilum kaṭitumuḻaṅkavum, avaṟṟi oṉṟum matiyātu niṉṉōṭu, cāntu pularnta periya mārpum (vīra) vaḷi cuṭar viṭukiṉṟa valiyamuṉ kiyum, kālil vīrakkaḻalum, piṟaṅkiṭāta valiya aṭiyum, pirakācikkiṉṟa vāḷumuṭiya, kēṭillāta pakivariṉ etir niṉṟu ulāvi, ‘ivetirtta vēntaṉukkut tiṟiyiṭuka’ eṉaccolli niṉṉi (avuyarntōrākiya vīrar) vāḻttu mpaṭi nī ataṟkēṟṟa taṉmiyi uṭiyi ātalāl, niṉ pakippulat telliyil niṉpakivarik kaṭiya kutiripūṭṭiya tērmicik koṭi, ciṉantapōrik kuṟittu eḻuvateppaṭi.
niṉ vīrar niṉṉōṭu valiya pakivariṉ muṉṉiṉṟu veruvi vēntarkkut tiṟiiṭuka eṉakkūṟittiṟi iṟuppittut tutikkum iyal piṉiyātaliṉ pōrik kuṟittu niṉtērmicikoṭi eḻuvateppaṭi eṉṟavāṟu.
eṭṭām patikam
poyyillāta celvakkaṭuṅkōvukku vēḷāvikkōmāṉ makaḷ matumaṉtēvi yīṉṟa makaṉ kollimalik kūṟṟattiluḷḷa nīr mikka maliyiṉucciyil pala vēṟpaṭiyuṭiya atikamāṉōṭu iraṇṭu periya vēntariyum orumittu veṉṟu, avaratu muracum koṭiyum kalaṉum koṇṭu pukaḻaminta, pōrkkaḷattē kaḷavēḷvi ceytu kuṟṟantīrnta makaḷir iraṅkumpaṭi mayiri aṟuttut takaṭūriveṉṟu ataṉ matilik kikkoṇṭu peṟṟa ariya valiyiyum pirakācik kiṉṟa pukaḻiyumuṭiya peruñ cēralirum poṟiyi kuṟṟantīrnta vārttiyuṭiya aricil kiḻār pattuppāṭṭup pāṭiṉār.
pāṭippeṟṟa paricil: tāṉum tēviyum puṟampōntu niṉṟu kōyi liluḷḷa vellām koṇmiṉeṉṟu oṉpatu nūṟāyiram kāṇattōṭu aracukaṭṭiluṅkoṭuppa, avar, yāṉ irappa itaṉi āḷka veṉṟu amiccup pūṇṭār. takaṭūreṟinta peruñ cēralirum poṟi patiṉēḻi yāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ.


oṉpatām pattu
81 ulakikkākkum aḻakiya ciṟappuṭiyatum (kariya) koḻumiyuṭiyatu mākiya (maliyiṟ paṭinta) mēkaṅkaḷ, akaṉṟa periyavāṉiṭattu atir kiṉṟa kōpattōṭu accantarum iṭi iṭittu, eḻuntu, vicumpi aṭintu kār kālatti aṟivikkum. apparuvattāl (kārkālam pirinta talivar cērntuṟiyum mullikkuriya perumpoḻutāka liṉ) varuttaṅ koḷḷā niṟkak, kaḷiṟupāyntu cellavum, katiyuṭiya kutiri vīrarit tāṅkiccellavum, pirakācikkiṉṟa koṭi aciyattēr cuḻaṉṟu tiriyavum aracar puṟattē cūḻntu taṅkiṉum añcātōrum, tamatukāva liṭaṅkaḷip (pakivar) kippaṟṟamuṭiyāta valiyuṭiyōrumākiya valiya vīrar, periya iruṇṭa irākkālattum ciṟanta vīravaḷi pirakācikka (kuḷirmikutiyāl) tōḷippiṇitta kikaḷi mēlē uṭiyarāy, nāḷum (tām mēṟkoṇṭa pōri) veṟṟic ciṟappuṭaṉ muṭittuk koḷḷum vēṭkiyarāyum, uyarttukkūṟap paṭum keṭātanalla pukaḻit taṅkuṭikku nāṭṭuvōrāyum, muḷvēliyiṭāta pācaṟiyil ulāvittirivar. (ivvāṟāṉapācaṟiyiliruntu) nāṭṭi akappaṭut tiṉatiṉāl akkālattu nērnta koḷḷiyi māṟṟi aḻaṉṟu ceyyum pōrttoḻili āṟumpaṭiceytu, niṉtāṉikkuttaraṅkaḷi (=patavikaḷi) utavi, vēṟṟu nāṭṭiṟtaṅkiya aṇṇal! mattaḷam pōṉṟa periya (palāp) paḻattiṉ (cāṟu) pōṉṟatum, kariya aḻakiya mūṅkiṟkuḻāyil viḷintatum, viḻāk koṇṭāṭiṉālottatumākiya iṉiya matuvipparukik, kāntaḷ māliyaṇinta ceḻuṅkuṭiyiluḷḷa celvarviṉōtamakiḻcci eyti iravalarkku īkiṉṟa vaṇṭukaḷ mūcum cōliyum. periya maḻiyumuṭiya kollimaliyiṟ pūtta iruvāṭci malarōṭu paccilikaḷiccūṭi, miṉ umiḻntāṟpōṉṟu cuṭarviṭum āparaṇam pūṇṭa cēṭiyarkaḷiyuṭiya, vaṇṭu moyttaliṉ niṟam kānta curuṇṭa kariya kūntaluṭiyavaḷum, pirakācikkiṉṟa neṟṟiyuṭiya vaḷum, vaḷinta kuḻikku aḻakupeṟap poruntiya nōkkuuṭiya vaḷum, māṭcimippaṭṭa āparaṇaṅkaḷaṇintavaḷumākiya (niṉ) tēvi kāṇutaṟ poruṭṭu niṉ tēr oru nāḷ puravi pūṇavēṇṭum. atutāṉ arivikku ākavēṇṭuvatalla. ataṉālē niṉṉōṭupōr ceykiyik kiviṭṭu niṉ muṉṉē vantu vaḻipaṭṭu nillāmiyāl, muṉpu niṉ valiyōṭetirttuppiṉ etirkkappeṟāta aracarkaḷ tuñcavēṇṭum; niṉ tōṭku viruntumākavēṇṭum; (ivvāṟu iraṇṭoru kāriyamāka itaṉic ceyka)
82 pāṭuvōr koḷḷak koḷḷak kuṟiyāta celvamum, pakivar kollak kollak kuṟiyātatāṉiyum, (pukaḻkāraṇamākiya) koṭi yum, perumiyum, amitiyum, aṟaṉum ātiyavaṟṟi aṟiñar collip pukaḻkiyāl keṭāta nalla pukaḻuṭiyavaṉum, pakivaratu nilattik kavarum tiruvuṭiyavaṉumākiya neṭiyōy! peruma! periya paki ātaliṉ (niṉpakivar niṉṉi añcittāntām vaḻipaṭum) teyvattit tamakkuk kāvaleṉṟu vaḻuttumpaṭi, vīrar añcātu aritākat taṅkum (pakivarukku) accantarum pācaṟiyiṭattu, palakoṭi acikiṉṟa muṉṉaṇiyuṭiya pakivaratu valiyapōrit tolitta tum muṉṉē pōrceytu paḻakiyatumākiya yāṉi, matam poḻintu kaṭuñciṉam mūṭi vaṇṭu moykkum taliyuṭiyatāy (pōr peṟāmi yāl) tiriya, pākar ataṉ ciṉatti aḷavu paṭuttavēṇṭip piṭiyip puṇarkkap, piṭiyip puṇarntum pōrvēṭṭut tiriyavum vīrar, pōrukku āyattamākavum, (iṉṉapoḻutu pōr nikaḻumeṉṟu aṟiyāmiyiṉ) kutirippaṭi cēṇam iṭṭu niṟkavum, tērmītu (pōr kuṟittuk kaṭṭiya) koṭikaḷ aciyavum, paricikaḷ muṉcoṉṉa (kālāḷ, kutiri, tēr) mutaliya vaṟṟiṉ pakkaṅkaḷiṟ kiṭantu (pōr kuṟittanāḷil) ārppavum, kāṭṭiluḷḷa viṟaki muṟittu tīkāyun taṉmiyuṭiyatākiya pācaṟiyil nī taṅki uṟiyum nāṭkaḷ palavāyiṉa vātaliṉ, niṉṉippāṭik kāṇavantēṉ.
83 niṉṉikkāṇpārkku, kariya mukiṟkūṭṭattiṉ muṉ (ororukāl) oḻuṅkutavaṟi eḻuntucellum vāṉiṟ paṟakkum kokkiṉ nīṇṭa oḻuṅkupōlak, kollum yāṉikaḷ neruṅkiya palaparicik kūṭṭat tōṭu nīṇṭa tērkaḷil acikiṉṟa koṭi viḷaṅkāniṟpa niṉpaṭi celluñcelavu mika iṉitākuka. avvāṟu aṉpuṟuvāri oḻiya, atu yārkku iṉpañ ceyyāteṉil: niṉ pala kutirippaṭikaḷ nāṭukeṭum paṭi aḻittu, nalla āparaṇaṅkaḷik koḷḷiyākap peṟum porutaṟ kariya kaṭiya ciṉatti etirttu māṟukoḷḷum pakivaratu pācaṟiyil taṅkuvōrkkākum.
84 ōci uyarak kuṟuntaṭiyālaṭikka atirkiṉṟa pōrkkappaṭṭa muracu kaṇ atirntatu pōṉṟa kārkāla muḻakkilum, māṟṟātu pācaṟiyiṟ pukuntu neṟṟippaṭṭa maṇintu niṟkum pōrkkuriya teṉṟu ellārālum collappaṭum yāṉiyik kaṭṭuttūṇiṉiṉṟum aviḻttuc cellum palavēṟpaṭiyuṭiya pūḻiyar kōvē! poṉtēruṭiya poṟiya! ulaki aḻikkum kāṟṟi ottamutalva! (pakip) pulattiṟ koṭiyaci yum kāvaluṭiya eyilkaḷ eṇṇiṉ elli aṟiyā; pala kutirikaḷ parantaṉa; (ākiyāl pakippulam namakku vellaṟku ariya toṉ ṟeṉṟu uṇarāy) nī avvāṟu valluṉi āṉapaṭiyi muṉpu aṟiyātu, iṉṟu pōrceytu aṟintaṉarāyiṉum, avar niṉṉōṭu kōpittu eḻuntu vantu ataṉip piṉṉumaṟivatallatu (niṉṉi) vaṇaṅkal aṟikiṉṟilar. iṉi avar, mukilpōl ārttumuḻaṅkum iḷam yāṉiyiṉ kīḻakappaṭṭa mūṅkil muḷiyiṉ kiḷi aḻivatu pōla aḻivatallatu uyyakkarutuvatu yātu?
kōpittu eḻuntuceṉṟu pōrkkappaṭṭa muracam, āravāram eḻuntu caṇṭiyi aṟivikkap pōrttoḻil pūṇṭa māṟṟāratu vēṟpaṭi niṟinta parappil, kālattāṟpeyyum māripeytu taṉatu toḻilāṟṟi ākāyattiṟ ceṉṟapuyal neṭuṅkālam maliyiṭattē ceṉṟu maḻi tōṉṟiyaviṭattu, palakuraluṭiya puḷḷiṉ oli eḻuntāṟpōlap pōraṟi kūvum cerukkuṭiya vil nīṅkāta vīrarōṭu iṭi iṭikkum malipōṟ kaḷiṟu nilattē uruḷa niliyāmi aminta pala pōric ceytu niṉ paṭikkūṭṭam āravārittirukkiṉṟa iruppiṉiyām iṉitu kaṇṭēm.
85 (iḷañcēralirumpoṟi) poṉṉiṉāl oḷiviṭumpaṭi vēlippāṭuka ḷamittuc ceytatum piṟar pūṇutaṟkariyatumākiya viḷaṅkum periya āparaṇaṅkaḷi aṇinta ceṉṉiyar perumāṉuṭiya (=cōḻaṉatu) (payaṉtarum) nalla maraṅkaḷ ceṟinta pala nāṭukaḷik kavarntu emakkut tantāṉ; acceṉṉiyar perumāṉi em muṉṉē piṭittuk koṇṭuvantu tammiṉeṉat tam paṭittalivari ēvac, ceṉṉiyar perumāṉiṉ paṭiyāḷar porutu tōṟṟup pōṭṭu ōṭiya veḷḷiya vēluṭiya celvak kaṭuṅkōvāḻiyātaṉeṉpavaṉ, tīñcuṉi kaḷuṭiya aḻakiya pakkaṅkaḷil pala kōṭukaḷ parantatum (taṉ mēl) ēṟi nāṭu muḻuvatiyum pārttaṟku ētuvākiyatumākiya maliyiṭattu kaḷḷuvārkkappaṭummakiḻccikkuriya kāli olakka irukkiyiṉkaṇ taṉ muṉṉōrkaḷākiya aracarkaḷip pōl aracavi paṇiyumpaṭi aṟatti nāṭṭuvataṟku maṟampuriyum taṉmiyi otta koḷki yuṭiyi! avaṉ tantanāṭukaḷ, pirakācikkiṉṟa civanta nāviṉāl meymiyākap pāṭiya pukaḻuṭiya kapilaṉ peṟṟa nāṭukaḷilum palavākum.
86 nilattilē irattam perukip, pōrkkaḷam pulāl nāṟṟam vīcumpaṭi koṉṟu pōriveṉṟa periya valiya vicālitta kiyil vellum vēluṭiya poṟiyaṉ eṉṟu (ellōrum) kūṟukiyāl yāṉ avaṉi accantarum kōpamuṭiyāṉ oru makaṉeṉṟu (muṉpu karuti ṉēṉ); avveṇṇam ippoḻutu kaḻintatu. appoṟiyaṉākiya, nalla pukaḻnili peṟṟa periya ulakil illātavarkaḷiṉ varuttam tīra nalkum ārāycci mikunta aruḷ uṭiya neñciṉaṉākiya pāṭuvōr vēntaṉum, acintu naṭakkiṉṟa talivaṉum ākiya avaṉ, puṉalilē makaḷir pāyntu āṭa mitantu poṉṉāṟ ceyta aḻakiya kuḻimītu tōṉṟiya cāntu mitantuvarum vāṉi āṟṟu nīriṉum niccayamāka iṉiya kuḷirnta cāyaluṭiyaṉ.
87 pala vēṟpaṭiyuṭiya cēraṉ, cantaṉamum akilum, poṅkum nuriyiṭattuc cumantu, teḷinta kaṭaliṭattē cellum veḷḷiya tali yuṭiya civanta nīriyuṭiya āṟṟiṉ nīr vaḻic celuttum, karumpāṟ ceyta teppattiṉum aruḷceyya vallaṉ; (ātalāl avaṉ pāl) pāṭiṉi! celvāyāka; (celliṉ) nalla āparaṇaṅkaḷip peṟukuvi.
88 ulakiṟ paranta aracaratu toḻiliṉ muṟi tavaṟātu, koṟṟaviyiṉ peya ruṭiya kāṭṭōṭu 1(vintāṭavi) malikaḷ uyarntu kaṭalāṟcūḻappaṭṭa akaṉṟa iṭattiyuṭiya ulakattut tamatu pukaḻparanta pakivar tēya, aliyuṭiya periya kaṭal piṟaṅkiṭumpaṭi vēl eṟintum, aḻakiya kaṭampi veṭṭiyum, porumporuṭṭu māṟupaṭṭa kaḻuvuḷip puṟaṅkaṇṭum, pukaḻpeṟṟa maṉṉari veṭṭikkoṉṟum, katiyuṭiya kutiriyip pakivariṭam kavarntum, cuṭarviṭukiṉṟa vākiyuṭiya naṉṉaṉi aḻittum, irattam cintiya kuvinta cōṟṟumaliyōṭu aḻakiya ayirimalit teyvamākiya koṟṟaviyip paraviyum, aracarum vēḷirum piṉceṉṟu paṇiya veṟṟi koṇṭōratu marapil vantōṉē! nīṇṭa piṭarimayiruṭiya ciṅkampōṉṟu vīram poruntiya kuricil! muracu parantolikkiṉṟatum, paricikaḷ variciyāka (niṟuttap peṟṟatu) mākiya iṭattil valiya kaḷiṟṟiṉmītu veṟṟikkoṭi acikiṉṟa pācaṟiyum, kāvaluṭiya matili aḻikkum kaṟkaḷi iṭi viṭātu eṟiyum kavaṇiyum, paṉṉāṭiyāl vaṭikkum tēṉiyu muṭiya koṅkar taliva! kōpittōrik koṉṟa poṉtēruṭiya kuricil! vaḷinta kaṭaluṭiya toṇṭiyōr poruna! peruma! collappaṭṭa māṭcimiyuṭiya uttama ilakkaṇaṅkaḷōṭum, polinta cāntōṭum kuḷirnta maṇam vīcukiṉṟa māli cūṭippūṇ kaḷic cumappavaḷum, kariya mukiṟkūṭṭaṅkaḷ periyamaḻi peytu vēṅki pūttuḷḷa ākāyatti muṭṭum uyarnta koṭumuṭikaḷi liruntu aruviōṭum maliyi ottamārpuṭiyavaḷum, tūrattē naṟumaṇam vīcappeṟukiṉṟa, nalla pukaḻaminta aḻakiya āparaṇaṅkaḷi uṭiyavaḷumākiyavaḷatu kaṇava! kāṟṟaṭittalāl uyarntu varum tiri aṭikkappaṭṭa kariyi uṭikkum taṇṇiya kaṭaṟkaric cōli yuṭiya nāṭukiḻavōy! yāṉ īṇṭu niṉṉik kāṇavantēṉ. nī nīṭuvāḻvāyāka; ellārālum pukaḻntu collappaṭṭa tumpippōri niṉakku veṟṟi taraṟku meymiyāka viṉavum valiyittarum koṟṟavi kūṭi uṟikiṉṟa (ayiri) maliyil ūṟṟeṭuttup pāy kiṉṟa pērāṟupōla, varivillātu varuvōr ceḻitta pala arum paṇṭaṅkaḷik koḷḷak koḷḷak kuṟiyātu, mēlum mēlum niṉnakar ciṟappaṭiyum. (ivvāṟu celvamaminta) cittirampōṉṟa aḻakiya nīṇṭa nakaril pāvi otta makaḷiriṉ naṭuvē tēvarulakam oḷipeṟa periya vicumpiṉ kaṇ uyarntu cellum ñāyiṟṟippōlap paṉṉāḷ viḷaṅkuka.
89 pala vēṟpaṭiyuṭiya perumpoṟiya! cemmiyāṉa vāṉam poḻu toṭu viḷaṅka, kāṭṭiṟ kūṭṭamākiya peṇmāṉ āṇ māṉōṭu cērntu tiriya, periya marakkiḷikaḷil paṭcikaḷum vaṇṭum iruntu ārppa paḻamuṅ kiḻaṅkum uṇṇak kuṟivu paṭātiruppa, pala pacu niri pul aruntittuḷḷa, celvamkuṟi talaṟiyāta vaḷam poruntiya ciṟappuṭiya periya pala putuvaruvāyākiya kūlam niṟiya, nalla pala ūḻikālam naṭuvu nilimi niṉṟu oḻuka, tiṉantiṉam nāṭṭavar toḻutu ētta, viṇ ulakattut tēvarkaḷ ētta, araciyal piḻiyātoḻiyap pōril mēmpaṭṭut tōṉṟi nī, kaṉavilum nīṅkāta pōrviyōṭu niṉṉiṭattu aṭaṅkiya neñcu mācupaṭutal aṟiyātu, kuḷirnta mayirc cāntu pūciya īrampularāta kūntaluṭiya kaliyāṇamakaḷir ceṉṟu nōkki tam vāḻnāḷi aṟiyum pirakācikkiṉṟa oḷiyuṭiya aruntatiyōṭu otta kaṟpuṭiya, oḷḷiya neṟṟiyuṭiya niṉ ariviyōṭu kāṇumpaṭi polintu nōyilliyākuka.
90 veḷḷi niṟkum muṟimiyil niṟka vāṉam maḻi peytaliṉ, accam tīrntu pātukāppākit, tuṉpan tīrntu, iṉpam mikavuntōṉṟip (peruka), (pārppār mutalāyiṉār tattamakku aḷavāṉa) tuṟi nūlkaḷi añcātu niṟiyakkaṟka, valiyiṉ mikkōr kōpikkum kaṭiya vali yiṉi añcāta oḷḷiya veṟṟivēntar yāṉikaḷōṭu āparaṇaṅ kaḷit tantu, niṉ paḻamiyip pukaḻntu ēviyatic ceyya, akaṉṟa ulakattavarkaḷukku naṭuvu nilimiyic ceytu, māyāta palapukaḻ vāṉil parakka vāḷvali purintu ciṟappup pūṇṭu, aṟakkaṭavuḷ vāḻtta naṉku āṭci purinta veṟṟiyuṭiya māntaraṉuṭiya marapiluḷḷōy! aḻakiya muṟimiyil ayirimalitteyvatti vaḻipaṭṭu, kaṭal aṭaṅka vēleṟintu, mali iṭattavum, nilattiṭattavumākiya araṇik kippaṟṟic ciṉanta pakivariṉ valiyic cāyttup peṟṟa perum pukaḻip palariṭattiṉiṉṟum pōkkiya veṟṟit tiṟamiyip peṟṟa uravōr umpal! kaṭṭiyōṭukūṭiya avarip paruppup puḻukku mutaliya puḻukkiṉiyuṭiya koṅkar kōvē! kaḷḷuṭiya kuṭṭuvar ēṟē! pōril mutukiṭṭār mēṟcellāta valiyatōḷuṭiya pūḻiyar meymmaṟi! ilaṅkukiṉṟa nīrpparappuṭiya māntiyōr poruna! vēḷiyiṉ veḷḷiya pūvoṭu curip pū māṟit toṭuttāṟ pōlap pācaṟiyil, kalanta (piṟapala) moḻikaḷippēcum vīrariṉ vēntē! paranta kaṭalpōṉṟa tāṅkutaṟkariya tāṉiyōṭu periya pōr ceyyum villi mārpil muṭṭumpaṭi vaḷittaliṉ nāṇ urōñcutalāl viḷaṅkiya, vēntar tiraṇṭālotta valiyuṭiya, vārntu alaṅkarikkap paṭṭatu pōṉṟa vicālittakiyiyum, naṭcattiraṅkaḷ pūttāṟpōṉṟu viḷakkuṭiya cēṇamum āyntamayirāṟ ceyyappaṭṭa kavariyumuṭiya kutirimītu ēṟi, piṭiyuṭiya vēlippiṭittu eṟintu, pakivari iṭumpi ceytali virumpum vīrariyumuṭiya peruma! uyarnta peruñciṟappiṉāl ōṅkiya pukaḻōy! kuḷirnta puṉaliṭattu āṭuvōriṉ āravārattil kalakka vevviya pōrceyyum maḷḷar teḷḷiya ōciyuṭiya kiṇippaṟiyikkoṭṭa, kūlaṅkaḷuṭiya nallavīṭukaḷil ēṟukaḷ oṉṟukkoṉṟu māṟāka ārppa, ceḻiya palākkaḷ uṭiya koḻuviya kuḷirnta vayaliliṭattuḷḷa kāviri cūḻnta cōliyuṭiya vayaliluḷḷa uḻavar, mattaḷaṅkoṭṭa vayaliluḷḷa mayilkaḷ maḻiyikkaṇṭāṟ pōl āṭum nalla nāṭṭippōla vaḷamporuntiya, cilampum aṭaṅkiya kuṇamum, āṟiyakaṟpum, tēṟiya nalla kuralum, vaṇṭukaḷ olikkiṉṟa kūntalumuṭiya oṇṭoṭiyiṉ kaṇava! iṭipōl muḻaṅkum muracum periya nalla yāṉiyumuṭiya iṟikiḻavōy; īram uṭimiyiṉ nīril vāḻvōri oppi; aḷattaṟkarumiyiṉ periyavāṉam aṉiyi; koḷḷakkuṟivu paṭāmiyiṉ kaṭal aliyi; paṉmīṉkaḷiṉ naṭuvē tiṅkaḷippōla malarnta cuṟṟattōṭu polintu tōṉṟutaliyuṭiyi; (ātalāl) niṉakku aṭitta nāṭkaḷ ulakattiṟ tiṅkaḷ aṉiyavākaveṉṟum, niṉṉuṭiya tiṅkaḷ yāṇṭu aṉiyākaveṉṟum, niṉṉuṭiya āṇṭu ūḻiyaṉiyavāka veṉṟum, niṉyāṇṭiṟku oppākiya appallūḻitam aḷaviṟpaṭṭa palavāy nillātu veḷḷavarampiṉavāka veṉṟum niṉintu niṉṉikkāṇpēṉ vantēṉ.
oṉpatām patikam
kuṭṭuvaṉ irumpoṟikku miyūr kiḻāṉ makaḷ vēḷmāḷ attuvañ ceḷḷi īṉṟa makaṉ, accantarum tāṉiyōṭu ciṉantu ceṉṟu iru peruvēntarum vicciyum vīḻa ariya kāvaṟkāṭuṭiya kaṟkōṭṭiyi ṉakattu aintu matilkaḷi aḻittu, pottiyi āṇṭa periya cōḻa ṉiyum vitti āṇṭa iḷam paḻiyaṉ māṟaṉiyum (tāṉ) kūṟiya vañciṉam palikkumpaṭi veṉṟu, vañci mūtūriṟkippaṟṟiya poruḷ kaḷip piṟarkku aḷittu, mantira muṟimiyil (ayirit) teyvatti vaḻipaṭṭu, taṉ mantiriyākiya miyūr kiḻāṉip purōkitaṉiṉum aṟaneṟi aṟivāṉākap paṇṇi, ariya valiya muṟimiyil nāṟcantiyi lamarnta koṭiya valiyuṭiya pūtarik koṇarntu ivviṭattu niṟutti, terinta muṟimiyiṟ pūciceytu, ulakattu uyirkaḷik kātta kuṟṟan tīrnta ceṅkōlum, iṉṉici muracu muṭiya iḷañcēralirum poṟiyip peruṅ kuṉṟūrkiḻār pattup pāṭṭup pāṭiṉār.
pāṭip peṟṟa paricil: mayakkam illārkku mayaṅkak koṭukka veṉṟu uvakiyiṉ muppattīrāyiraṅ kāṇaṅkoṭuttu, avar aṟiyāmi ūrum maṉiyum vaḷamikap paṭittu, ērum iṉpamum iyalvarapparappi, eṇṇaṟku ākā ariya paṇṭaṅkaḷākiya celvattōṭu paṉṉūṟāyiram pala paṭak koṭuttu aṉuppiṉāṉ akkō. kuṭakkō iḷañ cēralirum poṟi patiṉāṟāṇṭu vīṟṟiruntāṉ.
1. puṟattiraṭṭut tolkāppiya uri mutaliyavaṟṟiṟ kāṇap paṭṭa “iruṅkaṇyāṉi” eṉṉum ceyyuḷ.
iṭi iṭittu muḻaṅkaliṉ vicumpu atirvatu pōla kaṇ atirntu muḻaṅkum kaṭuṅ kuraluṭiya muracōṭum kāṟṟuppōṉṟu vēkamākiya ūrtiyōṭum, kāṭṭuttīyippōṉṟa niṟinta (taṭuttaṟkariya) kōpat tōṭu, periya iṭamuṭiya periya kaṭali nāṭi ōṭi nilantirikkum āṟṟip pōṉṟa vēkamākiya celaviṉiyuṭiya tāṉiyōy! niṉakku.
tolkāppiya uriyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa
“ilaṅku toṭi” eṉṉum ceyyuḷ
ilaṅkukiṉṟa pūṇ iṟukkiya maruppuṭiyamatam poḻintunilam atirum paṭi tiriyum veṟṟiyuṭiyakaḷiṟum, neruppu malarntāṟ pōṉṟu virinta taliyāṭṭattic cūṭṭiyakatiyuṭiya kutiriyum, pōrkkaḷattu koṭikaḷ oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu kalakkac cūtāṭumiṭaṅkaḷōṭu ūṉviṟpā riṭaṅkaḷiyum ottu (=koṭikaḷ kalantu acitaliṉ) uyarntu aruviyip pōṉṟu olikkum variyuṭiya alaṅkarikkappaṭṭa tērkaḷum varutalikkaṇṭu, (pakivar) muracaṅkoṭṭiṉar; periya ticikaḷ kalleṉṟu olikkumpaṭi kāṟṟāṭiyippōṟṟa ōciyuṭiya kompōṭu valampuric caṅku ārppa neṭiya matilum niritta ñāyilum kāvaṟkāṭum āḻnta akaḻum uṭiya, mēlē akaṉṟa āravārikkum araṇ kāvaliyuṭiyatāyiruntatu. ceñcu vīram aḻintu nili taḷarntu nīṅkiya paki aracar naṭuṅka ivviṭam vanta periya celavu naṉmiyi aḷippatākuka.
tolkāppiya uriyiṟ kāṇappaṭṭa “vantaṉeṉ” eṉṉuñ ceyyuḷ
māriyeṉṟu pārātum, paṉi eṉac cōrātum, pakivemmiyiṉāl aciyāta ūkkatti uṭiyōy! pakip pulattut taṅkiya veṟṟi yuṭiya koṭiya tāṉiyōṭu paki māṟāta māṟṟār pakitēya valimi mikunta kaṭṭuttaṟiyip piḷantu kaṭāyāṉi muḻaṅkum muḷvēli iṭāta pācaṟi yiṭattu, yāṉiyiyum, katiyuṭiya kutiriyiyum tērōṭu iravalarkku aḷittu irukkum irukkiyi! peruma! vantu kaṇṭupōka vantēṉ.



pattuppāṭṭu urinaṭi
muṉṉuri
caṅka nūlkaḷiṉ poruḷ pulavarkaḷukkum eḷitil viḷaṅkuvataṉṟu. ataṟkuk kāraṇam annūlkaḷ ceyyappaṭukiṉṟa kālattu vaḻaṅkiya coṟkaḷiṟ perumpālāṉa iṉṟu vaḻakku vīḻntu viṭṭamiyēyākum. poruḷ viḷaṅkutaṟ karumiyuṭiya innūlkaḷ vittuvāṉ, paṇṭitar, pulavarkaḷāl māttiram payilappaṭṭu varukiṉṟaṉa. potumakkaḷ caṅka nūlkaḷil collappaṭṭirukkum poruḷkaḷ evi eṉpati aṟiyamāṭṭārkaḷ. avarkaḷ caṅka nūṟporuḷkaḷi eḷitil aṟintu koḷḷum muṟiyil avaṟṟi urinaṭippaṭutta vēṇṭumeṉṉum uṇarcci iṟṟikkup patiṉintu āṇṭukaḷukkumuṉ namatu uḷḷat teḻuntatu. ākavē caṅka nūṟkaḷiṟ palavaṟṟi urinaṭip paṭuttalāyi ṉēm. av vurinaṭikaḷ peritum payaṉuṭiyaṉa veṉṟu tamiḻ aṟiñar tamatu karuttit terivittārkaḷ; māṇavar palarum avvāṟē aṟivitta ṉar. ippatippil kūṭiyaḷavu vacaṉaṅkaḷik kuṟukkiyum kaṭiya coṟkaḷi nīkkiyum uḷḷōm. namatu nūl kaḷit tamiḻ ulaku aṟiyumāṟu innūliṉ mutaṟpatippiyum piṟa nūlkaḷiyum veḷiyiṭṭa oṟṟumi niliyat talivar tiru em. ī. vīravāku piḷḷi avarkaḷukku eṉatu naṉṟiyum kaṭappāṭum eṉṟum uriyaṉa.
ceṉṉi
10-3-1949 na. ci. kantiyā.
1. tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi
tōṟṟuvāy
murukakkaṭavuḷ vīṟṟirukkum iṭaṅkaḷiṟ ceṉṟu avar aruḷip peṟṟu mīḷkiṉṟa murukaṉaṭiyāṉ oruvaṉ murukaṉ aruḷip peṟutaṟkup pakkuvam vāynta oruvaṉi vaḻiyiṭattē etirppaṭṭut, tāṉ ceṉṟu aruḷpeṟṟa varalāṟṟi avaṉukku eṭuttukkūṟi avaṉiyum murukaṉ pāṟ ceṉṟu avaṉ aruḷip peṟumāṟu celuttuvatāka amintuḷḷatu ippāṭal.
“akavaṟ pāvāl viṟalipāṇar
kūttar porunar nālvaru ḷoruvar
pariciṟkup pōvārip paricu
peṟṟu varuvā rāṟṟiṭik kaṇṭu
talivaṉ kīrttiyuṅ koṭiyuṅ koṟṟamum
aṟiva tāṟṟup paṭiyā kummē”
tirumurukāṟṟuppaṭi eṉṉum pāṭalic ceytavar kaṭiccaṅkat tiṟutikkālattil caṅkattukkut talimi tāṅkiyirunta āciriyar nakkīraṉārāvār. avar kālam ki. pi. 100-130. ikkālak kaṇakkum piṉṉāl varuvaṉavum āyirattu eṇṇūṟu āṇṭukaḷukku muntiya tamiḻar eṉṉum nūlāciriyar kuṟippiṭṭuḷḷapaṭi uḷḷaṉa.
tirupparaṅkuṉṟam
(murukaṉ aruḷpeṟṟumīḷum aṭiyavaṉ akkaṭavuḷiṉ aruḷip peṟa viḻivuṟṟirukkum maṟṟoru aṭiyavaṉip pārttuk kūṟukiṉṟāṉ:)
murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tōṟṟam
murukakkaṭavuḷ kuṟṟamillāta kaṟpiṉiyuṭiya teyvayāṉiyāriṉ kaṇavar. ulakiluḷḷa uyirkaḷ ellām makiḻumpaṭi ñāyiṟu mēruvi valañceyyum. murukakkaṭavuḷ aññāyiṟu kaṭaliṭattē tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōṉṟu iṭiviṭātu oḷi ceykiṉṟa tirumēṉiyiyuṭiyar; tammi aṭintāratu tīviṉiyip pōkkum valiya tāḷkaḷuṭiyavar; iṭiyi otta valiyum pakivariaḻitta āṟṟalumuṭiya kikaḷiyuṭiyar; kār kālattil kaṭal nīri vayiṟu nirampumpaṭi kuṭitta mukil ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum kiraṇaṅkaḷi vīcum ākāyattil tuḷikaḷic citaṟik kāṭṭil mutal maḻiyip peyyum. ataṉāl kāṭu kuḷircciyaṭiyum. akkuḷirnta kāṭṭil ceṅkaṭampu iruṇṭu taḻittu vaḷarum. ataṉ tēruruḷ pōṉṟa malarkaḷāl toṭutta mālikaḷ murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tirumārpiṟ kiṭantu aciyum.
teyvamakaḷir
mūṅkil tēvarulakit toṭumpaṭi maliyiṭattē vaḷarum. aṅkut teyvamakaḷir uṟivar. avarkaḷ viḷaṅkukiṉṟa tamatu ciṟiya pātaṅkaḷil ciṟiya cataṅkikaḷi aṇintiruppar. avarkaḷiṉ kālkaḷ tiraṭciyuṭiyaṉa; iṭi vaḷintu aciyum taṉmiyatu; tōḷkaḷ perumiyuṭiyaṉa. avarkaḷ uṭuttirukkum paṭṭāṭi tampalap pūcci pōṉṟu civanta iyaṟki niṟamuṭiyatu. avarkaḷ maṇikaḷ kōtta vaṭaṅkaḷi ari yiṭattē aṇintiruppar. avarkaḷiṉ aḻaku kiyiṉāl aḻaku ceyyap paṭātu iyaṟki vaṉappu amintuḷḷatu. avarkaḷ aṇintirukkum aṇi kalaṉkaḷ cāmpūnatameṉṉum uyar cempoṉṉiṉāl ceyyap paṭṭavi. avarkaḷiṉ tūya niṟam tūrattē viḷaṅkit tōṉṟum oḷiyuṭiyatu. ivviyalpiṉarākiya teyvamakaḷir cōliyiṭattē kūṭuvārkaḷ; neypūcap peṟṟa tamatu mayiril civanta kāmpi yuṭiya veṭcippūvi viṭu pūvākat tūvuvārkaḷ; avaṟṟiṉ naṭuvē kuvaḷi malariṉ itaḻkaḷik kiḷḷi viṭu pūvākat tūvuvārkaḷ; poṭṭiṭṭa tamatu neṟṟiyil cuṟāviṉ tiṟanta vāypōṟ ceyta talikkōlatti aṇivārkaḷ; 1cītēviyār eṉṉum talik kōlattuṭaṉē valampuric caṅkiṉ vaṭivākac ceyta talikkōlattiyum vittaṟkuriya iṭattē vippārkaḷ; muṭiya vēṇṭiya vakiyil muṭiyappaṭṭuḷḷa koṇṭiyil caṇpakappūvic cerukuvārkaḷ; ataṉmēl viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pūṅkottukkaḷi vip pārkaḷ; nīriṉ kīḻ niṉṟu mēlē eḻum kiḷikaḷ nīṭṭiya arumpiṉāl toṭutta māliyik koṇṭiyil vaḷiyamākac cuṟṟuvārkaḷ; iru kātukaḷilum acōkan taḷiric cerukuvārkaḷ; avi, aṇikalaṉka ḷaṇinta avarkaḷ mārpiṭattē kiṭantu aciyum. ivvāṟu tammi aḻaku ceytu koṇṭa teyvamakaḷir kōṅkiṉ moṭṭi otta tamatu taṉaṅ kaḷiṭattē cantaṉak kuḻampi appuvārkaḷ. accantaṉappūccu, maṇamuḷḷa marutam pūvi appiyatu pōlat tōṉṟum. cantaṉak kuḻampiṉ īram ular vataṉ muṉ vēṅkip pūviṉ tātiyum avarkaḷ ataṉmēl appu vārkaḷ; viḷāmarattiṉ ciṟiya taḷirik kiḷḷit tappāmal oruvarmēl oruvar eṟintu “kōḻiuyarnta veṟṟikkoṭi neṭitu vāḻvatāka” eṉṟu vāḻtti mali iṭaṅkaḷ ellām etiroli ceyyumpaṭi pāṭi āṭuvārkaḷ
murukak kaṭavuḷ tirumuṭiyil cūṭiya ceṅkāntaḷ māli
atteyvamakaḷir ivvāṟu āṭal pāṭal puriyum cōliyi yuṭiya maliyil maraṅkaḷ neruṅki vaḷarntirukkum; ākavē mantikaḷum ammaraṅkaḷil ēṟi aṟiyā. immara nerukkam uṭiya maliyiṭattuḷḷa ceṅkāntaḷ malarāl toṭutta māliyi murukak kaṭavuḷ tirumuṭiyil cūṭiyiruppār.
pēymakaḷ
murukak kaṭavuḷ kutiri mukattiyum makkaḷ yākkiyiyumuṭiya cūrapaṉmāvōṭu pōr ceytār. appoḻutu cūrapaṉmā kīḻ nōkkip pūtta kottukaḷiyuṭiya māmaravaṭivāka māyiyiṉāl vaṭivukoṇṭu niṉṟāṉ. appoḻutu murukakkaṭavuḷiṉ cevvēl virintu ceṉṟu avaṉi avaṉuṭiya 1aṟuvakip paṭikaḷum añcumpaṭi aḻittatu. appoḻutu pēymakaḷ veṟṟikkaḷattip pāṭit tōḷi acittut 2tuṇaṅkikkūttu āṭiṉāḷ. pēymakaḷ pōrkkaḷattil accantōṉṟum paṭi ulāvavum virumpiṉāḷ. avaḷatu kuṟukiya mayirkaḷ ularntavi; paṟkaḷ teṟṟiyavi; paciyakaṇkaḷ kōpattāṟ cuḻalvaṉa; pārvi accam viḷippatu; kātukaḷ mārpu aḷavum nīṇṭu acivaṉa; avi accantarum pāmpum pituṅkiya kaṇkaḷiyuṭiya āntiyumākiya kuṇṭalaṅkaḷip pūṇṭavi; uṭal coracorappuṭiyatu; naṭi kaṇṭōrāl añcattakkatu; kūriya nakaṅkaḷuṭiya vaḷinta viralkaḷ irattam aḷitalāl civappu ēṟap peṟṟavi; kikaḷ, muṭināṟṟamuṭiyavum kaṇkaḷit tōṇṭi uṇṇappaṭṭaṉavumākiya kariya talikaḷi ēntuvaṉa; periya vāykaḷ niṇantiṉpaṉa.
(ivvāṟu pēymakaḷ veṟṟikkaḷattē āṭiṉāḷ.) avuṇar kaḷuṭiya veṟṟi aṭaṅkiṟṟu, kaṭal nili kulintatu.
tirupparaṅkuṉṟam
pāṭavalla pulavaṉē! ivvāṟu perumi peṟṟa murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tiruvaruḷippeṟa nī mika virumpukiṉṟāyāyiṉ, nī ataṉi ippoḻutē tappāmal peṟukuvi. avar eṅku uṟivār eṉa nī kēṭpāyāyiṉ. ataṉi yāṉ kūṟuvēṉ kēṭpāyāka. maturimā nakariṉ kōpuravāyilil veṟṟikkoṭi aciyum; varintu kaṭṭappaṭṭa pantumpāviyum aṟuppāriṉṟit toṅkum. nakaril pala māṭaṅkaḷuṭiya terukkaḷuṇṭu. ivvaki aḻaku vāynta maturikku 3mēṟkē periya nelvayalkaḷ uṇṭu. kariya cēṟṟiṉiyuṭiya av vayalkaḷil muṟukkaviḻnta tāmari malarkaḷmītu vaṇṭukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ irāppoḻutil tuyilkoḷḷum; viṭiyaṟkālattil tēṉ maṇakkiṉṟa neytaṟpūvi ūtum; ñāyiṟu tōṉ ṟiyapiṉ cuṉi malaril iruntu āravārañ ceyyum ivvaki aḻakiya viṭattēyuḷḷa tirupparaṅkuṉṟam eṉṉum kōyilil murukakkaṭavuḷ eḻuntaruḷiyiruppār. (itu avar irukkum vīṭukaḷuḷ oṉṟākum)
2. tiruccīralivāy1
murukak kaṭavuḷ yāṉimītu irukkum tōṟṟam
murukakkaṭavuḷ piṇimukam eṉṉum yāṉimītu eḻuntaruḷuvār. piṇimukam eṉṉum yāṉiyiṉ neṟṟi aṅkucam veṭṭutalāl taḻumpu ēṟappeṟṟatu. neṟṟiyil poṉṉarimāliyum neṟṟippaṭṭamum kiṭantu aciyum; irupakkaṅkaḷilum maṇikaḷ kiṭantu olikkum. ataṉ naṭipiṟa rāl taṭuttaṟ kariya kaṭumiyuṭiya tātaliṉ kūṟṟuvaṉi okkum. murukak kaṭavuḷ aṇintirukkum 2aintu vēṟu vaṭivuṭiya muṭikaḷil oḷiviṭum irattiṉaṅkaḷ patikkappaṭṭuḷḷaṉa. avar kātilaṇintirukkum poṟ kuḻikaḷ matiyic cūḻntu nīṅkāta 3viṇmīṉkaḷippōl viḷaṅkum. avaratu tirumukam tavatti muṭippavarkaḷatu uḷḷattil viḷaṅkum oḷipōl viḷaṅkum.
āṟu mukaṅkaḷiṉ toḻilkaḷ 
murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ oru mukam iruṇṭa periya ulakam kuṟṟamiṉṟi viḷaṅkum poruṭṭup pala katirkaḷit tōṟṟuvikkum; oru mukam, aṉparkaḷ tutikka ataṟku makiḻntu avarkaḷ vēṇṭum karumaṅkaḷi muṭittukkoṭukkum; oru mukam antaṇaratu yākaṅkaḷil tīmi varāmaṟ kākkum; oru mukam mantiramuṭiya vētamuṟiyil oḻukum antaṇaratu yākaṅkaḷil tīmivarāmaṟ kākkum; orumukam vētattiṉ maṟi poruḷ kaḷi ārāynta iruṭikaḷ makiḻumpaṭi avaṟṟi uṇarttit tiṅkaḷ pōlat ticikaḷi ellām viḷakkuvikkum. oru mukam māṟupaṭṭup pōrk keḻunta pakivarik koṉṟu kōpitta uḷḷattōṭu pōhk kaḷatti virumpum; oru mukam kuṟavariṉ iḷamiyuṭiya makaḷākiya vaḷḷi yuṭaṉ makiḻcciyip poruntum.
paṉṉirukaraṅkaḷ puriyum toḻilkaḷ
murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ tirumārpiṉiṉṟu tōḷaḷavum uttama ilakkaṇa mākiya mūṉṟu varikaḷ nīṇṭu kiṭakkum. avaratu valiya tōḷkaḷ pakivar mēl paṭikkalaṅkaḷi eṟintu mārpippiḷantu vāṅkum perumi yuṭiyavi. avar tirukkaraṅkaḷiloṉṟu ākāyattē iyaṅkukiṉṟa iruṭikaḷukku apayaṅkoṭukkum; uyarttiya ak kiyiṉ iṇikki pakkattē kiṭakkum; oru ki toṭimītu āṭimēṟ kiṭakkum; oruki yāṉiyi aṅkucattāl celuttum;iru kikaḷ kariya kēṭakattiyum vēliyum valamākac cuḻaṟṟum; oruki muṉivarkaḷukkut tattuvaṅkaḷik kūṟi uriyiṟanta poruḷi uṇarttum kālattu mārpiṭattu viḷaṅkum; oruki tirumāliyit tiruttum; oru ki pāravaḷiyōṭu cuḻalum; oruki iṉiya ōciyuṭaṉ āṭukiṉṟa maṇiyi olikkum. oru ki nīla ākāyattil mikka maḻiyip poḻiyum. oruki intiraṉ makaḷākiya teyvayāṉikku maṇamāli cūṭṭum. ivvaki paṉṉirukikaḷum āṟu mukaṅkaḷukkup poruntum vaki toḻilpuriyā niṟkum.
cīralivāyil murukak kaṭavuḷ eḻuntaruḷum tōṟṟam
ākāyattil tuntumi olikkum. vayiramuṭiya kariyakompu vāttiyaṅkaḷum veṇcaṅkum muḻaṅkum. iṭiyēṟṟiṉ iṭippuppōṉṟa ōciyuṭiya muracu atirum. pala puḷḷikaḷ uḷḷa iṟakukaḷuṭiya mayil veṟṟik koṭiyiliruntu olikkum. murukak kaṭavuḷ ivvakip pala ārppuṭaṉ ākāyamē vaḻiyāka virintuceṉṟu mikka aḻakuṭiya cīrali vāy eṉṉum tiruppatiyil eḻuntaruḷiyiruppār. ituvē avaratu nili peṟṟa kuṇamākum.
3. tiru āviṉaṉkuṭi1
muṉivar tōṟṟamum iyalpum
tiru āviṉaṉkuṭiyilē muṉivōr murukak kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭuvar. avarkaḷ tittup poruntiya maravuri uṭiyiṉar; avarkaḷiṉ naritta muṭikaḷ, vaṭivu niṟam aḻaku mutaliyavaṟṟāl valampuric caṅki ottavi. avarkaḷiṉ uṭampu nīrāṭutalāl aḻukkiṉṟi viḷaṅkuvatu. paṭṭiṉiyiruntu virataṅ kāttalāl avarkaḷ mārpiṭattu eṉpuk kōvikaḷ tōṉṟum; avarkaḷiṉ pōrvi poruttittitta māṉtōlākum; avarkaḷ pala nāṭkaḷ paṭṭiṉiyiruppar; veṟuppum ciṉamum avariṭam tōṉṟuvatilli. avarkaḷiṉ aṟivu evvakip poruḷikkaṟṟa vallunarum aṟiyamuṭiyā tatu. palavaṟṟik kaṟṟārkkum avar talimiyāyirukkun taṉmiyar; kāmam, vekuḷi mutaliyavi avinta tōṟṟattiṉar; meyvaruttam uḷavēṉum maṉavaruttam ciṟitum aṟiyāta iyalpiṉar.
nāṟperuṅkaṭavuḷar
veḷḷiya naccup paṟkaḷiyum aḻalpōṉṟa mūcciyumuṭiya pāmpic cāka aṭikkum karuṭaṉi ūrtiyākavum koṭiyākavum uṭiya tirumāl kāttaṟṟoḻil purivar. muppuratti aḻitta veṟṟiyiyum, mūṉṟu kaṇkaḷiyum, iṭapakkoṭiyiyum, pākattē umātēviyāriyu muṭiya uruttiraṉ aḻittaṟṟoḻil purivar. nilattil tōykiṉṟa vaḷinta tutikkiyiṉiyum nāṉku maruppiṉiyumuṭiya airāvatamākiya vākaṉamum, āyiram kaṇkaḷumuṭiya intiraṉ nūṟu vēḷvikaḷ vēṭṭu ataṉ payaṉāl pakivari aṭakkiya veṟṟiyumuṭiyaṉ. muppattumūvar eṉṉumtēvarkuḻuviṉar nāṉkupiriviṉarāvar. kantaruvar melliya vārttiyiyum, muṟukkaviḻnta malar mālicūṭiya mārpiyum, pukiyik kiyiṉālaḷḷiṉāṟ pōṉṟa mācēṟāta uṭiyiyum yāḻvācikkum tiṟamiyiyu muṭiyar. kāntaruvamakaḷir makkaḷukkuriya nōyillāta uṭampum, poṉṉuri viḷaṅkiṉāṟ pōṉṟa aḻakiya tēmalum māntaḷir pōṉṟu viḷaṅkum niṟamum uṭiyar.
murukak kaṭavuḷ āviṉaṉ kuṭiyil vīṟṟirukkum tōṟṟam.
oru kālattil cerukkuṟṟirunta piramaṉi murukak kaṭavuḷ ciṟiyi liṭṭār. akkālattup paṭipput toḻil illi āyiṟṟu. ataṉāl tirumāl uruttiraṉ mutalāṉōrkkuriya kāttal aḻittaṟ ṟoḻilkaḷum illiyāyiṉa. tamakkuriya kāttal aḻittaṟ ṟoḻilkaḷip paḻamipōl peṟavirumpiya tirumālum uruttiraṉum murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ kōpattit taṇittup piramāvic ciṟiyiṉiṉṟum mīṭka eṇṇiṉārkaḷ; eṇṇi intiraṉ patiṉeṇkaṇaṅkaḷ muppattumūvar mutaliya tēvarkaḷuṭaṉ, viṇmīṉkaḷ pōṉṟa tōṟṟamum, kāṟṟil nerupputtōṉṟiṉālotta valiyum, iṭi otta kuralum uṭiyarāy antarattē cuḻaṉṟu tirintaṉar. kantaruvar tamatu makaḷirōṭu yāḻvācittup piṉṉē cella muṉivarkaḷ muṉṉē ceṉṟaṉar. ivvakit tōṟṟattuṭaṉ murukakkaṭavuḷ teyva yāṉiyāruṭaṉ āviṉaṉ kuṭiyil eḻuntaruḷuvar.
4. tiru ērakam1
antaṇar iyalpu
antaṇar ōtal, ōtuvittal, vēṭṭal, vēṭpittal, ītal, ēṟṟal eṉṉum āṟu toḻilkaḷum uṭiyar; tūya marapil utittōr; nāṟpat teṭṭu āṇṭu piramacāriyaṅ kākkum iyalpiṉar; tarumattiyē ennāḷum kūṟum koḷkiyar, nāṟcaturam, muccaturam, vilvaṭivamākiya ākavaṉīyam, takkaṇākkiṉi, kārukapattiyam eṉṉum muttī ōmpum celvamuṭiyar; nūlaṇitaṟkumuṉ oṉṟum aṇintapiṉ oṉṟumākiya irupiṟappuṭiyar; nīrāṭuṅkāl tōytta āṭiyip pularumpaṭi uṭukkum iyalpiṉar. ivvakip puṉitamuṭiya antaṇar naṟiya pūvik kiyilēnti, “namōkuma rāyā” eṉṉum mantiratti nāppuṭiyapeyara uccarittu murukak kaṭavuḷi vaḻipaṭuvar. ataṟku makiḻntu avar tiru ērakattil eḻuntaruḷuvar.
5. kuṉṟutōṟāṭal
murukak kaṭavuḷ kuṟamātarōṭu āṭum kāṭci
kuṉṟiṭaṅkaḷil vāḻum vēṭar villāṟ kolit toḻil puriyum iyalpiṉar; tēṉip puḷikkaviṭṭuc ceyta kaḷḷi mūṅkiṟkuḻāyiliṭṭu muṟṟumpaṭi vippar; akkaṭṭeḷivi mali ucciyiluḷḷa ciṟukuṭic cuṟṟattuṭaṉ uṇṭu kaḷippar; toṇṭakappaṟi koṭṭik kuravi āṭuvar. ataṉikkaṇṭa peṇmāṉ pōṉṟa cāyaliyuṭiya peṇkaḷ tāmum 1kuraviyāṭuvar. ataṉik kaṇṇuṟum, kacciṉiyum, taṇṭiyiyum, ciṟuvāttiyaṅkaḷiyum, kiṭāyiyum, mayiliyum, kōḻik koṭiyiyum, neṭumiyiyum, tōḷvaḷiyiyum, tukiliṉiyumuṭiya murukakkaṭavuḷ tāmum āṭali virumpuvar; virumpi mārpilē cantaṉam pūciya vēlaṉ eṉṉum veṟiyāṭṭāḷaṉ paccilik koṭiyiyum, cātikkāyiyum iṭi yiṭṭut takkōlakkāy, kāṭṭu malliki, veṇṭāḷi mutaliyavaṟṟik kalantu kaṭṭiya māliyi aṇivar; civanta āṭiyi uṭuttu, acōkan taḷirik kātiṟceruki yāḻōcipōluṅ kuraluṭiya pāṭumakaḷirōṭu kuṉṟukaḷtōṟum ceṉṟu 1kuravi āṭum kuṟattiyar kikaḷit taḻuvi, ēnti, muṉ kikoṭuttu viḷiyāṭuvar. itu murukak kaṭavuḷukku nili peṟṟa kuṇamākum.
6. paḻamutir cōli2
murukak kaṭavuḷ eḻuntaruḷum iṭaṅkaḷ
murukak kaṭavuḷ taṅkiyirukkum iṭaṅkaḷāvaṉa: ciṟutiṉi ariciyip pūvuṭaṉē kalantu vittu āṭṭu maṟiyi aṟuttuk, kōḻik koṭiyi niṟutti ūrkaḷ tōṟum koṇṭāṭukiṉṟa viḻākkaḷ, aṉparkaḷ tutittu maṉam porunti irukkiṉṟa iṭaṅkaḷ, vēlaṉ veṟiyāṭukaḷam, kāṭu, cōli, āṟṟiṭikkuṟi, pala ūrkaḷ, nāṟcanti, muccanti, putuppūviṉiyuṭiya kaṭampu, ūrukku naṭuvē irukkum marattaṭi, ātīṇṭu kuṟṟi mutaliyaṉa.
kuṟamātu muruka pūci ceyyum vaki
kuṟamātu akaṉṟa nakariṭattē kōḻik koṭiyi niṟuttuvāḷ; neyyum veṇkaṭukum appuvāḷ; vaḻipaṭum mantiraṅkaḷi uccarittu aḻakiya malarkaḷit tūvuvāḷ; māṟupaṭṭa niṟamuṭiya iraṇṭu paṭṭāṭi kaḷi uḷḷoṉṟum puṟam oṉṟamāka uṭuppāḷ; civanta nūli kiyiṟ kāppākak kaṭṭuvāḷ; veṇporiyit tūvuvāḷ. irattattōṭu picinta veḷḷariciyic ciṟupaliyākat tūvuvāḷ; pacci mañcaḷōṭu vācaṉi mikunta cantaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟit teḷippāḷ; cevvalari mālikaḷiyum piṟamālikaḷiyum orēmaṭṭamāka aṟuttu aciyumpaṭi tūkki, “malip pakkattuḷḷa ūrkaḷip paciyum piṇiyum pakiyum varuttā toḻika” eṉṟu vāḻttuvāḷ; naṟiya puki kāṭṭik kuṟiñcip paṇpāṭi mali aruviyōṭu pala vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkac civanta malarkaḷit tūvi, irattamaḷinta tiṉi ariciyip parappi murukakkaṭavuḷ makiḻumpaṭi vāttiyaṅkaḷi olikkac ceytu vaḻipaṭuvāḷ. ivvāṟu accantarum murukapūci ceyyappaṭum akaṉṟa nakariṭattum murukak kaṭavuḷ uṟivār. 
veṟiyāṭuvōṉ veṟiyāṭukaḷam olikkumpaṭi pāṭip, pala kompu kaḷi ūti maṇiyi olittup piṇimukam eṉṉum yāṉiyi vāḻtti vēṇṭiṉār vēṇṭiya varaṅkaḷip peṟavēṇṭumeṉṟu vaḻipaṭa murukak kaṭavuḷ avviṭattil eḻuntaruḷuvār.
paḻamutir cōliyiṉ iyaṟki vaḷam
maliyiṭattiṉiṉṟum koṭic cīlikaḷippōṉṟu āravārat tuṭaṉ aruvikaḷ kutittōṭum. avi akil maratti aṭittuk koṇṭu varum; cantaṉa marattit taḷḷum; kiḷikaḷ antarikkumpaṭi mūṅkiliṉ vērkaḷik kiḷaṟum; tēṉkūṭukaḷik keṭuttup palāccuḷikaḷi vāric curapuṉṉi malari utirttup peṇ kuraṅkiyum āṇ kuraṅkiyum naṭuṅkac ceyyum; peṇyāṉikaḷ kuḷirāl varuntumpaṭi nīrivīci, poṉṉum irattiṉamum oḷiviṭṭu ilaṅkumpaṭi kutittu, poṉ poṭiyik kariyil vīci, vāḻi muṟiyavum teṅkiṉ iḷanīrk kulikaḷ utiravum mōtum; miḷakuk koṭiyiṉ kariya pūṅkottukaḷic cāyttu, mayilkaḷum, pēṭṭuk kōḻikaḷum payantōṭac ceytu, āṇ paṉṟikaḷum karaṭikaḷum kalaḷikaḷil nuḻiyavum, kompuṭiya kāṭṭumāṭu muḻaṅkavuñceytu āravārikkum. ivvaki aruvikaḷ uṭiya paḻamutir cōliyil murukak kaṭavuḷ amarntiruppār.
murukakkaṭavuḷ kāṭci aḷittu aruḷ vaḻaṅkum tōṟṟam

yāṉ kūṟiya avvavviṭaṅkaḷilāyiṉum piṟaviṭaṅkaḷilāyiṉum murukakkaṭavuḷ vīṟṟiruppār. nī avarik kāṇumpōtu kikūppi vaṇaṅku. piṉpu piṉvarumāṟu tuti: imayamaliyiṉucciyil taruppi vaḷarnta cuṉi yil aṅkiyaṅkaṭavuḷ taṉ uḷḷaṅkiyil vāṅki viṭa aruntati allāta aṟuvarāṟ peṟappaṭṭa āṟu vaṭivu poruntiya celvaṉē! kallāliṉ kīḻirukkum kaṭavuḷiṉ putalvaṉē! periya mali ariyaṉ makaṉē! pakivar kūṟṟē! veṟṟi aḷippavaḷum aṇikalaṉkaḷaṇintavaḷumākiya kāṭu kiḻāḷ kuḻaviyē! vaḷinta villiyuṭiya tēvar paṭittalivaṉē! ellā nūlkaḷiyum aṟinta aṟivuṭiyōṉē! pōrt toḻilil nikarillātavaṉē! veṟṟiyuṭiya iḷamip paruvat tōṉē! arumaṟi yuṇarnta antaṇar celvaṉē! cāṉṟōr pukaḻntu collum coṟkaḷiṉ poruḷāyiruppavaṉē! teyvayāṉiyārukkum vaḷḷiyammiyārukkum kaṇavaṉē! vīrarkku iṭapamē! vēl tāṅkiya kiyiyuṭiya celvaṉē! kiruñca maliyip piḷanta veṟṟi yuṭiyavaṉē! amararulakit tīṇṭum uyarnta malikaḷi yuṭiya kuṟiñci nilattuk kurimi uṭiya vaṉē! kalviyil matayāṉi pōṉṟavarkkuc ciṅka ēṟu pōṉṟavaṉē! piṟarkkup peṟaṟkariya vīṭṭiṉiyuṭiya murukaṉē! vīṭupēṟṟiṉippeṟa virumpi vantavarkku ataṉi nukarvittalāl periya pukaḻi āḷuta luṭiyavaṉē! piṟarāl varuttamuṟṟu vantōrkku aruḷ ceyyum cēyē! periya pōrkaḷi muṭittu veṉṟu pakivarik kolkiṉṟa niṉmār piṭattup poṉṉāparaṇaṅkaḷi aṇinta cēyē! irantu vantōri vēṇṭuvaṉa koṭuttup pātukākkum neṭiya vēḷē! tēvarum muṉivarum ēttum tiruppeyaru yuṭiya talivaṉē! pōr valiyāl cūrapaṉmāviṉ kulatti aḻitta matavali eṉṉum peyaruṭiyavaṉē! ivvāṟu nī aṟinta tutikaḷik kūṟip pukaḻntu pāṭi, oppillāta meyññāṉatti yuṭiyavaṉē! umatu taṉmikaḷi ellām muṟṟa aḷaviṭutal palluyir kaḷukkellām aritu ātaliṉ niṉ pātatāmarikaḷip peṟa eṇṇi vantēṉ, eṉṟu nī karutiya vīṭupēṟṟi viṇṇappañ ceyvāyāka. uṭaṉē murukak kaṭavuḷic cēvittu niṟkiṉṟa vēṟu vēṟu vaṭiviṉiyuṭiya kuṟukiya pala pūtaṅkaḷ tōṉṟip, “perumāṉē! nī aruḷattakkavaṉākiya aṟivu mutirnta pulavaṉoruvaṉ niṉ pukaḻiṉi virumpik kēṭpōrkku uṟuti payakkum iṉiya pala vāḻtturikaḷik kūṟi vantāṉ” eṉṟu kūṟum. appōtu paḻamutir cōliyil eḻuntaruḷiyirukkum murukakkaṭavuḷ accantarum teyvat taṉmiyi uḷ aṭakkikkoṇṭu taṉatu iḷiya vaṭivik kāṭṭi, “nī vīṭupeṟa niṉintu vanta varavi nāṉ muṉṉamē aṟivēṉ; ataṉi niṉintu eytutal aritu eṉṟu añca vēṇṭām” eṉṟu uṉmēl aṉpuṭiya nalvārttikaḷip palakālum aruḷic ceyvār. piṉ iv vulakil nī oruvaṉumē piṟarkku vīṭaḷittaṟku uriyava ṉākat tōṉṟumpaṭi tiruvaruḷākiya paricilit tantaruḷuvār.

2. 1porunarāṟṟuppaṭi
pāṇaṉ oruvaṉ koṭi vaḻaṅkum vaḷḷalkaḷittēṭi alintu tirikiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ piṉṉē avaṉ maṉiviyākiya viṟaliyum kariya periya cuṟṟattiṉarākiya ciṟuvarum celkiṉṟaṉar. avarkaḷ celkiṉṟa iṭam kāṭṭuvaḻi.
pāṭiṉi ciṟanta aḻaki. avaḷ kūntal āṟṟiṉ karumaṇalpōṉṟu iruṇṭu neḷintatu; neṟṟi piṟipōṉṟatu; puruvam kolittoḻi luṭiya villi oppa vaḷintatu; aḻakiya kaṭikaḷuṭiya kaṇkaḷ kuḷirnta nīrmiyiṉa; iṉiya coṟkaḷi miḻaṟṟum civantavāyō ilavitaḻ pōṉṟatu; paṟkaḷ mutti niripaṭa vittāṟ pōṉṟavi; makarakkuḻikaḷ acintu ūcalāṭum kātukaḷ kattarikiyiṉ kuḻiccuppōl amintu aḻakuṟuvaṉa; kaḻuttō nāṇam varuttutalāl piṟari nōkkātu muṉ vaḷintirukkum iyalpiṉatu; tōḷkaḷ paruttut tiraṇṭa mūṅkilpōlat tiraṇṭavi; avar muṉkiyil aitamayiruṇṭu; avaḷ viralkaḷ uyarnta malikaḷil vaḷarum kāntaṭpūp pōṉṟavi; nakaṅkaḷ kiḷiyiṉ alaku pōl civantu viḷaṅkuvaṉa; taṉaṅkaḷ cuṇaṅkaṇintu īrkkiṭi pōkāta eḻucciyum aḻakumuṭiyaṉa; koppūḻ nīrccuḻi pōṉṟatu; iṭi uṇṭu eṉṟu piṟarāl aṟiya muṭiyātu oṭuṅki aciyum iyalpiṉatu; ari pala maṇikaḷ kōtta mēkalāparaṇam aṇiyap peṟṟatu; tuṭikaḷ peṇyāṉi yiṉ tumpikkipōṉṟu tiraṭciyuṭiyaṉa; aṭikaḷ ōṭi iḷitta nāyiṉ nākkup pōṉṟaṉa; vāy mayir oḻuṅku peṟṟa kaṇikkālukkup porutta māṉavi.
pāṭiṉi yāḻ vācittut teyvattit tutittal
ivvāṟu pēraḻaku vāynta pāṭiṉi, cātiliṅkam urukki vārttāṟ pōṉṟu civanta nilattilē naṇpakal veyyilil naṭantu celkiṉṟāḷ; kālkaḷil kūriya cukkāṉkaṟkaḷ uṟuttukiṉṟaṉa; ataṉāl paṉṉip paḻam pōṉṟu kālkaḷiṟ koppuḷaṅkaḷ uṇṭākiṉṟaṉa. avaḷ mēlum naṭattaṟku iyalātavaḷākiṉṟāḷ. yāṉikaḷ ulāvukiṉṟa kāṭṭuppāti yiṉ ōrattē ilikaḷ utirtta marāmaram niṟkiṉṟatu. ataṉ niḻal vali virittatu pōṉṟa aitākak kāṇappaṭukiṉṟatu. an niḻalil pāṭiṉi ciṟitu taṅki iruntu iḷippāṟukiṉṟāḷ. avaḷ taṉatu pāliyāḻi eṭukkiṉ ṟāḷ. aṅku uṟiyum teyvaṅkaḷ makiḻumpaṭi narampukaḷivāriyum, vaṭittum, untiyum, uṟaḻntum teyvaṅkaḷit tutikkum pāṭalkaḷip pāṭukiṉṟāḷ.
yāḻiṉ vaṭivu
ap pāliyāḻiṉ 1pattar māṉiṉ kuḷampu aḻuttiṉa iṭam pōṉṟu iraṇṭarukun tāḻntu naṭu uyarntuḷḷatu. iraṇṭu taliyum kūṭṭit tikkappeṟṟa pōrvi poṉṉiṟat tōliṉālāṉatu. iraṇṭu pattaruñ cērvataṟkuk kaṭāviya āṇikaḷ naṇṭiṉ kaṇ pōṉṟavi; vāy eṭṭānāḷ tiṅkaḷpōṉṟu uṇṇākkillātatu; kariya taṇṭu pāmpu taliyi nīṭṭiṉāṟpōṉṟa tōṟṟamuṭiyatu. vārkkaṭṭukkaḷ peṇkaḷ kiyilaṇiyum vaḷiyalkaḷ pōṉṟavi. viralāl alittu vācikkappaṭum narampukaḷ tiṉiyiṉ kuṟṟalarici pōṉṟavi.
paricil peṟṟuvarum pāṇaṉ porunaṉi etirppaṭal
ivvāṟu pāṇaṉ viṟaliyuṭaṉum taṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉum iḷippāṟi yirukka avvaḻiyē iṉṉōr pāṇaṉ taṉatu cuṟṟattōṭu varukiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉip pāṭip paricil peṟṟu varukiṉṟava ṉāvaṉ. vaṟumiyāl melivuṟṟirukkiṉṟa pāṇaṉi avaṉ nōkkik kūṟukiṉṟāṉ.
viḻāk koṇṭāṭuṅkālattuc cuṟṟattuṭaṉ mikunta cōṟṟi uṇṭu makiḻntum atu kaḻintapiṉ piṟi tōrviḻāk koṇṭāṭum ūri aṭintum tirikiṉṟa porunaṉē! yāḻiyuṭiya kūttarukkut talivaṉē! piṟar pukaḻi aracar capiyil uyarvaṭivikka valla vaṉē! yāṉ kūṟukiṉṟavaṟṟi virumpik kēṭpāyāka. vaḻi aṟiyāmiyāl ivvaḻiyit tappi vēṟoru vaḻiyiṟ ceṉṟu viṭātē. iv vaḻiyiṭattē nī eṉṉik kaṇṭatu nī muṟpiṟappiṟ ceyta nalviṉiyiṉ viḷivākum. uṉṉiyum uṉatu kariya cuṟṟattiyum eṉṟum varuttukiṉṟa paciyip pōkkikkoḷḷa virumpukiṉṟāyāyiṉ virivil eḻuntiru. yāṉum paḻa maraṅkaḷit tēṭi aliyum paṟavikaḷippōlap paricil taruvārit tēṭi alintēṉ.
taṉṉik karikāl vaḷavaṉ varavēṟṟup paricil vaḻaṅkiya vakiyiṉip pāṇaṉ eṭuttu moḻital
oru nāḷ karikāṟ cōḻaṉatu āravāramikka kōpuravāyili aṭintēṉ; vāyil kāppō ṉiṭamuṅ kūṟātu uḷḷē ceṉṟēṉ. celkiṉṟa makiḻcciyiṉāl eṉatu iḷippu nīṅkiṟṟu. eṉatu uṭukkiyiṉ kaṇkaḷ kipaṭutaliṉāl pāmpiṉ paṭattiluḷḷa poṟikaḷ pōṉṟa taḻumpukaḷ peṟ ṟiruntaṉa. nāṉ ataṉiṭattē iraṭṭit tāḷattip piṟappittu eṉatu vaṟumi tīrumpaṭi oru pāṭṭup pāṭiṉēṉ. appōtu viṭiveḷḷiyiṉ veḷiccam tōṉṟum viṭiyaṟkālam. uṭaṉē aracaṉ uṟaviṉarippōlat taṉṉiṭattil nāṉ uṟavu koḷḷumpaṭi pala mukamaṉ moḻikaḷ kūṟiṉāṉ; tāṉ eppoḻutum pārkkakkūṭiya aṇmiyil eṉṉi irukkumpaṭi ceytāṉ; eṉṉi aṉpuṭaṉ pārttāṉ; appārviyāl eṉatu elumpukaḷ urukumpaṭi kuḷirntaṉa; eṉatu āṭiyil īrum pēṉum iruntu āṭci ceytaṉa; atu pala tiyalkaḷiṭap paṭṭatāyum viyarviyāl naṉintatāyum iruntatu. avaṉ eṉatu paḻiya āṭiyik kaḷiyumpaṭi ceytāṉ. ataṟkup patil nūlpōṉaviṭam etu eṉṟu aṟiya muṭiyāta nuṇmiyuṭiyatum, pū vēlippāṭuṭiyatumākiya pāṭṭāṭiyi uṭukkumpaṭi tantāṉ. araṇ maṉiyilē aḻakiya āparaṇaṅkaḷi aṇinta peṇkaḷ iruntārkaḷ. avarkaḷ pāṭṭālum kūttālum aracaṉi makiḻvittārkaḷ. avarkaḷ poṉ kiṇṇattil 1kaḷḷi vārttut tantārkaḷ; avarkaḷ tarattara vaḻi naṭanta varuttam tīrum paṭi ataṉi niṟiya uṇṭēṉ; uṇṭataṉāl itu variyum kaḷḷuṇṇa villiyē eṉṉum varuttattip pōkkiṉēṉ. mālik kālattil kaḷippu mikuntavaṉāy araṇmaṉiyiṉ oru puṟattil taṅkiṉēṉ; tavañ ceyvār tamatu uṭampi aḻiyaviṭātu attavattāṟ peṟum payaṉip peṟuvatupōla vaḻi naṭanta varuttam ciṟitum illāmal pōkkiṉēṉ. kaḷ ḷuṇṭataṉāl uṇṭāṉa meyvaruttamallātu vēṟu varuttam yātumiṉṟi nittiri viṭṭeḻuntēṉ. aracaṉik kāṇpataṟku mutalnāḷ colluk kaṭaṅkāta vaṟumiyuṭiyavaṉāyiruntēṉ. aracaṉik kaṇṭa maṟṟa nāṭ kāliyil eṉṉik kaṇṭōr, nēṟṟu vantavaṉ ivaṉallaṉ eṉa aiyuṟṟa ṉar. nāṉ palavaki naṟumaṇap poruḷkaḷi uṭaliṟ pūciyiruntēṉ; ataṉāl vaṇṭukaḷ iṭiyaṟātu eṉṉi moyttaṉa. itu kaṉavāyiruk kalāmeṉṟu niṉittēṉ. itu kaṉavaṉṟu naṉavu eṉṟu eṉatu neñcu tuṇintu colliṟṟu. koṭiya vaṟumiyāl varuntiya eṉatu cuṟṟattiṉar makiḻcci yaṭintaṉar. “ippoḻutu māṟutalaṭintirunta pōtum nēṟṟu vanta iravalaṉē nī” eṉṟu eṉṉuṭaṉ vanta iḷiñar coṉṉārkaḷ. ataṉik kēṭṭa aracaṉ avarkaḷik kāvalāḷarkaḷ mūlam aḻittu avarkaḷukkuc ceyyum muṟimikaḷic ceytaṉuppiṉāṉ. yāṉum eṉ cuṟṟattiṉarum pullāṟṟiritta paḻutiyit tiṉṟu vaḷarnta cemmaṟikkiṭā yiṉ puḻukkiya iṟicciyi uṇṭōm; irumpukkampiyil kōttup poritta koḻutta iṟiccip poriyali vāyiṉ iṭappuṟattum valap puṟattum cērtti āṟṟi uṇṭu kaḷittōm. ivvāṟu iṟiccip pori yaliyum iṟiccip puḻukkaliyum uṇṭu aluttōm. iṉṉum pala vaṭi viṉiyuṭiya palakāraṅkaḷiyum uṇṭōm. kaḷḷuṇṭu pala nāṭkaḷik kaḻittōm. nīṅkaḷ cōṟum uṇṇutal vēṇṭum, eṉṟu kūṟi mulli muki pōṉṟa cōṟṟiyum porik kaṟiyiyum kaḻuttuvariyum nirampum paṭi aracaṉ uṇṇattantāṉ. pakalu miravum iṟicciyit tiṉṟataṉāl eṅkaḷ paṟkaḷ kolli nilatti uḻutakoḻuppōla muṉi maḻuṅkip pōyiṉa. iḷippāṟa nēramiṉṟi uṇavukaḷi uṇṭu veṟuttōm. nāṅkaḷ namatu ūrukkuc cellutal vēṇṭumeṉa orunāḷ avaṉiṭattil metuvākak kūṟiṉōm. atik kēṭṭuk kōpittavaṉpōl avaṉ yām varuntumpaṭi pārttu, “ematu kūṭṭatti viṭṭup pōkiṉṟīrō” eṉak kūṟiṉāṉ. avaṉ yāṉikaḷik kaṉṟukaḷuṭaṉ koṭuttāṉ; iṉṉum ūrtikaḷ, āṭikaḷ, aṇikalaṉkaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum tantāṉ. avaṟṟi vārikkoṇṭu iṉi ekkālamum emakku vaṟumi illiyākumpaṭi nām varukiṉṟōm.
karikālaṉiṉ ciṟappukkaḷ
iravalaṉē! karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉiṉ ciṟappukkaḷ cilavaṟṟik kūṟu kiṉṟēṉ kēḷ! avaṉ murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ cīṟṟampōṉṟa kōpamuṭiya vaṉ. avaṉ tāy vayiṟṟiliruntē aracurimiyip peṟṟup piṟantāṉ. tavaḻuṅkālam toṭṭē avaṉ mika valimi peṟṟiruntāṉ. avaṉ ātti māliyit tarittiruppāṉ. paṉaṅkuruttiṉāṟ kaṭṭiya māliyaṇinta cēraṉiyum, arattiṉvāy pōṉṟa vēppiliyāṟ ṟoṭutta māliyit taliyilaṇinta pāṇṭiyaṉiyum avaṉ veṇṇi eṉṉum pōrk kaḷattē porutu veṉṟāṉ.
iravalaṉē! avaṉmuṉ nī uṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉ uṉatu vaṟumi tōṉṟumpaṭi niṟpāyāyiṉ kaṟṟā taṉatu kaṉṟukkup pāl curantu koṭukka virumpuvatu pōla avaṉ uṅkaḷ vaṟumi nīṅkap pōtumāṉa paricil taruvāṉ; uṅkaḷ pāṭalkaḷik kēṭpataṉmuṉ koṭṭip pāciyiṉ vēr pōla aḻukkiyum tiyalkaḷiyumuṭiya uṅkaḷ tuṇikaḷik kaḷiyum paṭi ceyvāṉ; ciṟu kuñcaṅkaḷik kariyiluṭiya paṭṭāṭikaḷi uṭukkumpaṭi taruvāṉ; pūmaṇaṅ kamaḻkiṉṟa kāramuḷḷa kaḷḷip pōtum pōtum eṉṉumpaṭi poṉ vaḷḷattil vārttu nāḷtōṟum nalkuvāṉ; pāṭiṉiyiṉ curuṇṭa mayiriṭattē poṟṟāmari malaric cūṭṭuvāṉ; aṇintukoḷvataṟkup poṉṉari māliyiyum muttumāliyiyum koṭuppāṉ. yāṉit tantattiṉāl ceyta tāmari mukiyiṉiyuṭiya tēril nāṉku kutirikaḷippūṭṭi atil uṉṉi ēṟṟi ēḻaṭi piṉ ceṉṟu vaḻiviṭuvāṉ; piṉ “niliyillā ulakattup pukaḻi niṉintu nī peṟṟa paricilip piṟarkkuṅ koṭuttuc celluti” eṉak kūṟip pala maruta nilattu ūrkaḷiyum, pala yāṉikaḷiyun taruvāṉ.
cōḻanāṭṭu vaḷam
cōḻanāṭu mikka ceḻippuṭiyatu. tiṭarkaḷ tōṟum neṟkūṭukaḷ (kutir)kiṭakkum. makkaḷ teṉṉañ cōlikaḷiṉ naṭuvē vīṭukaḷamittu vāḻvar. kākkikaḷ irattam aḷinta cōṟṟi uṇṇum; uṇṭu vīṭṭic cūḻnta noccic ceṭiyiṉ niḻaliṟ poritta āmippārppukaḷi eṭuttup pacikkum pōtu uṇpataṟku maṟittuvikkum. kaṭaṟkari maṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷil uḻattiyar vaṇṭal iḻittu viḷiyāṭuvar. pākaṟ paḻatti yum palāppaḻattiyum tiṉṟa mayiṟ cēvalkaḷ vaḷinta kāñci marattilum marutilum iruntu peṭikaḷ aḻikkumpōtu paṟantu cellum; ceṉṟu yāḻiṉ ōci pōṉṟa vaṇṭiṉ pāṭalik kēṭṭu ataṟkup porunta nilavupōṉṟa maṇalil niṉṟu āṭum.
nāṭṭilē karumpum nellum ceḻittuvaḷarum; aṭumpum pakaṉṟiyum, puṉkum, ñāḻalum ēṉiya maraṅkaḷum neruṅki niṟkum. aṅkuṟivōr nāṭṭu vāḻkkiyi veṟuttārāyiṉ nīla niṟamuṭiya mullik koṭipaṭarnta kāṭu cārnta nilattē ceṉṟu vāḻvar. aṅkut taḷavum, mulliyum, tēṟṟāvum, koṉṟiyum kāyāvumākiya marañ ceṭikoṭikaḷ taḻittu niṟkum. mulli maruta nilattu vāḻvōr aṅku vāḻtali veṟuttārāyiṉ vēṟu nilattē ceṉṟu vāḻvar. kaṭaliṭattē iṟāl mīṉit tiṉṟa nāri puṉṉik kompil vantu taṅkum; piṉpu puṉṉi marattiṉmītu kaṭal cintukiṉṟa nīriṉ āravārattukkup payantu eḻuntu vāḻi marattil taṅkum. kaṭaṟkari ūrkaḷilē kulikaḷiyuṭiya teṅkukaḷum, kulikkamukum, kulivāḻikaḷum, pūkkaḷ malarnta curapuṉṉiyum kāṭci aḷikkum. avaṟṟiṉ ceṟivilē pērāntikaḷ uṟiyum. aṅku uṟiyum paratavar neytal nilatti veṟuttārkaḷāṉāl kuṟiñci nilattē ceṉṟu vāḻvar; tēṉum kiḻaṅkum viṟṟa kuṟiñci nila makkaḷ mīṉiṉ neyyōṭu kaḷḷiyum paṇṭamāṟṟuc ceytu pōvar. maruta nilattu vīṭukaḷil niṟkum kōḻikaḷ tiṉiyi uṇṇum. maliyiṭattē tirikiṉṟa mantikaḷ uppaṅkaḻiyil mūḻkum; kaḻiyiliruntu eḻumpiya nārikaḷ maliyiṭattut taṅkum. ivvaki nāṉku nilaṅkaḷum mayaṅki irukkum ulakattukku aruḷ ceytaṟkuc ceṅkōli eṭutta talivaṉ vāḻvāṉāka!
veyyiliṉ veppa mikutiyāl tāmariyum kulliyum tīyum; marak komparkaḷ kariyum; maliyiṭattu aruvikaḷ vaṟaḷum; mēkaṅkaḷ kaṭalnīri mukattal ceyyā. ivvakiyāṉa vaṟaṭcik kālattum nuriyit taliyiluṭiya kāvirinīr naṟik koṭiyiyum, narantam pulliyum, akiliyum, cantaṉamarattiyum nīrāṭum tuṟikaḷtōṟum taḷḷik kuḷatti lum marutanilat tūrkaḷilum pukum. nīrāṭu makaḷir nīr viḷiyāṭuvar. nel aṟuppōr arivāḷāl arinta neṟpōriṉi malipōl uyarumpaṭi aṭukkuvar. uḻavar tiṉamum kaṭāviṭṭup pōriṉi uḻakki nellit tiraṭṭuvar; tiraṭṭiya nellik kutirkaḷ niṟiyumpaṭi koṭṭuvar. varampu kaḷiyuṭiya vēlinilattil āyiram kalam nel viḷiyum. karikāṟ peruvaḷattāṉ ivvāṟu vaḷam poruntiya kāviri nāṭṭukkut talivaṉ. (iravalaṉē! nī kālantāḻātu ippoḻutē avaṉpāṟ ceṉṟu pāṭi uṉatum uṉatu cuṟṟattiṉaratum vaṟumiyik kaṭitiṟpōkki iṉitu vāḻvāyāka.)




3. 1ciṟupāṇāṟṟuppaṭi

mutuvēṉiṟ kālam
mūṅkilit tōḷākavuṭiya nilamakaḷukku malikaḷ mārpiṭattē poruntiya periya taṉaṅkaḷākum. mali ucciyiṉiṉṟum iṟaṅki āṟṟiṭik kuṟikaḷil piḷavupaṭṭup piṉ oṉṟu cērntu cellum kāṭṭāṟu avvaḻakiya taṉaṅkaḷil aṇiyappaṭum muttu vaṭamākum. kāṭṭāṟṟiṉāl kuḷircciyaṭinta maraṅkaḷ malarkaḷi niṟiyap pūkkum. kuyilkaḷ alakiṉāl kōtutalāl utirnta pūkkaḷ ularntu karumi aṭintirukkum; attōṟṟam iḷavēṉilākiya mātu taṉatu kūntali virittāṟ pōṉṟatu. kāṭṭiṭattē vēlpōṟ kūriya parukkik kaṟkaḷ kiṭakkum; mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil veyilāl veppamaṭinta avi vaḻi naṭappōriṉ kālkaḷik kiḻikkum; ākavē avarkaḷ meṉmiyāka naṭantu kaṭappamara niḻalil iruntu iḷippāṟip pōvar. mutuvēṉiṟ kālam ñāyiṟṟiṉ veppattāl pālittaṉmi uṭiyatāyirukkum. pālit taṉmiyāvatu kāliyum māliyum naṇpakalaṉṉa kaṭumi kūrac cōli tēmpik kūval māṟi nīrum niḻalumiṉṟi nilam payantuṟantu puḷḷum māvum pulampuṟṟu iṉpamiṉṟit tuṉpam perukuvatoru kālam.
viṟaliyiṉ tōṟṟam
ivvāṟu pālittaṉmiyuṭiya kāṭṭuvaḻiyē, aḻakiya mulli cūṭutaṟkaminta kaṟpuṭiya viṟali naṭattaliṉāl kaḷippaṭintu kaṭampamara niḻalil taṅki irukkiṉṟāḷ. avaḷuṭiya nōkku māṉ pōl maruṇṭa taṉmiyuṭiyatu; kūntal karumiyuṭiyatu; aṭikaḷ ōṭi iḷitta nāyiṉ nākkup pōṉṟavi; vāḻittaṇṭupōṟ paruttu oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu neruṅkiya toṭikaḷ yāṉiyiṉ tutikki pōṉṟavi; koṇṭi vāḻip potti pōṉṟatu; aṅku tēmal paṭarnta taṉaṅkaḷ kōṅkiṉ muki pōṉṟavi; paṟkaḷ aḻaku vāyntavi.
pāṇaṉ
pāṇaṉ poṟkampi pōṉṟu muṟukkiya narampuṭiya cīṟiyāḻi iṭap pakkattē taḻuvi naṭṭa pāṭi eṉṉum paṇṇi vācikkiṉṟāṉ. paricil aḷikkum vaḷḷalkaḷip peṟāmal kāṭṭiṭattē ippāṇaṉ ivvāṟu iruntu varuntum pōtu, nallik kōṭaṉip pāṭip paricil peṟṟu mīḷkiṉṟa oru pāṇaṉ avaṉi etirppaṭṭuk kūṟukiṉṟāṉ:
cēra nāṭṭiṉ ceḻumi
pāṇar talivaṉē! mēṟkē uḷḷa cēranāṭu mikka ceḻippuṭiyatu. aṅku erumikaḷ koḻutta mīṉkaḷ noṟuṅkumpaṭi vayalkaḷil naṭantuceṉṟu ceṅkaḻunīrp pūvi uṇṇum; uṇṭu miḷaku paṭarnta palāmara niḻalil kāṭṭu mallikiyākiya paṭukkiyil kiṭakkum; kāṟṟukkuk koṭi aciyum; mañcaḷiṉ melliya ilikaḷ ataṉ mutukit taṭavum; ivvāṟu kiṭantu atu tāṉ uṇṭa ceṅkaḻunīrppūvittēṉ nāṟumpaṭi meṉṟu aciyiṭṭut tuyilkoḷḷum. ivvāṟu ceḻumi mikunta nāṭukaḷi yum, valiya tōḷiṉiyum, ōṭukiṉṟa tēriṉiyumuṭiya cēraṉatu vañcimūtūril kiṭikkum paricilō nalliyak kōṭaṉ koṭukkum pariciliviṭak kuṟivuṭiyatu.
koṟki nakariṉ vaḷam
teṟkē uḷḷa koṟki nakarukkut talivaṉ pāṇṭiyaṉ. annakar tiri koḻikkum kaṭali elliyākavuṭiyatu. kaṭaṟkarikaḷiliruntu uppu vāṇikariṉ vaṇṭikaḷ cellum. 1avvaṇṭikaḷiṉ accuttaṅkum kuṭaṅkaḷ tātu paṉikkiṉṟa pūkkaḷuṭiya muṟṟiya nuṇāttaṭiyi uḷiyiṉāl kaṭintu ceytu cevvarakku pūcappaṭṭavi. kiṭēcciyāl ceyta pūmāliyi ceviyaṭiyil neṟṟimāliyāka aṇiyap peṟṟa valiya erutukaḷ vaṇṭiyi iḻuttuc cellum. uppu vāṇikar piḷḷikaḷip pōla vaḷarkkiṉṟa kuraṅkukaḷ vaṇṭikaḷōṭu varum. uppu vāṇikariṉ kuḻantikaḷ peṇkaḷiṉ paṟkaḷ pōṉṟa veḷḷiya muttiyum vāḷpōṉṟa kiḷiñciliyum 2ariyilē āparaṇamāka aṇintiruppar. akkuḻantikaḷōṭu mantikaḷ kilukiluppiyik kiyil vittuk kilukki viḷiyāṭum. tamatu kaṇavarit taḻuvum umaṭṭiyar kūntali aintu vakiyākak kōti muṭintiruppar. ivvāṟu ciṟappuṭiya koṟki nakari yum veṇkoṟṟak kuṭiyiyum tēriyum muttu māliyiyumuṭiya pāṇṭiyaṉiṭattiṟ ceṉṟu peṟum paricililum pārkka nalliyakkōṭaṉiṭam peṟum paricil periyatākum.
cōḻa nāṭṭiṉ ciṟappu
