cōḻanāṭṭiluḷḷa maruta nilattē malipōla vaḷarnta kaṭampa maraṅkaḷ nerukkamākap pūttirukkum. ampūkkaḷiṉ tampalap pūccikaḷip pōṉṟa tātu utirntu parantiruttalāl nīruṇṇum tuṟiyiṉ kari ōviyam pōl aḻaku peṟṟu viḷaṅkum. attuṟiyiṭattē malarntirukkum tāmari malarākiya pīṭattil tumpikaḷ tamatu peṭiyit taḻuvi makiḻntiruntu ‘cīkāmaram’ eṉṉum paṇṇippāṭum. kiḻakkup pakkattē ivvāṟu ceḻippu mikkuḷḷa nāṭṭukkut talivaṉākiya cōḻaṉ tūṅkeyil eṟinta vīravaḷi viḷaṅkum kiyuṭiyaṉ. avvaracaṉatu uṟanti nakariṟ ceṉṟu peṟum paricil nalliyak kōṭaṉiṭam peṟum pariciliviṭac ciṟiyatākum.
paruvam poyyātu maḻi peytalāl. celvamikunta malip pakkattē tiriyum mayil kūviyatik kēṭṭa pēkaṉ eṉṉum vaḷḷal atu kuḷir mikutiyāl kūvukiṉṟatu eṉa eṇṇit taṉatu pōrviyi ataṟku aḷittāṉ. ciṟiya pūkkaḷiyuṭiya mullik koṭi, ciṟupuṉṉiyiṉ naṟiyapū tēṉ tuḷikkiṉṟa vaḻiyiṭattē koḻukom piṉṟik koḻuntu eṟintu niṉṟatu. ataṉik kaṇṭa paṟampumalikkut talivaṉākiya pāri atu paṭartaṟkut taṉatu tērik koṭuttāṉ.
koṭip perum vaḷḷal eḻuvar
kāri eṉṉum vaḷḷal maṇiyiyum taliyāṭṭattiyu muṭiya kutiriyōṭu taṉatu nāṭṭiyum irappavarkkuk koṭuttāṉ. āy eṉṉum vaḷḷal pāmpu īṉṟu koṭutta oḷiyuṭiya nīlap paṭṭāṭiyi āliṉ kīḻ amarnta kaṭavuḷukku aṉpu mikutiyāl koṭuttāṉ.
iḷamiyik koṭuttaliṉ amiḻtattiṉ taṉmiyuṭiya nellikkaṉiyik kamukam pūkkaḷuṭiya pakkamaliyiṉiṉṟu peṟṟa atikaṉ ataṉi oḷaviyārukku aḷittāṉ. naḷḷi eṉṉum vaḷḷal taṉṉi naṭpuc ceytōr makiḻntu illaṟam naṭattutaṟku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷi avarkku nāṭōṟum nalkiṉāṉ.
kāri eṉṉum kutiriyip peṟṟa kāri eṉṉum vaḷḷaluṭaṉ pōr ceyta ōri eṉṉum vaḷḷal mutirnta curapuṉṉiyum kuṟiya malikaḷu muṭiya nallanāṭukaḷik kūttāṭuvōrukkuk koṭuttāṉ.
nalliyak kōṭaṉiṉ ōṅku pukaḻ
pāṇar taliva! ivveḻuvaruṭiya koṭikaḷiyum tāṅki niṟkum oruvaṉākiya nalliyak kōṭaṉiṭattu nī uṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉ celvā yāka. avaṉ pakivaratu mārpilum mukattilum veṭṭiṉa vīravāḷiyum, pulipōṉṟa valimiyiyu muṭiyaṉ; ōviyar kuṭiyil utittōṉ. naṟum pūkkaḷiyuṭiya curapuṉṉiyiyum, akiliyum, cantaṉamarattiyum nīrāṭun tuṟiyil puṉalāṭum makaḷirkkut teppamākat tarukiṉṟa nīrmōtum māvilaṅkiyiṉ aracar tōṉṟutaṟku iṭaṉāyuḷḷa ōviyar kuṭiyiṟ piṟantōṉ. avaṉatu piṉṉiṭāta aṭikaḷil vīrakkaḻal kiṭantu aciyum; maḻipōṉṟu vaḻaṅkukiṉṟa avaṉ kikaḷō kūttari varavēṟṟup piṭikaḷik koṭukkum.
paricil peṟa muṉ iravalaṉiṉ vaṟumi
iravalaṉē! yāṅkaḷ nalliyakkōṭaṉiyum avaṉ tantiyiṉ maliyiṭattēyuḷḷa celvattiyum pāṭic ceṉṟēm. appoḻutu namatu vaṟumi collukkaṭaṅkātatāka viruntatu. namatu vīṭṭil tāyiṉ muli uṇṇāmal irukkamāṭṭāta nāykkuṭṭikaḷ paci mikutiyāl cattamiṭṭaṉa. aṭukkaḷiyiṉ iṟapput taṭikaḷ kaṭṭaṟuntu kīḻē viḻuntaṉa. cuvaril eḻunta kaṟiyāṉ vīṭṭiṉ variccukaḷi uṇṭatu. oṭuṅkiya nuṇṇiya iṭiyum vaḷiyalaṇinta kiyum uṭiyavaḷum paciyāl varuntukiṉṟavaḷumākiya kiṇiyaṉuṭiya makaḷ kuppiyiṉiṉṟum nakattiṉāṟ kiḷḷiya kīriyi uppillāmal camittuk kariya cuṟṟattuṭaṉ paci nīṅkumpaṭi uṇṭāḷ. ivvakiyāṉa koṭiya vaṟumiyum paciyum iṉi namakku orupōtum illiyākumpaṭi nām matañcoriyum yāṉikaḷiyum periya tēriyum peṟṟu avviṭattiṉiṉṟum varukiṉṟōm.
neytalnilam
iravalaṉē! kaṭaṟkariyiṭattuḷḷa tāḻi aṉṉam pōl pūvi īṉum; cerunti poṉ pōṟ pūvi malarum; kaḻiyiṭattuḷḷamuḷḷi nīlampōṟ pūvi malarttum; puṉṉi muttuppōla arumpukaḷi mukiḻk kum. ivvakik kaḻicūḻnta ūrkaḷiyum kuḷircci poruntiya kuḷaṅkaḷi yumuṭiya eyiṟpaṭṭiṉattē celvāyāka. aṅku muṟukkaviḻnta arumpu kaḷāl kaṭṭiya māliyi aṇintavaṉum tōṭṭaṅkaḷ pala uṭiyavaṉu mākiyakiṭaṅkil eṉṉum ūrkkaracaṉik kāṇpāy. avaṉip pāṭuvāyā yiṉ avaṉ ataṟku makiḻntu viṟaliyum uṉatu cuṟṟamum makiḻntu uṇ ṇumpaṭi cuṭṭa kuḻalmīṉit taruvāṉ; paratavar, nīviruṇṇumpaṭi kaḷḷi yum tarukuvar. avar uṅkaḷukkuk koṭukkum kaḷ, tiriyiṉāṟ koṇṭu varappaṭṭu uṟaṅkukiṉṟa oṭṭakam pōṟkuvikkappaṭṭa akil viṟakip periya tōḷum mati pōl maṟuvillāta mukamum vēlpōṉṟa kaṇṇu muṭiya nuḷicciyar tīmūṭṭi erittuk kāycci arittatākum.
mulli nilam
neytal nilattik kaṭantu kāṭṭuvaḻiyē celvāyāṉāl aṅku avari pavaḷampōl pūttirukkum; kāyā mayiliṉ kaḻuttuppōl pūtta kariya arumpukaḷi uṭiyatāyirukkum; kāntaṭkoṭikaḷ kiviralkaḷ pōl malarntu kāṭciyaḷikkum; vaḻiyiṟ cellumiṭattu vāṉavil tōṉṟum; mullik koṭikaḷ paṭarntirukkum. veṭippukaḷil nīr kutikkum aruvikaḷiyuṭiya maliyil ñāyiṟu maṟiyum. ivvāṟu eḻum mālik kālattē nī vēlūri aṭivāyāyiṉ nī uṉatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉē uṇṭu paci tīrumpaṭi camitta cōṟṟip puḷiṅkaṟiyōṭum āmāṉiṉ cūṭṭiṟicci yōṭum eyiṟṟiyar taruvar.
āmūriṉ maruta nila vaḷam
māli toṭuttāṟ pōṉṟu malarnta pūkkaḷuṭiya kiḷitta kāñcimarattiṉ kiḷikaḷil irukkum poṉṉiṟa vāyiṉiyum nīla maṇiyiṉ niṟattiṉiyumuṭiya ciccilip paṟavi nīril mūḻki mīṉkaḷip piṭikkum. ciccilip paṟaviyiṉ kālkaḷ kiḻitta ilikaḷuṭiya tāmari yiṉ pūkkaḷil tēṉ nukarkiṉṟa nīla niṟattiṉiyum civanta kaṇṇiṉiyu muṭiya vaṇṭukaḷ tiṅkaḷic cērnta karum pāmpu pōlat tōṉṟum.
ivvāṟu aḻakiya vayalkaḷiyuṭiya āmūr, ariya kāvaliyum, akaṉṟa vīṭukaḷiyum, antaṇar kuṭiyiruppiyum uṭiyatu. vīṭṭiṉ uḷḷē irukkum uḻavaruṭiya maṉiviyar yāṉiyiṉ tutikki pōṉṟu piṉṉāl toṅkum piṉṉiya kūntaliyum, vaḷi aṇinta kiyiṉiyu muṭiya tam piḷḷikaḷikkoṇṭu num ellōriyum pōkātapaṭi taṭuppar; taṭuttup pūṇulakkiyāṟ kuttiya ariciyic camitta cōṟṟi naṇṭum pīrkkaṅkāyum kalanta puḻukkuṭaṉ uṇṇumpaṭi taruvar.
nalliyakkōṭaṉiṉ mūtūr
nalliyak kōṭaṉatu viḻāk koṇṭāṭum mūtūr cēymiyi laṉṟu. mēl nōkki erikiṉṟa neruppuc cāyntāl otta nākkum, veḷḷāṭṭu maṟikaḷ kiṭantu acikiṉṟa kātum, kavitta kālumuṭiya pēymakaḷ niṇattit tiṉṟu cirikkumpōtu viḷaṅkit tōṉṟukiṉṟa paṟkaḷ pōṉṟa vum, pōrkkaḷattē piṇattalikaḷip periya kālkaḷāl taḷḷutalāl civappēṟiṉavumākiya nakaṅkaḷiyuṭiya yāṉikaḷiṉ mataaruvi cintit teruvil eḻum tūciyi aṭakkum.
avaṉatu kōpuravāyil pāṇarukkum aṟivuṭiyōrukkum aṭikkappaṭātu
kōpuravāyil mēru kaṇviḻittup pārttāṟpōl, tōṉṟum. kiṇip porunarukkum, aṟivuṭiyōrukkum antaṇarukkum ataṉ vāyil orupōtum aṭikkappaṭātu; ēṉiyōrukku aṭikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. aracaṉ piṟar taṉakkuc ceyta naṉṟiyic ceyvāṉ; aṟivum oḻukkamum illātār avaṉiṭattil iṭam peṟār.
nalliyakkōṭaṉiṉ iṉiya paṇpukaḷ
taṉṉi nōkkiṉārkku avaṉ ekkālamum iṉiya mukamuṭiyaṉ. taṉatu vīrattik kaṇṭu añci aṭipaṇintārkkut taṉṉuṭaṉ iruppōr pukaḻumpaṭi aruḷuvāṉ; pōrmukattē keṭṭa taṉatu paṭiyit tāṅki vāḷvaliyiṟ ciṟanta vīrar pukaḻumpaṭi tāṉ virumpiyavaṟṟi muṭippāṉ; peṇkaḷ vacattaṉākāta taṉmiyaṉ; paricilar taramaṟintu avar peṟattak kavaṟṟik koṭukkum iyalpiṉaṉ. kalvi yillāta paricil vēṇṭuvōrk kum koṭātiruttali mēṟkoḷḷātu avarkaḷ tarattukkut takkavaṟṟik koṭuppāṉ; iyalici nāṭakaṅkaḷālum iṉiya moḻikaḷālum makiḻc ciyit tarum kūṭṭattiṉarōṭu pala viṇ mīṉkaḷukku naṭuvirukkum matiyam pōl viḷaṅkuvāṉ.
kuraṅkaṉ kiyip pāmpu cuṟṟiyatu pōṉṟa yāḻiṉ vārkkaṭṭu
iravalaṉē! nī ivviyalpuṭiyaṉāy irukkum nalliyak kōṭaṉikkaṇṭatum, kuraṅku pāmpiṉ taliyip piṭittāl appāmpu orukāl iṟukavum orukāl nekiḻavum ataṉ kiyi eppaṭic cuṟṟumō appaṭiyē yāḻttaṇṭiṉiṭattē nekiḻa vēṇṭiya iṭattu nekiḻntum iṟuka vēṇṭiya iṭattu iṟukiyum cuṟṟiṉa narampu tuvakkum vārkkaṭṭum, kāvi niṟam ūṭṭappaṭṭa pōrviyum, amiḻtattit tammiṭattē potinta taṉmiyuṭiya narampukaḷuṭiya cīṟiyāḻik kiyil eṭuppāyāka. eṭuttu aracaṉ, uvāttiyāyaṉ, tāy, tanti, tamiyaṉ mutaliyōrkkup palakālum kuvitta kiyi yuṭiyavaṉē! vīrar eṟitaṟku makiḻntu koṭutta mārpiyuṭiyavaṉē! ēriṉiyuṭiya kuṭikaḷukku niḻal ceyyum ceṅkōluṭiyavaṉē! eṉṟu avaṉatu cila pukaḻkaḷik kūṟuvāyāka.
aracaṉ pāṇarkkup paricilaḷikkum muṟimi
nī avvāṟu kūṟuvataṉmuṉ mūṅkil āṭiyi urittāṟpōṉṟa kuṟṟamillāta uṭiyi uṭukkumpaṭi taruvāṉ. pāmpiṉ nañcu ēṟi mayaṅkiṉāṟ pōla mayakkam tarum kaṭṭeḷi vittaruvāṉ. vīmacēṉaṉatu maṭinūlil kūṟiya muṟippaṭi camitta cōṟṟip poṟkalatti liṭṭut tāṉ uṭaṉ niṉṟu uṇṇumpaṭi ceyvāṉ. muṭivēntariṉ araṇkaḷi aḻittu appakivar nāṭṭiṟ peṟṟa poruḷik koṇṭu tammi virumpivantavarkaḷatum, pāṇaratum vaṟumiyip pōkkuvāṉ. taccar uḷikoṇṭu vēlippāṭu ceyta tērōṭu yāṉi, kutiri, aṇikalaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟit tantu uṉṉic cellumpaṭi vaḻi viṭuvāṉ. ātalāl, nī kuṟiñci nilattiyuṭiyavaṉum taḻiviravit toṭutta māli yaṇinta vaṉum perum pukaḻuṭiyavaṉumākiya nalliyakkōṭaṉiṭattuc celvāyāka.






4. 1perumpāṇāṟṟuppaṭi

pāṇaṉum cuṟṟattiṉarum alintu tirital
mutuvēṉiṟ kālattil orunāḷ pala katirkaḷiyuṭiya ñāyiṟu paranta ākāyattil niṟinta iruḷippōkki viḷaṅkukiṉṟatu. ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum valañceykiṉṟa mēruvi uṭiyatum kaṭal cūḻntatumākiya ivvulakilē tammi ātarippār iṉmiyāl yāḻi iṭappakkattē aṇitta perumpāṇaṉ oruvaṉ taṉatu kariya periya cuṟṟattuṭaṉ alintu tirikiṉṟāṉ. avaṉ tōṭpuṟattē aṇittirukkum yāḻ uḷnākkillāta vaṟuvāyuṭiyatu; ataṉ kavikkaṭi piṟi ēntiṉāṟ pōṉṟatu. vārkkaṭṭup peṇkaḷ kiyil aṇiyum vaḷiyali oppa nekiḻavēṇṭiya iṭam nekiḻntu iṟuka vēṇṭiya iṭam iṟukappeṟṟatu; kariya periya taṇṭu nīlamaṇi oḻukiṉāṟpōṉṟatu; narampukaḷ iḻukkappaṭṭa poṟkampikaḷ pōṉṟaṉa; pōrvi viriyāta kamukam pūvippōlat tiyalaḻuntumpaṭi tōli mūṭṭic ceyyappaṭṭaṉa.

paricil peṟṟu varum pāṇaṉ perum pāṇaṉi etirppaṭṭatu
ivvaki yāḻi iṭattōḷmītu cārttiya pāṇaṉ, maḻi illāmiyāl paḻutta marattit tēṭat tiriyum paṟavipōla ōriṭattil taṅkāmal āvicūḻnta maliyiṭattil ōṭittirikiṉṟāṉ. toṇṭimāṉ iḷantiriyaṉippāṭi avaṉpāl paricil peṟṟu mīḷkiṉṟapāṇaṉ oruvaṉ ipperum pāṇaṉi vaḻiyiṭattē etirppaṭukiṉṟāṉ; avaṉ ivaṉuṭiya melivik kaṇṭu kūṟukiṉṟāṉ: poli viḻinta tōṟṟattiṉiyum kalviyi veṟuttuk kūṟukiṉṟa nāviṉiyum uṭiya iravalaṉē! yāṉ iḷantiriyaṉatu aracirukkiyākiya kāñci nakariṉiṉṟum piṟarkkuk koṭukkak kuṟivupaṭāta celvattiyum, veḷḷiya taliyāṭṭatti yuṭiya kutirikaḷiyum yāṉikaḷiyum paricilāka vārikkoṇṭu varukiṉṟēṉ. cēracōḻa pāṇṭiyariṉum mikka talimi yuṭiyavaṉum cōḻar kuṭiyiṟ piṟantavaṉumākiya iḷantiriyaṉi nīyum uṉatu cuṟṟat tiṉarum niṉippīrāyiṉ uṅkaḷ vaṟumi oḻintuviṭum. nīvir cella vēṇṭiya vaḻiyiṉ iyalpiṉik kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. kēṭpīrāka!

iravalaṉē! avaṉuṭiya nakaril vaḻippōkkari alaṟum paṭi veṭṭi avaṉ poruḷik koḷḷi koḷḷum tīyōr illi. avaṉuṭiya kāṭṭil iṭiyēṟum iṭiyātu; pāmpukaḷum tīṇṭā; puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷum tīṅkiḻiyā. ivviyalpuṭiya kāṭṭiṭattē nīṅkaḷ iruntu iḷippāṟic celluṅkaḷ.
uppēṟṟic cellum umaṇar vaṇṭikaḷ

kāṭṭiṭattē cellum kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷ uṇṭu; avvaḻi kaḷil umaṇariṉ uppēṟṟiya vaṇṭikaḷ cellum. avvaṇṭi kaḷiṉ accumaram taṅkum kuṭaṅkaḷ mattaḷattiṉ vaṭiviṉa. akkuṭaṅ kaḷil accumaram māṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. accumarattiṉūṭē ēṇik kāl pōṉṟu nīṇṭa tiraṇṭa maraṅkaḷ kaṭāvappaṭṭirukkum. am maraṅkaḷil kuttukkālkaḷ pōla uyaramāṉa kampukaḷ nāṭṭappaṭ ṭirukkum. kampukaḷ nāṭṭiya muṉpuṟam tottuḷippāyāl vēyappaṭ ṭirukkum. atu, tiṉippuṉatti yāṉimēyātu iruntu kāval ceyva taṟku amitta paraṇmītu kaṭṭiya kuṭilippōl tōṟṟamaḷikkum. ataṉ muṉpuṟattē kōḻik kūṭukaḷ kiṭakkum. mēlē marattiṉāṟ ceytatum yāṉiyiṉ muḻantāḷ pōṉṟatumākiya uṟi tuḷi iṭṭuk kaṭṭit toṅka viṭappaṭṭirukkum. ataṉmītu, nāṭakamakaḷir āṭukaḷattil koṭṭappaṭum mattaḷatti oppa varintu kaṭṭappaṭṭa kāṭippāṉikaḷ vikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. umaṇap peṇ vaṇṭiyiṉ muṉpuṟattē yiruntu erutukaḷi aṭittu ōṭṭuvāḷ. avaḷ kiyiṭattē piḷḷiyiyum ataṟkuk kāvalāka pūvōṭu kūṭiya vēppiliyiyum vittiruppāḷ. valuvēṟiṉa uṭaliṉa rākiya umaṇar taḻiyiṉāl toṭutta māliyi mārpiṭattē aṇin tiruppar; vaṇṭiyi iḻuttuc cellum erutukaḷ iḷittu viṭṭāl avikkup patil pūṭṭutaṟkup pala erutukaḷi aṭittu ōṭṭic celvar. pala tuḷikaḷiyuṭiya nukattil orumukappaṭak kayiṟṟiṉāṟ kaṭṭiya pala erutukaḷ vaṇṭikaḷi iḻuttuc cellum. umaṇar avaṟṟiṉ pakkattē accu muṟiyātapaṭi kāttucceṉṟu uppiṉ viliyik kūṟuvar. ivvaki vaṇṭikaḷ pala kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷiṟ cellum.

vaṇikar miḷaku potikaḷik kaḻutikaḷil ēṟṟic cellum kāṭci
piṟa nāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vanta vaṇikar māṇikkam, muttu, cantaṉam mutaliyavaṟṟi evviṭaṅkaḷilum koṇṭu ceṉṟu viṟpar. avarkaḷiṉ mārpil ampukaḷ kiḻittalāl uṇṭāṉa puṇkaḷ āṟiya taḻumpukaḷ kiṭakkum. avarkaḷ ariyiṟkaṭṭiya uṭiyilē uṭivāḷ ceruki yiruppar; yāṉit tantattik kaṭintu vāḷiyiṭṭu ataṉi variyuṭiya tuṇiyil kōttut tōḷiṭattē toṅkaviṭṭiruppar; kālil cerupput tarittiruppar. ivviyalpiṉarākiya vaṇikar palāppaḻattip pōṉṟu otta kaṉamākac cērkkappaṭṭa miḷaku potikaḷic cumantu cellum kaḻuti kaḷiṉ piṉṉē kavarpaṭṭa vaḻikaḷāṟ celvar. kaḻutikaḷukku nīṇṭa cevikaḷum mutukilē taḻumpukaḷum uṇṭu.
pālinila makkaḷiṉ kuṭicikaḷ

ivvāṟu vaṇṭikaḷum kaḻutikaḷum celkiṉṟa vaḻikaḷ kūṭukiṉṟa cantiyil aracaṉāl niyamikkappaṭṭa cuṅkaṅkoḷvōr viṟpaṭiyuṭaṉ kāttu niṟpar. avar taṅkiniṟkum kāṭṭiṭattil eyiṉar kuṭiyiruppu uṇṭu. eyiṉariṉ ciṟu kuṭicikaḷ aṇilum eliyum nuḻiya muṭiyātapaṭi īntiṉ ōliyāl vēyappaṭṭu muṭpaṉṟiyiṉ mutukupōlat tōṉṟum. piḷḷi kaḷip peṟṟa eyiṟṟiyar akkuṭicikaḷiṉ orupuṟattē māṉ tōlil muṭaṅkik kiṭappar. ēṉiya peṇkaḷ pāriyiṉāl karampi nilattik kiḷaṟi eṟumpu cērttuvitta pullariciyi eṭuttu varuvar; eṭuttuvantu 1pārvimāṉ kaṭṭi niṟkum viḷāmaram niḻal ceykiṉṟa muṟṟattilē tōṇṭappaṭṭuḷḷa nila uralil iṭuvar; iṭṭu vayira ulakkiyiṉāl kuttuvar; āḻnta kiṇaṟṟil uvarnīri moḷvar; moṇṭu koṇṭuvantu paḻiya oṟuvāy pōṉa pāṉiyilē ulivārttu murinta aṭuppilē vittu ākkuvar. avar kuṭicikaḷi nīvir aṭivīrāyiṉ avarkaḷ tām camitta pullaricic cōṟṟi uppuk kaṇṭattuṭaṉ uṇṇumpaṭi tēk kiliyil iṭṭuttaruvar.

kāṉavar paṉṟi vēṭṭiyāṭutal
pāli nilattē nīriṉmiyāl alintu tirikiṉṟa māṉkaḷiṉ aṭiccuvaṭuṭiya vaḻikaḷ uṇṭu. avvaḻikaḷiṉ pakkattē maḻi vaṟaṇṭa kālattu nīr niṟṟaṟkut tōṇṭiya kuḻikaḷ kāṇappaṭum. kāṉavar avaṟṟiṟ patuṅkiyiruntu naṭu iravil nīruṇṇa varukiṉṟa attippūppōṉṟu vaḷinta maruppuṭiya paṉṟikaḷi vēṭṭiyāṭuvar. pakaṟkālattē vaḷinta vēliyiṭattē valikaḷi māṭṭi, aṅkānta vāyuṭiya nāy kaḷuṭaṉē ceṉṟu putarkaḷi aṭittut tāmarip pūviṉ puṟavitaḻ pōṉṟa ceviyuṭiya muyalkaḷi avaṟṟiṟkiṭavāmal valiyiṭattē cellumpaṭi ōṭṭi vēṭṭiyāṭuvar.
ivviṭattēviṭṭu appāṟ celvīrkaḷāṉāl eyiṉariṉ kōṭṭi tōṉṟum. ataṉ uyarnta matiṟcuvar ūkampullāl vēyappaṭṭirukkum. uḷḷē muḷvēliyālum ataṉ puṟattē kāvaṟ kāṭṭālum cūḻappaṭṭa akaṉṟa vīṭukaḷ kāṇappaṭum. avaṟṟiṉ vāyilil caṅkiliyiṟ kaṭṭiya nāykaḷ kāval kāttu niṟkum. tiraṇṭa maraṅkaḷiṉāl vīṭukaḷiṉ uṭ katavukaḷ tāḻiṭappaṭṭirukkum. kaḻukukaḷ niriyāka irukkum vīṭukaḷiṉ uṭpuṟattē pakivarik kuttiyatāl kūrmaḻuṅkiya vēlkaḷum, maṇi kaṭṭiya paricikaḷum, viṟkaḷum, ampukaḷum cārttivikkappaṭṭirukkum. talivāyilkaḷil maliyiṭattuḷḷa tēṉkūṭṭip pōṉṟa kīḻppuṟatti uṭiya ampuk kaṭṭukaḷum mikka ōciyuṭiya paṟiyum tūṅkum. iv viyalpiṉatākiya ūriṭattē celvīrāyiṉ īntiṉ vitipōṉṟa cōṟṟi nāy vēṭṭiyāṭik koṇṭuvanta caṅkumaṇi pōṉṟa muṭṭikaḷi yuṭiya uṭumpiṉ poriyalālē maṟittu eyiṉar tara nīvir maṉikaṭōṟum peṟukuvir.

kuṟiñci nilattu maṟavaṉ
pāli nilattik kaṭantu appāṟ celvīrāyiṉ kuṟiñci nilatti aṭivīrkaḷ. annilattut talivaṉ tōṉṟiya maṟakkuṭiyiluḷḷōr valimiyāl koḷḷiyaṭittuṇṇum uṇaviṉiyum, vāṭpōr ceyyum toḻiliyumuṭiyar. akkuṭiyiluḷḷa cūṟkoṇṭa makaḷir yāṉi etirppaṭṭālum, pāmpu tammītu ēṟi ūrntu ceṉṟālum, mēkam iṭintālum añcār. ivvāṟu maṟam mikka kuṭiyiṟ piṟanta annāṭṭut talivaṉ taṉatu coṟkēḷāta pakivaruṭiya nāṭṭakattē tuṇivar kaḷuṭaṉ kuṟivitta vilaṅkit tappāmaṟ piṭikkiṉṟa vēṭṭi nāyip pōṟ celvāṉ; avarkaḷiṉ pacukkūṭṭaṅkaḷik kavarntuvantu kaḷḷukku viliyākak koṭuppāṉ. piṉ taṉatu vīṭṭilē vaṭikkappaṭṭa kaḷḷi uṇṭu palarum kūṭum ūrnaṭuvē uḷḷa marattaṭiyilē kiṭāyi aṟuttu ataṉ iṟicciyi uṇṭu mattaḷaṅkoṭṭa iṭattōḷi valappuṟattē vaḷittu makiḻcci uṇṭākumpaṭi āṭuvāṉ.

mulli nilattu ūr
ivviyalpiṉatākiya ūrik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl mulli nilattu ūrkaḷi aṭivīrkaḷ. aṅku vīṭukaḷ kuṟukiya taṭikaḷik kālāka iṟukki avikaḷ mītu kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. kālkaḷil āṭṭu mantikaḷ niṉṟu tiṉṉumpaṭi taḻikaḷ kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. vīṭṭiṉ vāyilkaḷil ciṟu putarkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. vīṭṭiṉ katavukaḷ kayiṟṟāl variyappaṭṭavi. kayiṟṟi ṉāl varintu varaku vikkōl parappappaṭṭa paṭukkiyil kiṭāyiṉ tōli virittu ataṉ mītu mutiyavaṉ kāvalākap paṭuttiruppāṉ. muṟṟattē aṟiyappaṭṭuḷḷa muḷikaḷil pacukkaḷik kaṭṭum kayiṟukaḷ kiṭakkum ūric cūḻntu vēliyiṉ pakkattē veḷḷāṭum cemmaṟiyāṭum paṭut tirukkum.

mōr viṟkum iṭippeṇ
ivviyalpiṉatākiya mulli nilattu ūriṭattē paṟavikaḷ tuyil eḻukiṉṟa viṭiyaṟkālattē iṭip peṇkaḷ mattukaḷ āravārikkumpaṭi tayirik kaṭintu veṇṇeyi eṭuppar. tāḷuruvi acikiṉṟa kātum poṉṉiṟa mēṉiyumuṭiya āymakaḷ puḷḷiyākat tayirteṟitta pāṉiyi luḷḷa mōrip pūvāṟceyta cummāṭṭiṉ mītu taliyilvittuk kuṟiñci nilattē ceṉṟu viṟpāḷ; viṟṟataṉāl kiṭitta nel mutaliyavaṟṟik koṇṭu cuṟṟattāri uṇṇumpaṭi ceyvāḷ. kuṟiya kariya kūntali yuṭiya iṭimakaḷ tāṉ neyyi viṟṟavilikkuk kaṭṭiyākiya pacum poṉṉi vāṅkāḷāy pālerumiyiyum, nalla pacuviyum erumi nākiyum koḷvāḷ. ivviyalpiṉarākiya peṇkaḷum vāyimaṭittuc cīḻkki aṭikkiṉṟa varumākiya iṭiyarum uṟiyum kuṭiyiruppil taṅkuvīrāyiṉ avarkaḷ cilutta tiṉiyaricic cōṟṟip pāluṭaṉ taruvārkaḷ.

iṭiyar manti mēykkum pulveḷi
mulli nilattu ūrik kaṭantu cellum pōtu mantikaḷi mēykkum iṭiyarik kāṇpīrkaḷ. cerupput tarittiruttalāl avar aṭikaḷil taḻumpu ēṟiyirukkum; kōṭāriyik kiyil piṭittiruppatāl avarkaḷiṉ ki kāyttu viṟittirukkum. avar kiyilē pacukkaḷi aṭittu ōṭṭukiṉṟa kōli vittiruppar. kāvaṭiyiṉ iraṇṭu talikaḷi lum uṟikaḷit tūkkic celvatāl tōḷkaḷ kāyttu mayiruṭiyaṉavā yirukkum. avarkaḷ mayiriṭattē pāṟkaṟiyit taṭaviyiruppar; marak kompu kaḷiṉ ucciyiliruntum, koṭikaḷiliruntum paṟitta pūkkaḷi māliyākak kaṭṭi aṇintiruppar; ariyil oru uṭiyi uṭuttiruppar; pāṟcōṟṟi uṇavākak koḷvar; kaṉṟukaḷuṭiya pacuk kūṭṭattik kāṭṭil mēyum paṭi viṭṭuttām maraniḻalkaḷiliruntu, neruppuk koḷḷiyāl iṭṭa tuḷi kaḷiyuṭiya kuḻalkaḷil pālip paṇṇi iṉimiyākap pāṭuvar; kuḻali ūti veṟuttārkaḷāyiṉ kumiḻiṉ kompi vaḷittukkaṭṭiya araliyiṉ tumpākiya narampik kiyiṉāl teṟittu, vaṇṭiṉaṅkaḷ tammiṉattiṉ ōciyeṉṟu kātukoṭuttuk kēṭkumpaṭi kuṟiñcippaṇ pāṭuvar.
tāṉiyam viḷittum manti mēyttum vāḻum iṭiyar kuṭiyiruppu
ivvakip pullaṭarnta iṭattik kaṭantu muḷḷaṭarnta viṭarttar maraṅkaḷ cūḻnta māṭu taṅkumiṭattiyuṭiya ūrkaḷiṟ celvīrāyiṉ, vīṭukaḷiṉ muṟṟattē yāṉik kūṭṭam niṉṟāṟpōla varaku mutaliya tāṉi yaṅkaḷ niṟikkappaṭṭa kutirkaḷ tōṉṟum. talivāyilkaḷil yāṉiyiṉ kālkaḷpōṉṟa kutirkaḷ naṭappeṟṟirukkum. pukipiṭitta koṭṭilkaḷiṉ cuvarkaḷil vaṇṭic cakkaraṅkaḷum kalappikaḷum cārtti vikkappaṭ ṭirukkum. vīṭukaḷiṉ kūrikaḷ kariya varaku vikkōlāl vēyappaṭṭu mārikālattu mēkampōlat tōṉṟum. ivviyalpiṉavākiya kuṭiyiruppu kaḷi aṭivīrāyiṉ varakuṭaṉ avari vitiyi iṭṭuc camitta cōṟṟiṉi uṇṇumpaṭi aṅkuḷḷār taruvar.
maruta nilakkāṭci
ikkuṭiyiruppik kaṭantu celvīrkaḷāyiṉ vīṭu niṟinta uṇavup poruḷkaḷuṭiya uḻavariṉ vīṭukaḷik kāṇpīrkaḷ. uḻavar erutukaḷi nukattil pūṭṭip peṇyāṉiyiṉ vaḷinta vāy pōṉṟa kalappiyiṉ uṭumpiṉ mukam pōṉṟa periya koḻukkaḷ maṟiyumpaṭi amukki uḻutu vititta payirkaḷ tōṭṭaṅkaḷil viḷintu kāṭci aḷikkum. avaṟ ṟiṉiṭiyē taṅkiyirunta kāṭi aṟuppuk kālattukkaḷamariṉ āravārattuk kañcit taṉatu paṟakkalāṟṟātavum kaṭampiṉ pūppōṉṟu veṇṇiṟa muṭiyaṉavumākiya kuñcukaḷik kūṭṭik koṇṭu mulli nilattē ceṉṟu taṅkum.
amukki ūtukiṉṟa uliyil toḻil ceykiṟ kollaṉatu muṟinta kuṟaṭṭippōṉṟa kavarpaṭṭa kālkaḷuṭiya naṇṭukaḷ vayalkaḷilē, puṟṟukaḷil vāḻum. kōrip pulli vēruṭaṉ kompāl kutti eṭukkiṉṟa erutukaḷ naṇṭup puṟṟukaḷ aḻiyumpaṭi aṅku niṉṟu oṉṟōṭu oṉṟu caṇṭiyiṭum. ivviyalpiṉatākiya vayali uḻavar oppuravu ceytu nāṟṟu muṭikaḷi naṭuvar. vayalkaḷil vēli purivōriṉ irumput takaṭupōṉṟu tiriyāta melliya tōluṭiya piḷḷikaḷ caṇpakak kāyiṉ tāti mārpiṭattē pūciyiruppar. avarkaḷ kaḷi paṟippōr piṭuṅkip pōṭṭa neytaṟ pūvi aṇivati veṟuttārkaḷāyiṉ muḷḷiyiṉ pūvip paṟittup palliṉāl meṉṟu kiḻittu muṭinta kōrinārāl ataṉi māliyākak kaṭṭi īruṭiya kariyatali niṟiyumpaṭi cūṭuvar. avarkaḷ paḻañcōṟṟi veṟuttu varampilē kaṭṭappaṭṭuḷḷa ciṟiya kuṭiliṉ muṟṟattil avaliṭippar. avvōcikku añcik kiḷikaḷ paṟakkum. nellaṟuppōr vaḷinta katiruṭiya muṟṟiya nelliṉ tāḷ kaḷi aṟuppar; aṟuttuk kaḷaṅkaḷil uṟiyum teyvaṅkaḷ pali koḷ ḷumpaṭi pāmpuṟiyum marutamara niḻalil pōraṭukkuvar. pōrkaḷiṉ aṭip pakkaṅkaḷil tuṇaṅkiyāṭum pūtaṅkaḷiṉ veḷḷāṭikaḷip pōl cilanti nūlkaḷ cūḻntu kiṭakkum. pōrkaḷiṉ aṭiyippiṭittu virittuk kaṭākkaḷāl mitittu vikkōliyum kūḷattiyum maṇiyiṉiṉṟum pirittu īram pularumpaṭi mēl kāṟṟil kiyiṉāl tūvit tūṟṟiṉa poli mēruvip pōla uyarntu tōṉṟum. ivvāṟu ciṟappu mikka maruta nilañ cūḻnta kuṭiyiruppukaḷilē vīṭukaḷiṉ pakkattu iṟukkap peṟṟa muḷikaḷil āṉkaṉṟukaḷik kaṭṭum kayiṟukaḷ kiṭakkum; taliyit tiṟantu tāṉiyaṅkaḷ uḷḷē koṭṭappeṟṟa kutirkaḷ tōṉṟum. taccacciṟār ceyta ciṟu tērkaḷi uruṭṭic ceṉṟa kaḷippāl piṉḷikaḷ cevilit tāyaruṭiya pāli niṟiyavuṇṭu paṭukkiyilē kaṇvaḷarvar. ivvāṟu vaṟumi teriyāta kuṭiyiruppiṉiyuṭiya ūrilē celvīrāyiṉ aṅkuḷḷār neṟcōṟṟiṉik kōḻippēṭṭiṉ poriyalōṭu taruvar.
karumpu ālikaḷ
vayalkaḷilē pukicūḻnta koṭṭilkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. aṅkuk karumpu ālikaḷ āṭukiyiṉālē maliyiṭattil ciṅkam pāytalāl yāṉi payantu cattamiṭṭāṟ pōṉṟu āravāram uṇṭākum. ivvāṟu āṭṭippiḻiyappaṭṭa karuppañ cāṟṟi aṅkuḷḷār kaṭṭiyākak kāyccu var. akkoṭṭilkaḷiṟ celvīrāyiṉ karuppañcāṟṟiyum kaṟkaṇṭiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir.
mīṉ piṭippōr kuṭiyiruppu
ikkoṭṭilkaḷik kaṭantu appāṟ ceṉṟāl ērikaḷilum maṭukkaḷilum mīṉ piṭippāriṉ kuṭiyiruppik kāṇpīrkaḷ. avarkaḷiṉ vīṭu kāñci vañci mutaliya maraṅkaḷiṉ kompukaḷik kimaraṅkaḷukku naṭuvē tūṇāka nāṭṭi mūṅkiṟṟaṭikaḷi variccāka nirittu vittut, tāḻi nārāṟ kaṭṭit taruppip pullāl vēynta iṟappukaḷi uṭiyaṉa. tali vāyilkaḷ puṉṉik kompiṉāl kaṭṭappaṭṭirukkum. avaṟṟiṉ kūri kaḷil paṭaraviṭappaṭṭa curik koṭikaḷil kāykaḷ toṅkum. muṟṟattē mīṉkaḷi vāri eṭukkum valikaḷ kiṭakkum. iḷiyavarkaḷum mutiyavar kaḷum talivāyilil taṅki iruppārkaḷ; piṉpu iṟālum kayal mīṉum piṟaḻukiṉṟa āḻnta kuḷaṅkaḷil ulāvi mīṉkaḷip piṭippārkaḷ. avarkaḷ kuṟṟāta koḻiyalariciyik kaḻiyākat tuḻāvi aṭuvar; aṭṭa kūḻi akaṉṟa vāyuṭiya taṭṭuppiḻāvilē viṭṭu āṟṟuvar; pāmpu irukkum puṟṟiṟ kiṭakkum puṟṟām paḻañcōṟṟi otta puṟattiṉiyuṭiya nel muḷiyi iṭittuc cērumpaṭi atilē kalappar; piṉpu viralālē kalakki arippar. avar kuṭiyiruppil taṅkuvīrāyiṉ ivvāṟu arittuc cāṭiyil viṭṭu muṟṟiya kāramuḷḷa kaḷḷi mīṉ cūṭṭōṭu peṟukuvir.
ikkuṭiyiruppik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl aṅku nīrnilikaḷ kāṇap paṭum. tūṇṭilil kōkkum iriyi iṭṭu vikkum tōṟpiyi uṭiyavaṉum mīṉit tappāmaṟ piṭikkum tiṟamiyuṭiyavaṉumākiya pāṇaṉ mūṅkiṟṟaṭiyiṟ kaṭṭiya kayiṟṟiṭattuḷḷa tūṇṭilil iriyik kuṟṟi nīruḷviṭuvāṉ. avviriyik kavarntu akappaṭāmaṟ pōṉa vāḷimīṉ nīr niliyiṉ pakkattē niṉṟa pirampiṉ niḻalikkaṇṭu añcum. annīr niliyil pūtta kaṭavuḷ cūṭutaṟ kuriya tāmarip pūvic cūṭātu, poykikaḷil vāṉaviṟ pōṉṟu pūttuḷḷa kuvaḷiyiyum nīlattiyum piṟa pūkkaḷiyum avaṟṟip paṟippār uṅkaṭkuttara nīvir avaṟṟip peṟṟuc cūṭik koṇṭu appāṟ celvīrāka.
pārppār illam
appāṟ ceṉṟāl pārppār illaṅkaḷik kāṇpīrkaḷ. pacukkaḷ pacum puṟṟarikaḷittēṭi mēyac cella avaṟṟiṉ ceḻiya kaṉṟukaḷ pantaṟ kālkaḷil kaṭṭi niṟkum. kōḻi, nāy mutaliyavi av villaṅkaḷuḷ cellamāṭṭā. cāṇiyāl meḻukappaṭṭirukkum vīṭukaḷuḷ vaḻipaṭu teyvaṅkaḷiṉ uruvaṅkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. kiḷikaḷ vētam paṭikkum. arun tatiyippōṉṟa ciṟantakaṟpum nalloḻukkamum vaḷiyaṇinta ki kaḷumuṭiya pārppaṉap peṇkaḷ nalla cuviyuḷḷa uṇavukaḷi ākku vārkaḷ. avvīṭukaḷi aṭintāl irācāṉṉam eṉṉum cōṟṟiṉiyum, mātuḷaṅkāykaḷip pillikaḷāka arintu miḷakuttūḷ tūvik karuvēppili kūṭṭi neyyil vetuppiya poriyalkaḷiyum, māṅkāy ūṟukāy mutaliyavaṟṟiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir.
nīrāṭun tuṟi
nīrāṭun tuṟikaḷilē makaḷir viḷiyāṭip pōkaviṭṭup pōṉa kuḻiyiṉi nīla niṟamuṭiya ciccilip paṟavi eṭuttukkoṇṭu paṟavikaḷ niṟintirukkum paṉiyiṟ cellātu, antaṇar yākacāliyil naṭṭa yūpattiṉ mēlirukkum. atu cōṉakar pāymarattiṉ mēlēṟṟiya aṉṉa viḷakkip pōlavum viṭi veḷḷiyip pōlavum oḷiviṭṭu viḷaṅkum.
tuṟimukak kāṭci
kaṭaṟkariyilē mēṟkut ticiyiluḷḷa kutirikaḷiyum ticiyiluḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum koṇṭu vanta marakkalaṅkaḷ cūḻntu niṟkum. maṇal mikkuṭiya vītikaḷil toḻilāḷar kāvalpuriyum paṇṭa cālikaḷum paratavar vāḻum vāṉi muṭṭum māḷikikaḷum kāṇap peṟum. vīṭukaḷil viḷiporuḷkaḷ niṟintu kiṭakkum. vayalkaḷil uḻukiṉṟa erutukaḷip pacukkaḷ neruṅkātapaṭi āṭṭukkaṭākkaḷum nāykaḷum cuḻaṉṟu tiriyum.
peṇkaḷ pantāṭutal
uyarnta māṭaṅkaḷil uṟiyum pēraṇikalaṉkaḷi aṇinta makaḷir paciya maṇikōtta vaṭaṅkaḷ uṭiya ariyil kiṭakkiṉṟa melliya tukil aciya maliyilē āravārikkiṉṟa mayilkaḷ pōla ulāvuvar; kāliṭattuḷḷa poṟ cilampuka ḷolikka nūliṉāl varintu ceyyappaṭṭa pantiṉi aṭittu viḷiyāṭuvar; metteṉak kiyiṟ ṟaritta vaḷikaḷ aciyumpaṭi poṟkaḻaṅku koṇṭu munti otta vārnta maṇalil viḷiyāṭuvar.
kaṭkaṭi
kaḷḷuṇpār palarum pukukiṉṟa kaḷ viṟkum kaṭi vāyilil paciya koṭikaḷ āṭikkoṇṭirukkum. muṟṟattē teyvattukkut tūviṉa pū vāṭalkaḷ kāṇappaṭum. avviṭattē kaḷḷic camikkiṉṟa makaḷir vīṭṭil kaḻuviyanīr vaṭitaliṉ nilam cēṟāṭiyirukkum. ac cēṟṟil puraḷu kiṉṟa peṇ paṉṟiyōṭu cērkkik karuttāl cellāmal kuḻiyilviṭṭu, iṭittamāvi uṇavākak koṭuttu āṇpaṉṟi vaḷarkkappaṭum. nīvir neytal nilattiluḷḷa paṭṭiṉattiṟ ṟaṅku vīrāyiṉ appaṉṟiyiṉ taciyōṭu kaḷḷiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir.
kalaṅkari viḷakkam
tuṟimukattil vāṉi muṭṭumpaṭi uyarntatum kaṟṟi mutaliya vaṟṟāl vēyappaṭātatumākiya kalaṅkari viḷakkam tōṉṟum. atu kaṭaliṟ cellum marakkalaṅkaḷ tām cērum iṭatti aṟintu cellumpaṭi amik kappaṭṭatu. avviṭattik kaṭantu ceṉṟāl teṉṉaṅkīṟṟāl vēynta kūriyiyum muṟṟattē mañcaṭceṭikaḷiyumuṭiya vīṭukaḷik kāṇ pīrkaḷ. avvīṭukaḷil taṅkuvīrāyiṉ iḷanīriyum, vāḻip paḻattiyum, paṉiyiṉ nuṅkiṉiyum, vēṟu paṇṭaṅkiyum muṟṟiṉa vaḷḷimutaliya kiḻaṅkukaḷiyum uṇṇumpaṭi peṟukuvir. iccelvam poruntiya paṭṭiṉattē vaḻiccelvōr paci tīrumpaṭi cōṟṟi ākkukiṉṟa pāṉi acintu viḻumpaṭi kamukiṉ pakkattē niṟkum teṅkiṉkāy viḻum.
tiru eḵkā
ippaṭṭiṉatti viṭṭuc ceṉṟāl pala maraṅkaḷ aṭarnta iṭaṅkaḷil amikkappaṭṭa viṇṇittoṭum māṭaṅkaḷ viḷaṅkum. ūrkaḷil peṇkaḷ vaḷḷikkūttu āṭuvar. ivvakip puṟanāṭukaḷik kaṭantapiṉ kāntaḷ vaḷarkiṉṟa maliyiṭattu yāṉi kiṭantāṟ pōlap pāmpaṇi yilē tirumāl paḷḷi koḷkiṉṟa tiru eḵkāvi aṭivīrkaḷ.
aṅku veyyil nuḻiyātavum ili nerukkattāl kuyilkaḷ nuḻikiṉṟavumākiya iḷamarac cōlikaḷ uṇṭu. accōlikaḷuḷ uḷḷa kāñci marattilē paṭarntirukkutam kurukkattik koṭiyiṉ varikaḷi yuṭiya pūkkaḷ, vārnta maṇaliṭattē kuḻikaḷil niṟkum nīril viḻuntu appavāṇikar cuṭṭa appam pālilē kiṭantāṟ pōl tōṉṟum. muṉpu nīrniṉṟa appoḻilkaḷiṭattē, iḷampiṟiyik karumpāmpu paṟṟiṉāṟ pōla makaravāyeṉṉum talikkōlamum, vaṇṭukaḷ cūḻum neṟṟiyum, kaḷḷuṇṇalāl kuḷircci poruntiya kaṇkaḷumuṭiya makaḷir pakaṟ poḻutil viḷiyāṭuvar. avarkaḷōṭu nīvirum viḷiyāṭuvīrkaḷāka. piṉpu, nīṅkaḷ periya nīrāṭum tuṟiyil iḷavēṉil iṉpam nukarpavar kaḷōṭu iruntu iḷippāṟuṅkaḷ; cūl koṇṭa kamukiṉ vayiṟṟip pōṉṟa paccik kuppikaḷi (toṉṉikaḷi) uṇṭu kaḻittu nāṭōṟum periya makiḻccikkuriya avviṭattē iruṅkaḷ; nummuṭiya kariya taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya iṉiya yāḻi vācittut tiru veḵkāvilirukkum kaṭavuḷi vāḻtti avviṭattiṉiṉṟum celluṅkaḷ.
kāñci mūtūr
appāṟ ceṉṟāl kāñci mūtūri aṭivīrkaḷ. aṅkuc cōli kaḷil parikkōlik kiyilēyuṭiya yāṉippākar cōrntirukkum pōtu, cūluṭiya manti yāṉikku neyyuṭaṉ kalantu vikkappaṭṭa ariciyit tiruṭikkoṇṭu ceṉṟu uṇṇum. tēr ōṭutalāl terukkaḷ kuḻintirukkum. pukaḻuṭiya mūtūriṭattu vāḻum makkaḷ kaṭikaḷil viṟṟa lum vāṅkalum ceyvar. ulakattē koṭuppārik koṭātu taṭutta1 am mūtūr paricilarkku aṭiyāta vāyiluṭiyatu; ceṅkaṟkaḷāl eṭukkap paṭṭa matilkaḷ kāvaṟkāṭu cūḻnta pakkaṅkaḷi uṭiyaṉa. annakar, pulāl nāṟṟa muṭiya kaṭal cūḻanta ivvulakil, puṟākkaḷirukkiṉṟavum pūvāmaṟ kāyk kiṉṟavum maraṅkaḷuṭ ciṟanta palāvip pōṉṟu ciṟappuṭiyatu; palacamayatta varkaḷ toḻumpaṭi eṭukkappaṭum viḻākkaḷāl ciṟantatu.
aivar nūṟṟu vari veṉṟatu
aivar tāmarimukiyuṭiya neṭiya tēri uṭiyar. tarumaṉ mutalāṉōr, piṟiyi mēlē cūṭi antik kālattē ulāvukiṉṟa mukilip pōṉṟavum, veḷḷiya kompuṭiyavumākiya yāṉikaḷiṉ piṇatti iratta āṟu iḻuttuc cellumpaṭi porutu turiyōtaṉaṉ mutaliya nūṟṟuvarum maṭiyumpaṭi veṉṟāṉ. attarumaṉ mutalāṉōr pōla iḷantiriyaṉ maṭṭuk kaṭaṅkāp paṭiyuṭaṉ; avaṉ vanta pakivari veṉṟu paricili virumpi aṭikkalamākac ceṉṟārkkup pātukāvalākak kāñci nakaril vīṟṟirukkiṉṟāṉ.
aracaṉ kāñci mūtūril iravalarppurantu vīṟṟirukkum iyalpu
taṉṉōṭu māṟupaṭṭa aracaṉiṉ maṉṟukaḷil makkaḷiṉṟāmpaṭi aḻittalum, taṉṉi virumpivantōr nāṭu tirumaṭantipolivu peṟṟirukkumpaṭi aḷittalum avaṉukku eḷitu. maliyiṭattiṉiṉṟum iḻikiṉṟa aruvi am maliyiluḷḷa paṇṭaṅkaḷi vārikkoṇṭu vantu kaṭalukkuk koṭuttāṟ pōlat tiṟikaḷuṭaṉ vanta palanāṭṭu vēntar avaṉuṭiya talivāyilil kāttuniṟpar. aṉṉōr, kaṅkiyik kaṭakka virumpiṉōr tōṇikkuk kāttiruntu tūṅkiṉatu pōlayāṉi mutaliya celvaṅkaḷōṭu neruṅki niṉṟu kālam pārttiruppar.
iḷantiriyaṉiṉ nalliyalpukaḷ 
māṭattirukkum cēvaṟpuṟā, yāṉikku vaḷinta pūṇ ceykiṉṟa kollaṉatu kūṭattil eḻunta ōcikku veruvit tuyil nīṅkum. kaṭavuḷ iruppataṟkum ilakkumi uṟivataṟkum iṭamāyuḷḷa ittoṉṉakaril vēntaṉ iruppāṉ. avaṉ kaṭaliṉ naṭuvē ñāyiṟu tōṉṟi oḷiceytāṟ pōla mayakka millāta aṟivuṭiyaṉ. avaṉ vaṟiyarāy naliveyti ṉōrkkum vaṟumiyuṟ ṟirantōrkkum virumpiyavaṟṟik koṭuppāṉ; pulavarukku āparaṇaṅkaḷi aḷippāṉ; paricilarukku aḷittuk koṭumi illāta amiccarōṭu vīṟṟiruppāṉ. nī avaṉip pārttu, “yāṉiyik kaṇṭa ciṅkakkuṭṭi ataṉ mattakattik koḷḷa virumpi ṉāṟ pōlap pakivaratu matilkaḷi aḻittu aracaruṭiya muṭikkalam mutaliyavaṟṟi vāṅkikkoṇṭu vīramuṭi puṉiyum veṟṟiyiṉiyum, pakip pulattukkoṇṭa koḷḷiyākiya uṇaviṉiyum, māṟupaṭṭa araca rōṭu cantuceyyum viruppamiṉmiyiyum uṭiyavaṉē! toṇṭik koṭiyic cūṭiya marapil utittavaṉē! vīrarkku vīrattik koṭukkiṉṟa vaṉē! koṭiyārkkuk koṭiyavaṉē! celvamuṭiyōrkkuk koṭuttali virumpukiṉṟavaṉē! pōrttoḻilil pukaḻpeṟṟavaṉē! kaṭaliṭattē ceṉṟu cūraṉik koṉṟa murukaṉip peṟṟa perumiyuṭiya vayiṟṟi yum pēykaḷāṭum tuṇaṅkik kūttiyumuṭiya iṟivikkup pēymakaḷ cila noṭi coṉṉāṟpōlak kuṟiyāta koṭiyuṭiya niṉpukaḻiṟ cila vaṟṟik kūṟip pukaḻntu vantēṉ; neṭitu vāḻka!” eṉṟu colli yāḻi vācikkum muṟimiyil vācippāyāka. mayilkaḷ āravārikkiṉṟa iḷamarak kāṭṭil mantikaḷ pāyvatāl cintikkiṭakkum malarkaḷiyuṭiya kāṭum, teyvaṅkaḷ uṟikiṉṟavum, kiṉṉarap paṟavi pāṭukiṉṟavum, muṟṟattē māṉum puliyum tuyilkoḷḷa yāṉi muṟittukkoṇṭu vanta viṟakāl muṉivar ōmañceykiṉṟavumākiya cāralkaḷum pala aruvikaḷumuṭiya malikaḷukkut talivaṉākiya aracaṉ celvamum yākkiyum nili yillāta ivvulakil pukaḻip peṟutal naṉṟu eṉa uṇarntu uṉṉi virumpi aḻippāṉ;
iḷantiriyaṉ iravalar pukaḻakkēṭṭup paricil vaḻaṅkum taṉmi
aḻittu uṉatu ariyiṟ kiṭakkum koṭṭip pāciyiṉ vērip pōṉṟa kiḻinta uṭiyip pōkkip pālāviyi ottu viḷaṅkukiṉṟa nūlāl ceyta paṭṭāṭikaḷi nīyum uṉatu cuṟṟattiṉarum uṭukkumpaṭi taruvāṉ. vaḷinta arivāḷip piṭittalāl kikāyppēṟiṉa maṭiyaṉ ākkiya iṟicciyiyum cōṟṟiyum iṉiya cuviyuṭiya piṟa uṇavu kaḷiyum veḷḷik kalaṅkaḷiliṭṭu uṉatu piḷḷikaḷiṉiṭiyē vittu āciyuṭaṉ uṇṇumpaṭi ceyvāṉ. kār kālattu miṉṉal tōṉṟiṉāṟ pōṉṟu viṟali mayiriṭattē cūṭumpaṭi poṉmāliyiyum poṟṟāmarip pūviyum aḷippāṉ; caṅkiṉ niṟampōṉṟa veḷḷiya kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya poṟṟēriṉit taruvāṉ; atōṭu amiyātu paki aracariṭamiruntu kavarnta kutirikaḷiyum cēṇattiyum koṭuppāṉ; nāvalan taṇ poḻil kuṟṟamiṉṟi viḷaṅkumpaṭi ivi oḻinta paricilkaḷiyum vaḻaṅku vāṉ. iravalaṉē kālan tāḻttātu kaṭitil avaṉpāl ēkuka.


5. 1mullippāṭṭu
naṟcoṟkēṭṭal
mullikkuriya kārkālam nērntatu. pōrvayiṟ pirinta talivariṉ pirivip poṟukkamāṭṭāta irācamātēvi tuyaruṟṟut tuyil koḷḷap peṟāḷāyiṉāḷ. avaḷ kaṇkaḷ muttuppōṟ kaṇṇīri utirttaṉa. avaḷ taṉatu nekiḻnta kivaḷikaḷit tiruttiṉāḷ; ampu titta mayilpōla naṭunaṭuṅki aṇikalaṉkaḷ nekiḻappeṟṟāḷ. ataṉik kaṇṭu varuntiya mutupeṇṭu pala iṉiyamoḻikaḷik kūṟiṉāḷ; talivaṉatu māṉam pukaḻ mutaliyavaṟṟiyum, araciyal naṭappataṟku avaṉ piriya vēṇṭiya iṉṟiyamiyāmiyiyum eṭuttukkāṭṭiṉāḷ. talivitaṉatu āṟṟāmi mikutiyāl mutupeṇṭu kūṟiyavaṟṟit teḷiyum tiṟami illāḷāyiṉāḷ. aññāṉṟu ammutupeṇṭu irācamātēviyi nōkki, “paṭittalivar ūrppuṟattuḷḷa pākkattē naṟcoṟkēṭpārip pōkkiṉār. avarkaḷ tām nāḻiyiṟkoṇṭu ceṉṟa nelliyum mulli malariyum tūvit teyvatti vaṇaṅki naṟcoṟ kēṭka niṉṟārkaḷ. kuḷirāl naṭuṅkum iṭicci tōḷiṟ kaṭṭiṉa kiyaḷāyp pōntu, tāmpiṟ piṇikkappaṭṭu niṟpaṉavum muliyuṇṇāmiyāl cuḻaṉṟu varuntukiṉṟavu mākiya kaṉṟukaḷi nōkki, ‘uṅkaḷ tāymār nirampamēyntu ippoḻutē varuvar’ eṉṟāḷ. annaṟcolliṉ palaṉāka niṉtalivar pakavariveṉṟu avariṭattiṟ peṟṟa tiṟip poruḷkaḷuṭaṉ makiḻcci peruka ippoḻutē varuvar eṉak kūṟiṉāḷ. ataṉic cevik koṇṭa talivi talivar ivvāṟu pirintālaṉṟi araciyal nikaḻāteṉa maṉatiṟ koḷvāḷāyiṉaḷ.
irācamātēvi aracaṉiṉ varavi etirnōkkik kiṭattal
cakkarattiyum valampuriccaṅkiyum tāṅkiya periya kikaḷiyum, ilakkumiyit tāṅkum mārpiṉiyumuṭiya tirumāl māvaliyiṭam kuṟaḷ vaṭiviṟ ceṉṟu mūvaṭi maṇṇirantār. ataṉi aḷikkavēṇṭi māvali aṅkiyil nīr peyta ñāṉṟu tirumāl neṭiya vaṭivu koṇṭār. kaṭa liṭattu nīrikkuṭitta mukilkaḷ anneṭiya mālippōla uyarntu mali yil taṅki mālik kālattil maḻiyip peytaṉa. araṇmaṉiyiṭattup paḷḷiyaṟiyil poṟpāvi ēnti niṉṟa takaḷiyilē viḷakku niṉṟerin tatu. ēḻunili māṭattiṉ mūṭṭuvāykaḷi ṉiṉṟum corikiṉṟa nīrttāri yiṉ ōcikaḷ āravārittaṉa. ivvāṟu nikaḻtalum talivaṉ varuvēṉ eṉṟu kūṟic ceṉṟa paruvakālam vantatātaliṉ avaṉ tavaṟātu varuvāṉ eṉṉum eṇṇattōṭu irācamātēvi paṭukkiyil kiṭappāḷāyiṉaḷ. iḵtu ivvāṟākap paki aracaṉmītu paṭi eṭuttuc ceṉṟa talivaṉatu nili mēl varumāṟuḷḷatu.
pācaṟi vakuttal
pakimēṟ ceṉṟa aracaṉatu paṭivīrar kāṭṭāṟu cūḻnta kāṭṭiṭattē piṭavam mutaliya putarkaḷi veṭṭiṉārkaḷ; pakip pulattē kāval kāttu niṉṟa veṭṭuvar araṇkaḷi aḻittārkaḷ; araṇāka nāṟpuṟamum muḷ vēli iṭṭārkaḷ; ataṉ naṭuvē paranta pāṭi vīṭṭi amittārkaḷ. aracaṉ appācaṟiyiṉiṭattē, orukiyip paṭukkiyiṉ mēl vittum oru kiyi muṭiyuṭaṉ cērttiyum iruntu, yāṉiyi eṟintu paṭṭa vīrari niṉintum ampu titta varuttattāl cevi cāyttup pulluṇṇāmal niṟkum kutirikaḷi niṉintum, ippaṭi nonta aḷavukku nāḷi ev vāṟu porutum eṉṟu niṉintum varuntuvāṉ. appācaṟiyil taṅkum pala moḻikaḷip pēcum paṭi vīrarukku naṭuvil ōriṭatti aracaṉukkuk kōyilāka ellārum amittārkaḷ; kālkaḷik kūṭamāka naṭṭārkaḷ; kayiṟṟi vaḷittuk kaṭṭiṉārkaḷ; valiya viṟkaḷi ūṉṟiṉārkaḷ. avaṟ ṟiṉ mītu tuṇikaḷit tūkkiṉārkaḷ. attōṟṟam mukkōl antaṇaṉ mukkōlil taṉatu kāvi uṭiyi iṭṭuvitta taṉmiyi ottiruntatu. piṉpu pūttoḻilit taliyiluṭiya eṟikōlkaḷi ūṉṟiṉārkaḷ; paricikaḷi niriyāka vittārkaḷ; pala niṟamūṭṭiya tuṇikaḷik kuttuk kālkaḷil tittu avaṟṟi vaḷittu vittārkaḷ. ivvāṟu amikkap paṭṭa aracaṉatu kūṭārattiṉ vāyilil caṭṭiyiṭṭa valiya yavaṉar puliyic caṅkaliyiṟ kaṭṭivitta vaṭivaṉi eḻutiṉar.
aracaṉatu il
aṅkuk kālattiṉ aḷavi aḷantu colluvōraracaṉi vaṇaṅki vāḻtti nāḻiki vaṭṭiliṟ ceṉṟa nāḻiki ittaṉi eṉak kūṟiṉar. appōtu tūveṇtukiluṭuttapeṇkaḷ 1viḷakkik koḷuttik kāṭṭiṉārkaḷ. aracaṉ tiriccīli toṅka viṭappaṭṭa uḷḷaṟiyil tuyilumpaṭi ceṉṟāṉ.
pācaṟik kāṭci
teruviṉ nāṟcanti taḻikaḷāl vēyappaṭṭiruntatu. aṅkuk kāvalāka niṟuttappaṭṭa yāṉi karumpiyum neṟkatirōṭu kalantu kaṭṭiya iliyiyum atimaturat taḻiyiyum uṇṇātu avaṟṟāl taṉatu neṟṟiyittuṭittum kompiṉmītu iṭṭa taṉatu neṟṟiyiṭattē avaṟṟikkoṇṭum niṉṟatu; yāṉip pākar yāṉip pēccākiya vaṭa moḻiyic collik kavar uṭiya parikkōlāl kavaḷatti uṇṇumpaṭi kuttiṉārkaḷ. ciṟṟāṭkaḷ ney koppaḷikkiṉṟa tirikkuḻāyil nīṇṭa tiriyik koṇṭu oḻuṅkāyamitta viḷakkukaḷik koḷuttiṉārkaḷ. viḷakkukaḷ aviyuntōṟum tam kiyiṭattuḷḷa pantattik koḷut tiṉārkaḷ.
aracaṉ veṟṟi peṟṟu mīḷumiṭattu vaḻiyiṟ kāṇumkāṭci
talippākiyum caṭṭiyum tarittu meykkāppāḷar aracaṉik kāvalākac cūḻntu tirintaṉar. caṭṭiyiṭṭa ūmarākiya milēccar paḷḷi koḷḷumiṭattic cūḻntu tirintaṉar. ivvāṟu maṇiyōci aṭaṅkiya naṭuccāmattum maṟṟiyanāḷ ceyyavirukkum pōri niṉintu aracaṉ tuyil koḷḷāṉāyiṉāṉ. aṭuttanāḷ avaṉ pakivarikkollak karuti vitta vāḷip piṭittu veṟṟiyip peṟṟāṉ; paki aracar virumpum nilaṅkaḷi veṉṟa veṟṟiyiṉ aṭiyāḷamāka veṟṟikkoṭiyi uyarttiṉāṉ. avaṉ mīṇṭu varum kāṭṭu vaḻiyilē kāyā kariya pūkkaḷip pūttatu. koṉṟi poṉṉiṟa malarkaḷi malarntatu; veṇkāntaḷ uḷḷaṅkipōl virintatu. muṟukiya kompuṭiya āṇmāṉ peṇmāṉōṭu tuḷḷi viḷiyāṭiṟṟu; tiraṇṭa tōṉṟi irattampōṟ pūttatu. veṟṟik kaṟikuṟiyākiya kompum caṅkum muḻaṅkiṉa. niritta cēṉikkum kutirip paṭikkum muṉ vanta aracaṉatu tēriṟ pūṭṭiya kutirikaḷ irācamātēviyiṉ cevi nirampumpaṭi āravārittaṉa.1


6. 1maturik kāñci
aracaṉ nallāṭciyil nikaḻvaṉa
ulakam periya tirikaḷ vīcukiṉṟa kaṭali elliyāka vuṭiyatu. atu tēṉkūṭu tūṅkum ucciyaṉavākiya malikaḷiyuṭiyatu. ticikaḷ kuḷiravum, maraṅkaḷum palluyirum taḻikkavum vāṉam poyyātu peytatu. ñāyiṟum tiṅkaḷum kaḷaṅkamiṉṟi viḷaṅkiṉa. viṇmīṉkaḷ cellum muṟimiyiṟ ceṉṟaṉa. makkaḷ paciyum piṇiyumiṉṟi vāḻntaṉar. aracaṉ ulakattik kāttalāl tikku yāṉikaḷ varuttan tīrntaṉa. ivvaki ulakip poymoḻiyāta amiccaruṭaṉ pala ūḻikaḷilum tamatu pukaḻ nilikkumpaṭi āṇṭa uyarnta aracar marapil utittavaṉē! accantarum pōrkkaḷattē kuṟittalip piṇaṅkaḷ eḻuntu tāḷattukku āṭumpaṭi, kompukaḷuṭiya yāṉiyiṉ piṇantiṉṟa pēymakaḷ āravāram poruntiya tuṇaṅkik kūttāṭuvāḷ.
irāvaṇaṉi akattiyar teṟkē iyaṅkātu tavirttatu
cōṟaṭukiṉṟa pēy, vīrariṉ taliyākiya aṭuppil aracaruṭiya irattamākiya uli ciṉamākiya tīyiṉālē kotittup poṅkukiyāl vīra vaḷiyaṇinta tōḷuṭiya kikaḷi akappiyākak koṇṭu tuḻāvic camitta ūṉcōṟṟip piṟaṅkiṭāta vīrarkku vēḷvi ceyyum. iv vakittākiya pōri vellum paṭiyuṭiya irāvaṇaṉ tamiḻ nāṭṭi āḷātapaṭi pōkkiṉa potiyiṉ maliyil irukkum akattiyaṉukkup piṉ cāṉṟōṉāyirukkum ciṟappuṭiyavaṉē!
vaṭimpalalampa niṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ
mukapaṭāttiyum neṟṟippaṭṭattiyum kōpattiyum, matattiyu muṭiya malipōluyarnta pōrkkaḷiṟu kōpittu vīrarik koṉṟu tirintatu; pakivarmēṟ celkiṉṟa kutirikaḷiṉ vēkattāl eḻukiṉṟa puḻuti ñāyiṟṟi maṟittatu; kutirikaḷ pūṭṭappaṭṭa tērkaḷ kāṟṟip pōl virintuceṉṟaṉa; vāḷvīrar vāḷiṉāl virintu veṭṭiṉar. iv vāṟu naṭakkiṉṟa pōril cēra cōḻarkaḷākiya iruperuvēntarōṭu kuṟunila maṉṉarum iḷikkumpaṭi porutu veṉṟatō ṭamiyātu kuṟiñcinila maṉṉariyum veṉṟu pakivar nilaṅkaḷik kikkoṇṭu poṉ ṉāratti mārpil aṇinta vaṭimpalampaniṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ marapil vanta vēntē! pakivar kiṭṭutaṟkariya kāvaṟkāṭṭiyum uyarnta kōpura vāyil kaḷiyum nīṇṭa matiliyum niritta1 cūṭṭiṉiyumuṭiya ampukaḷ umiḻum araṇiyum matiyātu ceṉṟu pakivar nilaṅkaḷik koṇṭavaṉē!
kaṭaliṭattē vēkamāka aṭikkiṉṟa kāṟṟāl vaḷikiṉṟa tiriyik kuṟukkē piḷantōṭumpaṭi pāy virittuc ceṉṟu celva mikutaṟkuk kāraṇa mākiya carakkukaḷi iṟakkumpaṭi kariyic cērntu karumēkañ cūḻnta malikaḷ pōla marakkalaṅkaḷ cūḻntu niṟkum kaṭaliyum akaḻiyiyu muṭiya cāliyūrukkut talivaṉē!
vayaliṭattuḷḷa payirkaḷ taḻikkumpaṭi nīrniṟinta kuḷaṅkaḷil tulākkōliṟ kaṭṭi iṟikkum paṉṟip pattal eṉṉum iṟi kūṭiyiṉ ōci orupāl eḻum; orupāl erutukaḷiṟ kaṭṭappaṭṭa maṇi ōci olik kum; orupāl payirkaḷiṭattē viḻum kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭuvōr ōci eḻum.
kuṭṭa nāṭṭuc ciṟappu
ivaṟṟōṭu nīla malaruṭiya kaḻimuḷḷikaḷ niṟkum maṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷuṭiya kaṭaṟkariyil niṉṟu paratavar makaḷir āṭum kuravi āravāramum cērntu olikkum. ūrkaḷiṉ vevvēṟiṭaṅkaḷil viḻāk koṇṭāṭum ōcikaḷum olikkum. ivvāṟu celvamikunta ūrkaḷi liruntu kiṇip porunarukkuk kaṉṟuṭaṉ piṭikaḷiyum kompuḷḷa kaḷiṟu kaḷiyum koṭuttup poṟṟāmarip pūvic cūṭṭi nalla aṇikalaṉ kaḷiyum koṭukkum kuṭṭa nāṭṭāri veṉṟa vēntē!
neṭuñceḻiyaṉ kuṟunila maṉṉariyum cēra cōḻariyum veṉṟatu
ivvulakil toḻilkaḷuḷ mēlākac collappaṭum uḻavu vāṇikam eṉṉum iraṇṭāl peṟappaṭṭa periya celvattāl pukaḻniṟinta kuṭikaḷ uṉatu nāṭṭil vāḻvar. mutuveḷḷili eṉṉum kuṟunila maṉṉaṉatu nāṭu celvam mikuntatu. vēṉilāl mēkam vaṟappiṉum, tōṉṟavēṇṭiya kālattē tōṉṟum veḷḷi teṟkē eḻiṉum āṟṟu veḷḷam vantu perukutalāl annāṭṭil viḷivumikum; muṟṟi viḷinta neṟkatirkaḷ kāṟṟilacintu olikkum; avaṟṟi aṟuppōr āravārippar. avviṭattē taṅkip paṟavikaḷ ōci ceyyum, cuṟākkaḷ tirikiṉṟa kaṭalil veṇmaṇal paranta kariyil kuṭamuḻāp pōṉṟa kāykaḷit tāḻikaḷ tāṅki niṟkum. tāḻikaḷi vēliyākavuṭiya kuḷirnta iḷamarakkāvil vantu vīcukiṉṟa nīrt tuvaliyiṉ ōciyum, paṭakil mīṉ piṭikkac ceṉṟōr vantu iṟaṅkum āravāramum, kariyaperiya kaḻiyiṭattuḷḷa uppup pāttikaḷil veḷḷiya uppi viṟkum aḷavar oliyum, ciṟukuṭiyāyp periya toḻilkaḷic ceyvōriṉ āravāramum kalantolikkum. ivviyalpuṭiya nāṭukaḷiyuṭiya kuṟunila maṉṉarum, nāṉilattōrum ēval kēṭkumpaṭi virintu ceṉṟāy; ceṉṟu tīk koḷuvit taliyālaṅ kāṉam eṉṉum ūriṟ ṟaṅkip perunilamaṉṉa riruvarum kuṟunila maṉṉar aivarum paṭumpaṭi pōrveṉṟu avar muracik koṇṭu kaḷa vēḷvi vēṭṭāy. ivvāṟu purintavaliya puyaṅkaḷi yuṭiyavaṉē!
naṉmakkaḷiṭiyē talimiyum, caṅkum muttumuṭiya caṅku kuḷippār cēriyum, kaḷḷi uṇṇum kuṭikaḷuṭiya ūrkaḷumuṭiya koṟkiyiluḷḷōr virumpum porunaṉē!
pōril pāḻaṭinta pakivar nāṭu
nī niṉ ūriṭattē iruttali virumpātu pakivar mēṟceṉṟu porutali mēṟkoḷvi; yāṉikaḷip parappip puṟaṅkoṭuttār mēṟ cellāta paṭiyōṭu murukaṉ pakimēṟ celvatupōlat taṭiyaṟap pakivariṭattiṟ celluvi; ceṉṟu nāvalan tīviluḷḷa cōḻa maṇṭalam, toṇṭi maṇṭalam eṉṉum maṇṭalaṅkaḷi aṭippaṭuttip pakivar niṉ ēval kēṭkumpaṭi oḻukuvi; niṉṉōṭu naṭpuk koṇṭavar kuṭiyi uyarttuvi. ammuṟiyiṉāla niṉ kulattup periyōr oḻukiya aracamuṟi ituveṉṟu avarkku viḷakkuvi. piṉṉar ammaṇṭalaṅ kaḷic cūḻavirukkum kuṟunila maṉṉar araṇkaḷiluḷḷavaṟṟi eḷitiṟ koṇṭu avaṟṟi ellām niṉakkeṉap pātukāvātu ūriṭattiruntu piṟarkkuk koṭuppi. paṉi oḻukum malikaḷuḷḷa kāṭukaḷik kaṭantu pakivariṉ uḷnāṭukaḷilē pukuntu avar araṇmaṉik kikkoḷvi. toṉṟutoṭṭuvarum pakivar niṉ ēval kēṭṭu oḻukāmiyāl avar nilatti aṭintu, maraṅkaḷic cuṭṭu malikaḷi nīṟākkum iṭipōṉṟāy. aracar paṭi eṭukkum muṟimiyil eḻunta niṉpaṭiyiṉ āravāram viṇṇil olittatu. kutirikaḷ cellum vēkattil tūci eḻuntatu; caṅkum kompum ārttaṉa; vīrar maḻi pōl ampukaḷit tūviṉar; kāvaṟ kāṭṭi veṭṭi matilkaḷik kikkoṇṭu cuṟṟattārōṭu kūṭiya paki variṉ valiyit tolittaṉar. maruta maraṅkaḷi neruppu uṇṭatu. nāṭeṉṉum peyar peṟṟa iṭam kāṭeṉṉum peyar peṟṟatu. pacuttiraḷ taṅkumiṭam puli taṅkumiṭamāyiṟṟu; ūrāyirunta iṭaṅkaḷ pāḻāyk kiṭantaṉa. vaḷiyaṇinta kiyum aḻakumuṭiya makaḷir tuṇaṅkik kūtti yum tāḷa aṟutiyuṭiya kuravik kūttiyum maṟantaṉar. cāṉṟōr irunta ampalaṅkaḷil iraṭṭi aṭikaḷiyum accantarum pārviyiṉiyu muṭiya pēymakaḷir ulāvi āṭiṉar. illuṟi teyvaṅkaḷ ulāvum akaṉṟa ūriṭattu makkaḷi uḷḷēviṭum vāyil kāppōr niṉṟa vāyilkaḷil maṉa varuttamuṭiya peṇkaḷ iruntu varunti aḻutaṉar. paciyāl ularnta kuṭikaḷukkup pātukāvalāka ayal nāṭukaḷiluḷḷa cuṟṟattār vantu cērntaṉar. periya māḷikikaḷilē ventu vīḻnta karinta kutirilē cūṭṭiṉiyuṭiya kūkic cēval pēṭṭuṭaṉiruntu kuḻaṟiṟṟu. ceṅkaḻunīr neruṅkivaḷarum iṭamakaṉṟa poykiyiṭattu yāṉi niṉṟāl maṟiyum vāṭkōriyuṭaṉ caṇpakak kōriyum vaḷarntaṉa. erutukaḷ uḻuta viḷikiṉṟa vayalkaḷil peṇpaṉṟikaḷōṭu āṇpaṉṟikaḷ ōṭit tirintaṉa. niṉ ēval kēḷāmiyāl pakivaratu nāṭukaḷ ivvāṟu keṭṭup pāḻāyiṉa. an nāṭukaḷi āḷavēṇṭumātaliṉ aṅkup pala āṇṭukaḷ taṅki yiruppi; nī aṅkut taṅkutalāl annāṭukaḷ muṉṉilumatikam ciṟappaṭitaṟkuk kāraṇamāyuḷḷa vellum pōriṉiyuṭiya talivaṉē! teṉticikkumari vaṭatici imayam, kiḻakkum mēṟkum kaṭal ākiya ellikaḷukkuṭpaṭṭa nāṭṭiluḷḷār ellārum niṉ ēval kēṭkumpaṭi veṟṟimikunta taṉmiyuṭiyi oru poy collit tēvarulaki amutattōṭu peṟalāmāyiṉum ataṉivirumpātu meyyuṭaṉ naṭpuc ceytaluṭiyi. ivvulakiluḷḷa ellāruṭaṉum tēvarkaḷum pakivar kaḷāyp paṭi eṭuttuvariṉum pakivaruk kañcip paṇiyāy. teṉṟicik kaṇ malikaḷ niṟiyumpaṭi vāṇaṉ eṉṉum acuraṉ vitta poruṭṭiraḷ kiṭippatāyiṉum piṟar kūṟum paḻi varumeṉpatāl ataṉi virumpāy; piṟarkkuk koṭukkum pukaḻi virumpuvi. pukaḻuṭiyavaṉē! ellārālum toḻappaṭum piṟi nāḷtōṟum vaḷartal pōla uṉatu veṟṟit tiruviṉāl uṉ kāl vaḻiyil varuvōriṉ veṟṟiyum talimuṟi talimuṟiyāka vaḷarvatāka. pakivaratu ākkam tēypiṟit tiṅkaḷ pōla nāṭōṟum tēyvatāka. cēymik kaṇ ceṉṟu viḷaṅkum pukaḻ oru pōtum keṭātu nili peṟuvatāka.
tiruvaḻuti nāṭeṉṉuñ ciṟappup peyar peṟṟa pāṇṭiya maṇṭalam kuṟiñci, mulli, marutam, neytal, pāli eṉap periyōrāl variyaṟuk kappaṭṭa aivaki nilaṅkaḷum porunti iṉitu viḷaṅkum.
maruta nilaṅkaḷ
kīḻtticik kaṭalil nīriyuṇṭa mēkam mēṟṟicik kaṭaṟkariyi luḷḷa maliyiṟṟaṅki marutanilattē maḻiyip poḻiyum. ataṉāl eḻunta veḷḷam kavalik kiḻaṅku akaḻnta kuḻikaḷil pāyntu ōci ceyyum. maliyiṭattuḷḷa yāṉikkūṭṭaṅkaḷ añcumpaṭi iṭiyōṭu kūṭip peykiṉṟa perumaḻi mikutalāl āṟu muṭṭi āṟṟiṭik kuṟiyil ēṟumpaṭi veḷḷampāyum. avveḷḷattit tēkki vitta kuḷattu nīrāl vayalkaḷil yāṉi niṉṟāl teriyātu vaḷarnta nel viḷintirukkum. maṭukkaḷilum poykikaḷilum tāmarip pūvum, itaḻ virinta nīlappūvum melliya iliyiṉiyum vaṇṭukaḷiyumuṭiya āmpaṟpūvum, vaṇṭukaḷ taṅkum naṟumaṇamuḷḷa piṟa pūkkaḷum viḷaṅkum. orupāl, vaḷinta koṇṭiyuṭiya valiñar, campaṅ kōḻikaḷ uṟaṅkumpaṭi vaḷḷik koṭikaḷit taḷḷi mīṉkaḷi vārikkoṇṭu vantu vili kūṟi viṟkum kampali eḻum. karumpiṟku iṭṭa āliyiṭattu ōciyum, kaḷi paṟikkumōciyum orupāl eḻum. vayaliṭattuc cēṟṟilē kiṭakkum kiḻa erutiṉ varuttan tīrkkum kaḷḷuṇṭa kaḷamariṉ āravāramum nellaṟup pōriṉ āravāramum orupāl eḻum. maḻittuḷikaḷit tūṟum mēkan taṅkum tirupparaṅ kuṉṟil viḻāk koṇṭāṭu mōciyum, makaḷir kūṭṭam tamatu kūntal tamkaṇavar māliyuṭaṉ cērumpaṭi putuppuṉalāṭum ōciyum oru pāl vāṉil olikkum. palavakippaṭṭa ōcikaḷōṭu paṟavikaḷ olittuk koṇṭirukkumpaṭi mīṉkaḷi aṟukkiṉṟa pāṇar vīṭukaḷil eḻum pāṭal ōciyum cērntu olikkum.
mulli nilam
mulli nilattē veḷḷiya mucuṭṭippūvum mullip pūvum pāṟikaḷ mītu utirntu parantu kiṭakkum. kariya kāliṉiyuṭiya varakum eḷḷiṉ kāyum muṟṟiyirukkum. orupāl makkaḷ tiṉiyiṉi aṟuppar. aṅkumiṅkum āḻnta kuḻikaḷil irattiṉaṅkaḷ kiṭantu viḷaṅkum. paḷḷaṅ kaḷil niṟkum nīril neytal pūttirukkum.
kuṟiñci nilam
kuṟiñci nilattē aivaṉa nellum tōri nellum vaḷarntirukkum. orupāl tiṉiyiṭattil kiḷiyi ōṭṭumōciyum, orupāl avariyit tiṉṉum āmāvi ōṭṭum ōciyum, orupāl kuṟavaṉ āṇpaṉṟiyip poṟik kiṭaṅkil vīḻttik koṉṟatiṉāl uṇṭāṉa ōciyum eḻum. orupāl vēṅkip pūvip paṟikkum makaḷir puli puli eṉṉum āravāra mum, orupāl paṉṟiyik kollum puliyiṉ āravāramumākiya iv vōcikaḷellām maliyiṭattu etiroli ceyyum.
pāli nilam
aruvikaḷillāta aḻaku kuṉṟiṉa pāli nilattu ūkampul vik kōl pōṟ kāyntirukkum. mali muḻiñcukaḷ cūṟāvaḷiyi mukantu koḷḷum. ataṉāl vēṉiṟ kuṉṟu kaṭalpōl olikkum. iliyāl vēynta kuṭicikaḷil vāḻvōrum, māṉtōlākiya paṭukkiyiyum, taḻi viraviṉa māliyiyum uṭiyōrumākiya iḷiyōr āṟalikaḷ varik kākkumpaṭi aracar āṇiyāl curattē villōṭu celvar.
neytal nilam
neytal nilattē makkaḷ muttiyum caṅkiyum kaṭal tanta pal vakip paṇṭaṅkaḷiyum viṟpar. yavaṉa mutaliya nāṭṭiṉar kutiri mutaliya vaṟṟik koṇarntu koṭuttu veḷḷuppum tīmpuḷiyum koḻutta mīṉ kaḷit tuṇittu uppiṭṭu ularttiya karuvāṭum eṉṟivaṟṟik koṇṭu pōvar.
maturi nakarc ciṟappu
ivvintiṇippāṅkum aḻakuṟap peṟṟup pāṭal cāṉṟa naṉ ṉāṭṭu naṭuvil tiruvālavāy, tirunaḷḷāṟu, tirumuṭaṅki, tirunaṭuvūr eṉṉum nāṉkum kūṭutalāl nāṉmāṭakkūṭal eṉap peyar peṟṟa maturi mānakaram uḷḷatu. atu pūmātiṉ aḻakiya mukampōl polivu peṟ ṟirukkum. viyi āṟṟaṅkariyil vāṉaḷāvi uyarntu nerukkiya marak kiḷikaḷil mantikaḷ tāvi viḷiyāṭum; mayilkaḷ irukkum; mali ucci kaḷiṉiṉṟum kutikkum nīr koṇṭuvantu kuvittamaṇal kuvintu kuṉṟu kaḷākak kiṭakkum. ivvāṟu maraṅkaḷālum maṇaṟ kuṉṟukaḷālum ciṟappuṭi yatu āṇṭuḷḷa cōlikaḷ. nīrāṭuṅ karikaḷilē maraṅkaḷ utirtta malarkaḷ mālipōla viyi āṟṟiṟ cellum. avvāṟṟiṉ kaṇṇuḷḷa tuṟikaḷtōṟum pāṇ cātiyiṉar kuṭiyiruppukaḷuṇṭu. nakaric cūḻnta akaḻi, periya celvamuṭiya māṉaviṟalvēḷiṉ ampi ūri otta nāṭukaḷip paṟikoṭutta aracar paṭi eṭuttu vantu puṟaṅkāṭṭi ōṭuvataṟ kētuvāka maṇṇuṟa āḻntirukkum. nakaric cūḻnta matilkaḷ curaṅkavaḻi yuṭiyaṉavāy vāṉuṟa ōṅki niṟkum. ney pūcutalāl karumi eytiya neṭiya nilikaḷiyum, tiṇṇiya kataviṉiyumuṭiya vāyil mītamarnta māṭaṅkaḷmēka mulāvum malipōla uyarntirukkum. māntarum māvum iṭiyaṟātu pōkkuvarattuc ceytaliṉ kōṭṭivāyil iṭiyaṟātu ōṭu kiṉṟa viyi āṟṟiṉip pōṉṟirukkum. vāyilik kaṭanta aḷavil akaṉṟa terukkaḷum avaṟṟiṉ irupuṟaṅkaḷilum cāḷaraṅkaḷ palavaminta vīṭukaḷum uyarntu tōṉṟum; avvīṭukaḷum vītiyum āṟum ataṉ iru kariyumpōl tōṉṟum. kaṭivītiyil pala moḻikaḷ pēcum makkaḷiṉ mikka āravāram eḻum. orupāl muraci olittu viḻāvic cāṟṟu mōci kāṟṟāl kontaḷikkum kaṭalolipōl muḻaṅkum. nīrik kiyāl kuṭintu viḷiyāṭiṉāl eḻum ōciyuṭiya vāttiyaṅkaḷiṉ oliyik kēṭṭōr terukkaḷil niṉṟu āṭi āravārañ ceyvar. aṅkāṭit teru vilē kōyilkaḷukku viḻākkaḷi naṭattik kaṭṭiṉa aḻakiya koṭikaḷum, paṭittatalivar peṟṟa veṟṟiyiṉ poruṭṭu eṭutta veṟṟik koṭi kaḷum, kaṭkaṭikaḷil kaḷḷiṉ kaḷippu mikutiyic cāṟṟukiṉṟa koṭikaḷum periya maliyiṭattu aruvi aciyumāṟu acintu koṇṭirukkum.
tērppaṭi
paṉimīṉum caṅkum ulāvukiṉṟa kaṭalil pāymaram aṭiyōṭu muṟiyumpaṭi kāṟṟaṭikkiyiṉāl marakkalaṅkaḷ naṅkūrakkaṟ kaṭṭiya kayiṟ ṟuṭaṉē niṉṟulāvi nīrc cuḻiyilē akappaṭṭu niṉṟu cuḻalum. aracaṉuṭiya yāṉippaṭi ammarakkalaṅkaḷi oppak kōpam mikuntu cuḻaṉṟu kuttuk kōṟkārarik koṉṟu pākari eṟintu kāliṟ caṅkiliyip piṇittiruk kum kaṭṭuttaṟiyi muṟittaṉavum muṉṉum piṉṉum caṅkolikkap paṭuvaṉavumākiya matayāṉikaḷi uṭiyatu. tērppaṭi, kāṟṟip piḷantu ñāyiṟṟi aṭiyavēṇṭumeṉa virumpip paṟakkum ciṟanta kāliyuṭiya aṉṉac cēvali otta veṇṇiṟak kutirikaḷ pūṭṭap peṟṟuk kāṟṟeṉac cellum tērkaḷiyuṭiyatu.
kutirippaṭi
kutirippaṭi vaṭṭamākavum nērākavum ōṭum kaṭuñcelaviṉi yuṭiyatu. kālāṭpaṭi kaḷḷuṇṭu mayaṅki yāṉipōṟ cellum vīrari yuṭiyatu.
nāṟpaṭiyiṉ celavukkup paṇṇiyam viṟpōr añciyiruttal
ivviyalpuṭiya yāṉiyum tērum kutiriyum vīrarumākiya nāṟpaṭikaḷ vantu mīḷkiṉṟamiyāl iṉiya uṇavup paṇṭaṅkaḷi viṟpārum, poṟpūkkaḷ cūṭiya taliyiṉarum vīrakkaḻalaṇinta kāliṉaru mākiya vīrariṉmuṉ niṉṟu koṭṭappaṭum vīramattaḷattiṉatu kaṇpōṉṟu vaṭṭamāṉa pūntaṭṭilē iṭṭu vanta pūviṉi viṟpārum, cuṇṇam viṟpārum, cuṇṇāmpuṭaṉ veṟṟiliyum pākkum viṟpārum iṉṉu yirañci ēṅkiyiruppar; nāṟpaṭikaḷum akaṉṟapiṉṉar varuttam nīṅki, māṭa māḷiki kūṭa kōpuraṅkaḷiṉ kuḷirnta niḻalilē iruntu iḷip pāṟuvar.
aṅkāṭi makaḷir
iḷamakaḷir aḻakum civantaniṟamum āṭavari varuttum kaṇkaḷum, nāruritta āmpaṟṟaṇṭu pōṉṟa niṟamumuṭiyarā yiruppar; nēriya kūriya paṟkaḷuṭiya vāyum, toyyiṟ kuḻampāl koṭi eḻutap peṟṟavum nekiḻntu cintuvatupōṉṟu aḻaku tēmal parantavumākiya iḷiya taṉaṅ kaḷum, miyoḻukiṉāṟ pōṉṟa neruṅkiya kūntalum, mayiliṉ cāyalum, melliya pēccumuṭiya nalliyalpiṉar. avarkaḷ tammik kōlañ ceytu metteṉa naṭantu kiyit taṭṭi, kāmanukarcciyaṉṟi vēṟoṉṟuṅ kallā iḷiñarōṭu makiḻntu cērvar. caṅkupōl naritta mayiriṉik kōtip piṉṉē muṭinta kiḻappeṇṭir av viṉpam nukarum aḻakiṉi yuṭiyār virumpum poruḷkaḷi naṟumpūvuṭaṉ ēntic ceṉṟu maṉikaṭōṟum ulāvi viṟpar. mēkam mukappavum āṟukaḷ perukavum kaṭaliṉ nīr kūṭutal kuṟital iṉṟiyirukkum. atupōla aṅkāṭit teruvil palar vantu koḷḷak koḷḷak kuṟiyāmaluṅ koṇṭuvarak koṇṭuvarap paṇṭaṅkaḷ mikāmalum irukkum.
viḻākkāṇum peṇkaḷiṉ mukam aciyum koṭikaḷiṭiyē tōṉṟutal
mālikkālattē pūttoḻiluṭiya paṭṭāṭikaḷi uṭuttu ari yilē uṭivāḷic cerukit tōḷilē mēlāṭitarittu, vīrakkaḻalum vēppamāliyum māṇikka māliyum ceṅkaḻunīr māliyum aṇinta celvar, kutirikaḷ pūṭṭiya tēril kālāṭkaḷ cūḻa vītikaḷil celvar. māṭaṅkaḷtōṟum nilāmuṟṟattu niṉṟu viḻākkāṇum poṉṉāparaṇaṅ kaḷiyum pūttoḻiluṭiya vaḷikaḷiyum cilampiyum aṇinta makaḷiriṉ mukaṅkaḷ, koṭikaḷiṉ mītu mantamārutam paṭṭu acitalāl avaṟṟiṉ iṭiyē mēkattil maṟiyum tiṅkaḷ pōla orukāl tōṉṟi orukāl maṟiyum.
mālik kālattuk kaṭavuḷarukku viḻā eṭuttal
celvar tamatu aḻakiya uṭaliṟ ṟaṭaviyirukkum puṉuku mutaliyavaṟ ṟiṉ naṟumaṇam terukkaḷiṟ kamaḻum. peṇkaḷiṉ matiyi otta mukaṅkaḷ maṟuviṉṟit tōṉṟum. civaṉi ēṉik kaṭavuḷarilum mēlākak koṇṭu, vāṭāta pūkkaḷiyum itaḻ kuviyāta kaṇṇiṉiyum aviyākiya uṇa viṉiyu muṭiya māyōṉ murukaṉ mutaliya teṅvaṅkaṭkup pali koṭuttaṟku antik kālattiṟkuc ciṟitu muṉpu toṭaṅkiya viḻāvil vāttiyaṅkaḷ olikkum. puttamatak koḷkiyiṉarākiya iḷamakaḷir tāmarip pūvip piṭittāṟ pōlac ciṟu piḷḷi kaḷi eṭuttukkoṇṭu, taṅkaṇavarōṭu pūcikku vēṇṭum pūviṉiyum tūpaṅkaḷiyum kikaḷil ēnticceṉṟu vaṇaṅkum puttap paḷḷikaḷ ōriṭattu viḷaṅkum.
pala vēṟu camayattavar uṟiyumiṭaṅkaḷ
ciṟanta vētaṅkaḷi ōti yākāti karumaṅkaḷic ceytu piramaṅkaḷ tāṅkaḷēyāy vīṭṭiṉpatti ivvulakiltāṉē aṭikiṉṟa pirama vittukkaḷ iruppiṭam orupāl tōṉṟum. malik kūṭṭam pōla māṭa māḷikikaḷ uyarntu tōṉṟum nakaril pūkkaḷik kiyiṟ koṇṭu virataṅ kāppōr tutikkiṉṟa amaṇap paḷḷiyiṭattu mūṉṟukālamum mūvulakiṉ ceytiyum muḻutuṇarum camaṇamuṉivar uṟivar. appāl tulāk kōl pōṉṟu naṭuvu niliyuṭiya tarumattik kūṟum aṟaṅ kūṟavi tōṉṟum. yākaṅkaḷip paṇṇip periya cuvarkkatti ēṟap pōkum antaṇar aracaṉi aṭakkuvar. aracaṉ taṉṉiṭattuḷḷa naṉmiyum tīmiyum neñcat tālē kaṇṭu attīṅkukaḷi ārāyntu avaṟṟilē oḻukāmal tāṉ aṭakkukiṉṟa cuṟṟattiṉiyuṭiyaṉ. kāvitippaṭṭam kaṭṭap peṟṟavarum, talippāki tarittavarum aracaṉi aṟattiṉiṉṟum tiṟampāmal pātukāp pavarumākiya amiccar irukki appāl tōṉṟum. itaṟku appāl, paruntukaḷ iḷippāṟiyiruntu piṉpu uyarap paṟakkum taṉmiyavum pala toḻil māṭcimip paṭṭavumākiya, piṟanāṭukaḷiṉiṉṟum vantu celpavar kaḷuṭiya maṉikaḷ malikaḷ pōṉṟu tōṉṟum.
iṭiviṭātu maḻi peykiṉṟamiyāl viḷivuṭiya mōkū riṭattuk kōcar viḷaṅkiṉāṟ pōla paḻiyaṉ eṉṉum kuṟunila maṉṉa ṉuṭiya, purōkitar, cēṉāpatiyar, tūtar, oṟṟar eṉṉum nālvakip periya kūṭṭattiṉarum viḷaṅkuvar.
antik kaṭi
caṅki aṟuttu vaḷiyal mutaliyaṉa ceyvōrum, maṇikaḷit tuḷiyiṭuvōrum, poṟkollarum, poṉvāṇikarum, āṭi viṟ pārum, cempi niṟuttu vāṅkuvārum, pūkkaḷiyum cāntiyum viṟpārum, ōviyakkārarum, neytaṟ ṟoḻil ceyvōrum piṟaruṅkūṭi antik kaṭi yil oruvarkkoruvar kāl neruṅkumpaṭi niṟpar. avvāravāram paṉaṅ kuruttu māliyaṇinta cēraṉuṭiya periya nāḷōlakka iruppilē aṟivuṭiyōr kūṭit tarukkaṅkaḷik kūṟum āravāram pōṉṟiruntatu.
aṟak kūḻccāli
aṟakkūḻc cāliyil palāppaḻam, vāḻippaḻam, muntirikippaḻam mutaliya paḻaṅkaḷiyum, pākaṟkāy, vāḻikkāy, vaḻutuṇaṅkāy mutaliya kāykaḷiyum ilikkaṟikaḷiyum kaṇṭacarukkarit tēṟṟiyum, iṟicci yuṭiya cōṟṟiyum, kiḻaṅkukaḷuṭaṉē pāl mutaliyavaṟṟiyum parimāṟu vōr koṇṭuvantu paci tākaṅkaḷāl varuntuvārkkum vaṟiyavarkkum iṭuvar.
marakkalaṅkaḷiliruntu paṇṭaṅkaḷi iṟakkum ōci
marakkalaṅkaḷ koṇṭuvanta paṇṭaṅkaḷip paṟippatāl paṭṭiṉattē periya āravāram eḻum. paṇṭaṅkaḷi iṟakkuvatāl palliṭaṅkaḷilum eḻum ōci kuñcukku iriyik kavarntu paṟantu celkiṉṟa palvakip paṟavikaḷiṉ āravāratti ottirukkum.
mālik kālattu makaḷir ceyalkaḷ
ñāyiṟu mēṟkumaliyil maṟintatum, niṟi uvāt tōṉṟum pōtu periya nakariṭattuvāḻum peṇṇip peṇkāmuṟum aḻakiya makaḷir oḷiyuṭiya viḷakki ēṟṟuvārkaḷ; tām kātalikkum iṉiya kaṇavaric cērtaṟku neṭiya kūntaliṟṟaṭaviya mayirccānti alittu nīkkuvārkaḷ; kattūriyiyum naṟiya cantaṉattiyum taṭavuvārkaḷ; melliya nūlāṟ ceyta āṭikku akiṟpuki ūṭṭuvārkaḷ. ivvāṟu mālik kālam ōci uṭiyatāyirukkum. kaṇavarip pirintu kūṭṭatti virumpi yiruntārkku nōy ceyyum muṉ yāmam kaḻintatu.
vili makaḷir
nīla ākāyattē viḷiyāṭum teyvamakaḷir varuttumāṟupōlat tammik kaṇṭōruṭiya neñci varuttip poruḷ vāṅkum parattiyar viḷakkiṉ oḷiyil palarum oṉṟu cērakkūṭuvar. kaṭaṟkarit tuṟiyil kuvintamaṇalil yāḻ vācittu mattaḷaṅ koṭṭi āṭuvar. ataṉi veṟuttuk kuvaḷi malarāl toṭutta māliyi āṭiyiṉ viḷimpilē paṭumpaṭi aṇivar. veḷḷiya pūkkaḷāṟ kaṭṭiya mālikaḷi ākāyattil ceṉṟu nāṟumpaṭi koṇṭiyil vittu muṭivar. tamatu vaṭivaḻaki virumpi vanta uḷḷūrārum piṟanāṭṭārumākiya celva iḷiñarip pala vañcaṉiyuṭiya tam moḻikaḷāl kūṭṭic ceṉṟu avarkaḷ mārpu tam mārpiṟ paṭumpaṭi muyaṅkuvar. poruḷ peṟumaḷavum aṉpuṭiyār pōla vañcaṉiyākat taḻuvi avarkaḷiṉ celvam ellāvaṟṟiyum vāṅkuvar. pūvirikiṉṟa kālamaṟintu ataṉ nuṇṇiya tāti uṇṭu tātaṟṟa vaṟiya pūvip piṉ niṉiyāmal nīṅkum vaṇṭukaḷippōl tammik kalan tāruṭiya neñcu kalaṅkumpaṭi avaruṭiya kūṭṭattik kiviṭuvar; muṟ paṭṭa palaric cērnta cērkkiyāl kulinta oppaṉikaḷip piṉṉum aḻaku ceyvar. arumpukaḷ alarum ceṅkaḻunīrp pūviyum ēṉiya malar kaḷiyum koṇṭu maḻikku malarnta ciṟiya putarippōl tōṉṟumpaṭi tamatu kūntali vēyntukoḷvar; alarum paruvamaṭintu malarnta apputiya viṭupūkkaḷ terukkaḷeṅkum kamaḻum. vaḷiyaṇinta kiyuṭiya avvilimātar ki vīci naṭantu maṉikaṭōṟum ceṉṟu paḻamarattit tēṭic ceṉṟu ataṉ paḻaṅkaḷi uṇṇum puḷḷiṉam pōlap poruḷ koṭuttaṟkuriya iḷiñarōṭu viḷiyāṭuvar.
tirumāl piṟanta ōṇanāḷ viḻā
avuṇar kūṭṭatti veṉṟavarum poṉṉāṟ ceyta māli yaṇintavarumākiya tirumāl piṟanta ōṇanāḷil ūriṭattu viḻā naṭakkum. pōrttiṉavuṭiya maṟavar kaḷḷuṇṭu oruvarōṭoruvar porutu neṟṟiyilē kāyam paṭṭavarkaḷākat tōṉṟuvar. pōr paḻakum yāṉi ōṭṭutalāl ōṭukiṉṟa yāṉayip parikkōṟkārar ceṉṟu piṭippar; ataṉip piṭikkumaḷavum pōr yāṉi aṇukātapaṭi nīlattuṇiyil iṭṭu vittirukkum 1kappaṇattic citaṟuvar; kappaṇaṅkaḷ kālittuḷikka añcātavarkaḷākiya maṟavar neṟṟiyiṟpaṭṭa kāyaṅkaḷuṭaṉ tirivar.
kuḻantiyi īṉṟu iṭukkaṇ nīṅkiya makaḷir nīrāṭik kaṭavuḷip paravutal
īṉṟaṇimi nīṅkiya celva makaḷir teyvattiṉaruḷāl ōr iṭukkaṇumaṟṟuk kuḷattu nīriṟ kuḷippar. appōtu mutaṟ cūṟkoṇṭa makaḷir ivvāṟē iṭukkaṇiṉṟip putalvarip payattal vēṇṭumeṉṟu teyvattip paravuvar; piṉpu tamatu cuṟṟattuṭaṉ pūcikku vēṇṭum poruḷkaḷ palavaṟṟuṭaṉ 2tēvarāṭṭiyarōṭu yāḻiṟ cevvaḻip paṇṇi vācittu muḻavu koṭṭic ciṟupaṟi olippa mayil pōla metteṉa metteṉa naṭantu kiyāṟṟoḻutu pāṟcōṟu mutaliyavaṟṟi uṇpar. kār kālattē pūtta kuṟiñcip pūvic cūṭiya makaḷir kaṭappa māliyic cūṭu kiṉṟa murukaṉi meyyiṭattē niṟutti vaḻipaṭṭuk kikōttu maṉṟu kaṭōṟum kuraviyāṭuvar. ivaṟṟōṭu cērikaṭōṟum eḻum pāṭṭukkaḷum kalattaliṉālē vevvēṟu āravāram olikkum. naṉṉaṉ koṇṭāṭum piṟanta nāḷiṭattuc cēriyiluḷḷār koṇṭāṭukiṉṟa viḻāvāl āravāram uṇṭāṉāṟ pōlak kampali niṟinta mutaṟcāmam kaḻintatu.
yāmattil teyvaṅkaḷ iyaṅkutal
patiṉintu nāḻikikoṇṭa pātiiravil iraṇṭāñ cāmattukkum nālām cāmattukkumiṭiyil teyvaṅkaḷ ulāvum. caṅkukaḷ āravāra miṉṟik kiṭakkum; maṭap pattiṉiyum kaḷippiṉiyum oḷḷiya aṇikalaṉ kaḷiyumuṭiya makaḷir tuyil koḷvar. iṉiya pākōṭu cērttuk karitta māvi vittirukkum appavāṇikar iruntu uṟaṅkuvar. tirunāḷilē kūttāṭum kūttar kūttu oḻintu uṟaṅkuvar. pēykaḷum teyvaṅkaḷum uṟavukoṇṭu cuḻaṉṟu tiriyum.
nakar kāvalar
nakar kāvalar pulipōl vīramuṭiyar; tuyil koḷḷāta kaṇṇar; añcā neñcar; kaḷavut toḻiliyum tantiraṅkaḷiyum kaṇṭaṟiyum nuṇ ṇaṟiviṉar; kuṟi tappāta ampiṉar; terukkaḷil nīr perukkeṭuttōṭum kāriruḷ mūṭiya yāmaṅkaḷilum ūr cuṟṟivarum iyalpiṉar; kaṟutta uṭa liṉar; kariya uṭiyiṉar; vāḷkik koṇṭa kiyiṉar; melliya nūlēṇi cuṟṟiya ariyiṉar; viḻittakaṇ imikku muṉ maṟintōṭum valiyuṭiya kaḷvariyum kaṭitiṟ piṭikkum āṟṟaluṭiyar. ivviyalpiṉarākiya kāvalar ulāvik kāval puritalāl eṅkum oliyiṉṟi aṭaṅkiya kaṭalip pōl aṭaṅkiyirukkum. paṭukkiyil tuyilvōr iṉitu tuyilvar.
vikaṟiyil nikaḻvaṉa
vikaṟik kālattē vētatti muṟṟa ōtukiṉṟa antaṇar avaṟṟiluḷḷa tutikaḷip pāṭuvar. parikkōṟkārar yāṉikaḷukkuk kavaḷattit tīṟṟuvar. tēriṟ pūṭṭum kutirikaḷ pantiyil niṉṟu pulli mellum. paṇṭam viṟpār palvēṟu paṇṭaṅkaḷuṭiya tamatu kaṭikaḷi meḻukuvar. kaḷ viṟpōr kaḷippiṉiyuṭiya kaḷḷukku vili kūṟuvar. tiṇṇiya cuvarkaḷuṭiya vīṭukaḷiṉ katavukaḷ tiṟappatālum mūṭuvatālum oli ceyyum. kaḷḷi uṇṭukaḷittōr muḻaṅkukiṉṟa maḻali vārtti kaḷik kūṟuvar. niṉṟēttuvār niṉṟu vāḻttuvar. 1vitāḷikar tattamatu tuṟikkuriya pāṭalkaḷip pāṭuvar. nāḻiki colvōr collap paḷḷi eḻucci muracu olikkum. poṟiyiṉiyuṭiya kōḻic cēval viṭiyaṟ kālatti aṟivittuk kūvum. cēvaṟ paṟavikaḷ tattam peṭikaḷi aḻittu niṉṟu tīṅkural ceyyum. kūṭṭil uṟikiṉṟa karaṭi, puli mutaliya vilaṅkukaḷ muḻaṅkum. piṭiyōṭu kūṭiya yāṉikaḷ piḷiṟum. pōril mutuku kāṭṭiya vīraratum veṭṭuṇṭa vīraratum yāṉikaḷum, pakivar ūric cuṭukiṉṟa viḷakkilē pārttu nirikāttu niṉṟa vīrari māḷaveṭṭi vēlkōlāka aṭittu vanta pacuttiraḷum, tōṟṟa vēntar tiṟiyākak koṇṭu vanta āparaṇaṅkaḷum araṇmaṉiyi nōkkic ceṉṟu koṇṭirukkum. ivvāṟu kaṅkiyāṟu kaṭaliṟ kalantāṟ pōla ulakiṟ poruḷkaḷellām maturiyi aṭiyum.
aracaṉ tuyileḻuntu ceyvaṉa
immānakaril kōyil koṇṭu ceṅkōl celuttukiṉṟa pāṇṭiyaṉ, viḷaṅkukiṉṟa aṇikalaṉkaḷ pūṇṭa makaḷir poṉṉāṟ ceyta vaṭṭilkaḷilē vārtta naṟumaṇam vīcum kaḷḷik koṭukkak koṭukka ataṉiuṇpāṉ; uṇṭu ciṟanta kaṟpum, aḻakiya tēmalum, maṇi kaḷaḻuttiya poṉṉaṇikaḷum, māntaḷir pōṉṟa niṟamum, kūriya eyiṟum, viḷaṅkukiṉṟa makarakkuḻi poruntiya kātum, malarnta centāmari pōṉṟa mukamum, vaḷikaḷumuṭiya araciyiṉ tōḷil muyaṅkuvāṉ, tūṅku mālikaḷ toṅkum paṭukkiyil tuyil koḷḷuvāṉ. tāṉ ceyyum “nallaṟaṉum oṇ poruḷum cintittu” vikaṟi yāmattut tuyileḻu vāṉ; eḻuntu kālikkaṭaṉkaḷi muṭippāṉ; mārpilē māli tarittu, kiyilē vīravaḷiyiyum viralilē kaṇiyāḻiyiyum ceṟittu, kañci yiṭṭa tukili uṭuttu, ataṉ mēl aṇiyum aṇikalaṉkaḷi aḻakuṟa aṇintu kolumaṇṭapattil vīṟṟiruppāṉ. irunta aḷavilē ēṉātip paṭṭam mutaliya ciṟappup peṟṟa paṭittalivar avaṉatu veṟṟit tiṟatti vāḻttuvar.
aracaṉ ōlakka maṇṭapattiliruntu vīrarkkum piṟarkkum paricaḷittal
piṉpu avaṉ tāṉ nāṭu nakar peṟumpaṭiyākavum veṟṟi ciṟakkum paṭiyākavum utaviya vīrari ellām aḻittum pāvalaruṭaṉē pāṇar pāṭṭiyari varavēṟṟum yāṉi tēr eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa paricukaḷ aḷippāṉ. avvāṟu avaṉ paṟpala poruḷkaḷik koṭuttalāl an nāṭṭil vāḻum kuṭimakkaḷ celva vaḷampeṟṟiruppar. ivvāṟellām ciṟappuṟ ṟirukkum vēntē! vāḻka.
ivvulakil pukaḻ ōṅka vāḻnta maṉṉar māṇṭaṉar
ivvulakiṟ pukaḻuṭiya paṟpala vēntar vāḻntaṉar. pukaḻ ceṟinta vēntar celvavaruvāyiṉiyuṭiya tamatu nakariliruntu yāḻ vācittāṟ pōṉṟu pāṭum viṟaliyar aṇiyumpaṭi vaḷikaḷi nāṭōṟum nalkiṉar. koḻutta iṟicciyit tiṉṟu cōṟṟi veṟuttuk kaḷḷarunti avaṟ ṟālum āci taṇiyap peṟātapāṇar makiḻumpaṭi pala piṭiyāṉikaḷiyum, tam cuṟṟattiṉar makiḻumpaṭi paki aracar araṇkaḷi aḻittuk kavarnta poruḷkaḷiyum vaḻaṅkiṉar. viṭiyaṟ kāliyil vantu pāṭum1cūtarkkut tēruṭaṉ kutiriyik koṭuttaṉar. periya kuppikaḷ vaṟṟumpaṭi kaḷḷuṇṭu vañci cūṭip pūcip pularnta cantaṉatti yuṭiyōrit tamakkup paṭittalivarākak koṇṭaṉar; tammi vaḻipaṭṭōr ēval kēṭṭu vaḻi paṭātōr nāṭukaḷ tiṟi aḷittu ēval kēṭkumpaṭi pōrmēṟ ceṉṟaṉar; uyarap paṟakkum paruntukaḷum paṟakkalāṟṟāta ucciyuṭiya araṇkaḷuṭiya pācaṟi yiṭattē viṭiyaṟkālattē nāḷ muracam olikkumpaṭi taṅkiṉar. pakivar paṭikkuk kēṭuṇṭāka veṉṟu eṇṇip piṉṉum aḻikka vēṇṭumeṉṟu virumpi nilaṅkaḷi aḻittaṉar. ivvaki valiyiṉiyum veṟṟi muraciṉiyu muṭiyarāy viḷaṅkiṉar. ivvāṟu pukaḻ viḷaṅkum paṭiyum ulakam ēval kēṭkumpaṭiyum vāḻntu piṟappaṟa muyalātu māṇṭa maṉṉar kaṭaṟkari maṇalilum palarāvar. aṇṇalē! ap payaṉiṉmi yālē nīyum avvāṟu kaḻital ākā teṉṟu ivvāḻviṟ periyatāyiruppa toru poruḷi yāṉ kūṟukiṉṟēṉ. aḵtu eṉṉāṟ kāṭṭutalaritu. ataṉik kaṇṇaṉip pōṉṟavum yākacāli mutu kuṭumiyip pōṉṟavum tollāṇi nallāciriyariṭattiṟ kēṭpāyāka. kantaḻiyiṭattiṟ kaṇṭaviyappiyum piṉpu peṟṟa amitiyiyum talimi peṟṟa palar colla nī kēṭpāyāka. perumāṉē! nalla ūḻikkālattē ittuṇik kālamirutti yeṉap pālvari teyvattālē variyappaṭṭu nī aṟutiyākap peṟṟa nāḷ muḻutum iṉitākap pēriṉpatti nukarntiruppāyāka. ataṉi nukarātu aimpoṟikaṭkum muṉṉiṟkap paṭuvaṉavākiya innukar poruḷ kaṭku niṉṉōṭu eṉṉa uṟavuṇṭu? iṉi uṉṉiṭattu uṇṭākiya māyi keṭuvatāka.

7. 1neṭunalvāṭi
kūtir kālattiṉ iyalpu
paruvam poyyāta mukilkaḷ mēruvi valamāka vantu ulakam kuḷirumpaṭi maḻipeytaṉa. mantikaḷi aṭittu ōṭṭum kōlik kiyiluṭiya iṭiyar veḷḷatti veṟuttavarkaḷāy ēṟukaḷuṭiya mantikaḷi mēyumpaṭi mēṭṭunilattē viṭṭaṉar. avarkaḷ mārpiṭat taṇinta kāntaḷ itaḻkaḷāṟ kaṭṭiya mālikaḷ maḻittuḷikaḷ vīḻtalāl aḻaku aḻintaṉa. kuḷirāl varuntum iṭiyar kiyiṭattē neruppik koṇṭu alakuṭaṉ alaku cērntu paṟpaṟi koṭṭumpaṭi naṭuṅkiṉar. vilaṅkukaḷ mēyātu niṉṟaṉa. kuraṅkukaḷ kuḷirāl naṭuṅkiṉa. kāṟṟu mikuti yāl paṟavikaḷ maraṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum nilattil vīḻntaṉa. pacukkaḷ kaṉṟukkup pāl koṭātu kālāl utittaṉa. putarkaḷil niṉṟa mucuṭṭi veṇṇiṟa malarkaḷi malarntatu. pīrkkup poṉṉiṟap pūvip pūttatu. virintu pāyum nīrukketirē kayal mīṉkaḷ nīnticceṉṟaṉa. maḻi ōyntatum paciyakā luṭiya kokkuttiraḷum variyuṭiya nārikkūṭṭamum paranta kariya vaṇṭa lum cēṟum paranta īramuṭiya veṇmaṇaliliruntu mīṉkaḷi uṇṭaṉa. vāṉil eḻunta veḷḷiya mukilkaḷ tuḷikaḷic citaṟiṉa. vayalkaḷil nīrukkumēl uyarntu vaḷarnta neṟ payirkaḷīṉṟa katirkaḷ muṟṟi viḷin tiruntaṉa. parutta aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya kamukiṉ nīlamaṇi pōṉṟa taliyiṉi yuṭiya kāykaḷ uḷḷē nīr niṟintāṟpōl tiraṇṭu muṟṟiyiruntaṉa. malarkaḷ niṟinta cōliyiṭattuḷḷa maraṅkaḷ kiḷikaḷilē taṅkiya nīric corintaṉa. uyarnta māṭaṅkaḷum celvamum malinta paḻiya ūriṭattu akaṉṟu nīṇṭa terukkaḷ āṟu kiṭantāṟ pōl kāṭciyaḷittaṉa. taḻi viravit toṭutta mālikaḷik kaḻuttil aṇintavarum, vaṇṭu moyk kiṉṟa kaḷḷi uṇṭukaḷippu mikuntavarkaḷum, muṉṉumpiṉṉum toṅkum paṭi uṭiaṇintavarkaḷum, muṟukkēṟiya uṭampiṉarumākiya milēccar maḻittuḷikku añcātavarkaḷāy terukkaḷilē tirintaṉar. māṭap puṟākkaḷ irākkālamum pakaṟkālamum aṟiyātu mayaṅkiṉa; cēvaṟ puṟā taṉatu pēṭṭuṭaṉ veḷiyiṟ ceṉṟu iritēṭi uṇṇātu palaki mītu iruntu kaṭuttakālkaḷ āṟumpaṭi kāli māṟṟi māṟṟi iruntatu.
makaḷir kūntalukku naṟumpuki ūṭṭutal
vēli purivōr kāvaluṭiya vīṭukaḷil cāntarikkum ammi yilē kattūri mutaliya paciya kūṭṭi arittaṉar. ākavē, vaṭanāṭṭār koṇṭuvanta veḷḷiya cantaṉak kaṟkaḷum teṟkē kiṭikkiṉṟa cantaṉak kaṭṭikaḷum payaṉpaṭātu kiṭantaṉa. kuḷir mikutiyāl kūntaliṭattu māliyic cūṭa muṭiyāta makaḷir ataṉiṭattuc cila malar vittu muṭikka virumpic, cantaṉak kaṭṭiyi erittu ataṉiṭattē akiṟkaṭṭiyiyum cevvarakkiyum iṭṭuppukittaṉar. kitērnta kammiyarāl aḻaku peṟac ceyyappaṭṭa cenniṟa ālavaṭṭaṅkaḷ civanta uṟikaḷil iṭappaṭṭum cilantiyiṉ veḷḷiya nūlāṟ cūḻappaṭṭum vaḷinta muḷikaḷil toṅkiṉa.
iḷiyōrum mutiyōrum tīkkāyntaṉar
paṭukkiyiṭattil iḷavēṉiṟ kālattuk kuḷirnta teṉṟaṟ kāṟṟi vīcukiṉṟa cāḷaraṅkaḷil ulāvuvāriṉmiyāl avaṟṟiṉ iraṇṭu katavukaḷum cērumpaṭi tāḻiṭṭaṭikkappaṭṭiruntaṉa. vāṭik kāṟṟuk kalleṉṉum ōciyuṭiya ciṟu tivalikaḷi eṅkum parappiṟṟu. iḷi yōrum mutiyōrum kuḷir mikutiyāl kuvinta vāyuṭiya karakattil nīruṇṇātu tīccaṭṭiyiluḷḷa neruppil kuḷir kāyntaṉar. āṭum makaḷir tāmpāṭukiṉṟa pāṭalkaḷik kariya taṇṭiṉiyuṭiya yāḻil vācittaṟku narampukaḷit tamatu taṉaṅkaḷiṭattē taṭaviveppam ūṭṭi yāḻip paṇ niṟkum muṟiyil niṟuttiṉārkaḷ. ivvāṟu kārkālam maḻi mikuntu kūtir kālamāka nili peṟṟatu.
ciṟpa nūlār aracaṉukku māḷiki amittal
cittirit tiṅkaḷiṉ mattiyil oru nāḷil patiṉintāvatu nāḻikiyil aracaṉuṭiya maṉi amippataṟku nāḷ kuṟikkappaṭṭatu. ciṟpanūlōr am mukūrttattil nūli nērē piṭittut ticiyi aṟintu atticikaḷukkuriya kaṭavuḷari vaḻipaṭṭaṉar; piṉpu aracaṉukku vēṇṭiya maṉikaḷiyum vāyilkaḷiyum maṇṭapaṅkaḷiyum vakuttaṉar. avi kaḷic cuṟṟi matil iṭappaṭṭatu. matiliṉ vāyil irumpiṉāl amikkap paṭṭuc cevvarakkup pūcappaṭṭatu. ataṉ katavukaḷ cērttut tāḻiṭumpaṭi iraṇṭāka amikkappaṭṭaṉa. 1uttarakkaṟkaviyil palamaraṅkaḷ iṭi veḷiyiṉṟic cerukappaṭṭaṉa. uttarak kaṟkaviyiṉ naṭuvē ilakkumiyum irupuṟattum ceṅkaḻunīrp pūvumākiya vēlippāṭu ceyyap paṭṭiruntatu. nilikaḷ mītu ney pūcic ciṟuveṇkaṭuku appappaṭṭiruntatu. vāyil maliyi iṭiyē tiṟantāṟpōṉṟu yāṉikaḷ veṟṟik koṭiyuṭaṉ cellumpaṭi uyaramuṭiyatu. ataṉiṭattē kavarimāvum aṉṉappuḷḷum tāvittirintaṉa. muṟṟam veṇmaṇal parappappaṭṭu ilakkumi taṅkum ciṟappup peṟṟiruntatu.
antappuram
pantiyil niṉṟu veṟuttaṉavum piṭarimayiriṉi yuṭiyaṉavumākiya kutirikaḷ pulli atakkutalāl ōci eḻuntatu. nilā muṟṟattil nīr vantu koppaḷikkumpaṭi amikkappaṭṭa kuḻāy mīṉiṉ aṅkāntavāyip pōṉṟiruntatu. kuḻāyiṉiṉṟum viḻunta nīr niṟitaliṉālē kalaṅki ōṭum aruvi ōci ceytatu. ataṉ pakkattē mayilkaḷ kompu vāttiyattiṉ oli pōl āravārittaṉa. ivvōcikaḷellām malikaḷil eḻukiṉṟa āravāratti ottaṉa. kōyiliṉ antap purattēyuḷḷa tūṇkaḷ nīla maṇiyāl amikkappaṭṭavi pōṉṟu karumiyum tiraṭciyum peṟ ṟiruntaṉa. uyarnta cuvarkaḷ cempiṉāṟ ceytāṟ pōṉṟa pala vēlip pāṭukaḷuṭiyaṉavāyiruntaṉa. cuvariṉ pala viṭaṅkaḷil pala pūkkaḷi yuṭiya koṭikaḷ viḷaṅkumpaṭi ceñcāntiṉāl eḻutappaṭṭiruntaṉa. avi pala niṟamuṭiyaṉavākat tōṉṟiṉa. kōyiliṉ antappurattē yavaṉar ceyta pāvi viḷakkiṉ ney niṟinta takaḷiyil periya tirikaḷ koḷutti eriya viṭappaṭṭiruntaṉa. araci irukkum antappuram viḷakkil ney vaṟṟi oḷi maḻuṅkiya kālattup pāṇṭiyaṉallatu vēṟu kuṟṟēval ceyyum āṇkaḷ tiriyit tūṇṭāta ariya kāvaliyuṭiyatu.
araci paḷḷikoḷḷum kaṭṭil
paḷḷiyaṟiyilē pāṇṭimātēvi paḷḷikoḷvāḷ. avaḷ paḷḷi koḷḷum kaṭṭil vaṭṭavaṭiviṉatu; nāṟpatiyāṇṭu niṟintu pōrk kaḷattē paṭṭa kaḷiṟṟiṉ tāṉāka vīḻnta maruppiṉic cīvik kammiyarāl mūṭṭuvāy teriyāmal porutti amikkappaṭṭatu. taccar tamatu uḷi kaḷāl iliyiṉ vaṭivaṅkaḷi ataṉ iṭiyē cetukki yuḷḷārkaḷ; pala niṟa mayirkaḷi1 uḷḷē vittu avaṟṟiṉ mēlē ciṅkam mutaliyavaṟṟi vēṭṭiyāṭukiṉṟa vaṭivākac ceyta takaṭukaḷi vittum, mullip pūkkaḷiyum piṟapūkkaḷiyum amittum cāḷaraṅkaḷākat tiṟanta veḷi kaḷi āṇikaḷāl tittum pala vēlippāṭukaḷ ceytuḷḷārkaḷ. nūliṟ kōkkappaṭṭa muttu mālikaḷ kaṭṭilic cūḻntu toṅkumpaṭi tūkkap paṭṭaṉa. naṭuvēyuḷḷa veḷi puliyiṉ niṟamuṭiya nāṭāviṉāṟ piṉṉap paṭṭatu. kālkaḷ cūlkoṇṭa peṇkaḷiṉ taṉaṅkaḷ pōṉṟu tiraṇṭu ōriṭattil ciṟumiyum ōriṭattil parumiyum tōṉṟumpaṭi vēlippāṭu ceyyappaṭṭiruntaṉa. ivviyalpiṉatākiya paṭukkimītu pēṭṭuṭaṉ cērnta aṉṉac cēvaliṉ tūviyip parappi ataṉmēl kañci tōytta veḷḷit tukiliṉi virittu ataṉ mītu ceṅkaḻunīr mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ pūvitaḻkaḷ parappappaṭṭaṉa.
tōḻiyar araciyit tēṟṟutal
irācāmātēvi appaṭukki mītu kiṭantu aracaṉatu pirivāṟṟā miyāl tuyar uḻantu taṉatu aṇikalaṉ mutaliyavaṟṟi veṟuttāḷ. muttukkaḷik kōttuc ceyta kaccuc cumanta taṉaṅkaḷ mītu tālināṇ oṉṟumē kiṭantatu; poṉvaḷikaḷ kiṭantu taḻumpēṟiya kiyil valampuric caṅkāṟ ceyta vaḷiyalum kāppākak kaṭṭiya nūlumē kiṭantaṉa; pūttoḻiluṭiya paṭṭāṭiyi uṭukkum ariyil nūlāṟ ceyta mācēṟiya āṭi kiṭantatu; kiyiṉāl taṭavāmiyāl ularnta metteṉṟa mayirkaḷ citintu neṟṟiyiṟ paṭumpaṭi kiṭantaṉa. ivvāṟu kūtir kālattu naṭuyāmattē araci tuyiliṉṟik kiṭantāḷ. appoḻutu avaḷ tōḻiyar, “uṉatu maṉatukkiṉiyavar ippoḻutē varuvar” eṉpaṉa pōṉṟa poruḷōṭu puṇarāp poymoḻiyum mey moḻiyumākiya urikaḷ pala vaṟṟic collit tēṟṟiṉar; avaḷ avaṟṟik kēṭṭum tēṟāḷāyiṉāḷ. meḻuku pūcivaḻitta kaṭṭiliṉ mēṟ kaṭṭi mēlē putitāka eḻutiya tiṅkaḷiṉ pakkattē irukkum urōkiṇiyip pārttu, “itu pōṉṟu yāmum piriviṉṟiyirukkap peṟṟilēmō” eṉṟu cintittup perumūcceṟintāḷ. kaṇkaḷiṉiṉṟum tatumpukiṉṟa nīrit taṉatu 1muṭakku mōtiram aṇinta civanta viralāl eṭuttut teṟintāḷ. māntaḷir pōṉṟa mēṉiyum ammēṉiyiṉ iṭiyē paranta cuṇaṅkum, mūṅkil pōṟ ṟiraṇṭa melliya tōḷum, kacciṉāl valittuk kaṭṭappaṭṭa tāmari muki pōṉṟa taṉaṅkaḷum, oṭuṅki vaḷikiṉṟa iṭiyum, metteṉṟa taṉmiyum uṭiyarākiya tōḻiyar, avaḷatu cempañcu ūṭṭappaṭāta aṭikaḷit tuyil uṇṭākumāṟu mella varuṭiṉārkaḷ. niṟaṅkaḷ ūṭṭi eḻutap peṟāta vaṭivuṭiya ōviyam pōṉṟavaḷāy taṉimiyōṭu kiṭakkum aṉpumiku kiṉṟa arivikkuk koṟṟaviyi nōkkip paravukiṉṟavaḷ kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.
koṟṟaviyip paravum tēvarāṭṭi kūṟukiṉṟāḷ.

ammā! kēṭpāyāka. cēraṉ, cempiyaṉ, titiyaṉ, eḻiṉi, erumi yūrāṉ, iruṅkō vēṇmāṉ, porunaṉ mutalil eḻuvarōṭu porutal kuṟittuc ceṉṟa aracaṉ māṟṟāriṉ pācaṟiyiṭattuḷḷāṉ.

neṟṟip paṭṭattiyuṭiya yāṉikaḷik koṉṟa vīrar māṟṟāriṉ viḷaṅkum vāḷāl veṭṭik kāyappaṭuttappaṭṭaṉar. aracaṉ avarkaḷ puṇ kaḷip parikarittaṟku naṭuyāmattē nittiri koḷḷātavaṉāy veḷiyē pōntu tirivāṉ. vāṭikkāṟṟu vīcuntōṟum viḷakkukaḷ teṟku nōkkiya taliyuṭiyaṉavāy eriyum. vēppamāliyit taliyilē kaṭṭiya vēluṭaṉ muṉṉē naṭantu celkiṉṟa paṭittalivaṉ puṇpaṭṭa vīrari muṟiyē kāṭṭuvāṉ. aracaṉ avarkaḷiṉ akam malarumpaṭi mukamalarntu iṉṉuri kūṟuvāṉ. āṅkāṅkuk kaṭṭi niṟkum kutirikaḷ tammītu viḻum maḻittuḷikaḷi utaṟum. aracaṉ vāḷeṭuttaṟkut tōḷilē vitta valakkiyaṉāy iṭattōḷiṉiṉṟum naḻuvi vīḻkiṉṟa uttariyatti iṭappakkatē aṇippāṉ. muttumāli kaṭṭappaṭṭa veṟṟikkuṭi maḻiyi maṟikkum. ivvāṟu pācaṟiyiliruntu pōr ceykiṉṟa toḻil aracaṉukku veṟṟiyuṇṭāki iṉitu muṭivatāka.

8. 1kuṟiñcippāṭṭu
(itu tōḻi naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟatākac ceyyappaṭṭa pāṭal. aṟattoṭu niṉṟa leṉpatu kaḷavi veḷippaṭuttal. talivi pāṅkikku aṟattoṭu niṟkum, pāṅki cevilikku aṟattoṭu niṟkum, cevili naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṟkum, naṟṟāy tanti taṉṉiyarukku aṟattoṭu niṟkum.)
tōḻi naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṟṟal
tāyē vāḻvāyāka; aṉṉāy! yāṉ kūṟuvati virumpik kēṭpā yāka. viḷaṅkukiṉṟa neṟṟiyiyuṭiya eṉatu tōḻi taṉṉuyirittāṉ vittirāmikkuk kāraṇamākiya tāṅkutaṟkariya ōr eṇṇatti maṟittu vittirukkiṉṟāḷ, anniṉiviṉāl avaḷatu āparaṇaṅkaḷ nekiḻn taṉa. maruntiṉāl māṟṟaṟ kariya avaḷatu nōyi uṉakkuc collutal eḷitaṉṟu; ākavē ataṉi nāṉ eṉṉuḷḷē aṭakki vikkalāyiṉēṉ. avaḷuṭiya aḻaku keṭṭatu; tōḷkaḷ melintaṉa; vaḷikaḷ kaḻaṉṟaṉa. ivvāṟu nikaḻumpaṭi avaḷ taṉimiyāl varuntum varuttattik kaṇṭum atu nērvataṟkuriya kāraṇam yātu eṉa aṟiyum aṟivu illiyāyiṉi. nī varuttamaṭintu ūriṭattuk kaṭṭiṉālum kaḻaṅkiṉālum aṟintu kuṟi colluvāri viṉāviṉāy. avar atu teyvattāl nērnta varuttam eṉakkūṟiṉar. ātaliṉāl nī paṉṉiṟa malarkaḷum tūpaṅkaḷum cantaṉam mutaliyavum koṭuttup pala teyvaṅkaḷi vaḻippaṭṭaṉi. ataṉāl an nōy taṇiyāmi kaṇṭu varuttamuṟṟu varuntiṉāy. yāṉ avaḷiṭat tuḷḷa āṟṟutaṟkariyanōy yāteṉak kēṭṭēṉ. avaḷ eṉṉi nōkki, “muttālum maṇiyālum poṉṉālum ceyta ariya aṇikalaṉkaḷ tolintaṉavāyiṉum avi piṉ kiṭikkavum peṟum. atupōlaṉṟik kuṇaṅkaḷiṉ amitiyum, mēmpāṭum, oḻukkamum keṭṭāl ak kuṟṟattik kaḻuvi muṉpōlap pukaḻi niṟuttutal kuṟṟamaṟṟa aṟiviṉi yuṭiya muṉivarkkum muṭivataṉṟu eṉap paḻiya nūlkaḷi uṇarnta aṟiñar kūṟuvar. talivarum yāṉum kātal maṇañ ceytu koṇṭō meṉpati nām iruvarum tāykkut terivittuk koḷvōmāyiṉ namakkup pukaḻ iṉṟip paḻi varuvatilli yākum. ivvāṟu nām nam kātal maṇatti veḷippaṭuttiyapiṉpu, tamar talivarkkē nammik koṭukka nērntilarāyiṉum nām uyir pōkumvariyum ivvaruttattip poṟut tiruntāl namakku maṟumiyil ikkūṭṭam varuvatākum eṉṟu kūṟiṉāḷ; māṉ pōṉṟa pārviyiṉṟi mayaṅkiya nōkkaṅkoṇṭu ceyaloḻintu ayarntu āṟṟamuṭiyāta nōy uṭiyaḷāyiṉāḷ. niṉakkum ivaḷ varuttattukkum añci iraṇṭu periya accattālē yāṉum irupaki aracaric cantu ceyvikkum cāṉṟōrip pōla varuntukiṉṟēṉ. irukuṭikaḷum ottiruttaliyum kuṇattiyum cuṟṟattu utavikaḷiyum ottu nōkki ikkaḷavu maṇattic ceytōm. muṉpu atu nikaḻnta vakiyic colla virumpiṉēṉ. tāyē! ataṉik kēṭṭuk kōpam koḷḷā tiruppāyāka.
taliviyum tōḻiyum tiṉippuṉattē kiḷi ōṭṭutal
“periya katirkaḷiyuṭiya tiṉiyuḷ vīḻkiṉṟa kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭip pakaṟpoḻutu kaḻiyāmuṉ vīṭucērvīrāka” eṉṟu eṅkaḷip puṉattē pōkkā niṉṟāy. yāṅkaḷum ceṉṟu marattiṉucciyilē amikkappaṭṭa pirampiṉāl piṉṉi amitta paraṇil ēṟi iruntōm; iruntu kavaṇum taṭṭiyum piṟavumākiya kiḷiyōṭṭum karuvikaḷi muṟiyē kiyil eṭuttu, “āyō” eṉṟum coṟkaḷi naṭuvē collik kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭi ṉōm. veyil mikunta pakaṟkālattē paṟavikaḷ tām virumpum cērkki kaḷukkuc ceṉṟaṉa. kaṭaliṭattē nīri uṇṭa mēkam kuṟu muḻakkañ ceytu murukak kaṭavuḷiṉ vēl pōl viḷaṅkumpaṭi miṉṉi maliyiṭattu maḻiyip poḻintatu. malic cikarattiṉiṉṟum veḷḷiya aruvi kutittōṭiṟṟu.
nāṅkaḷ cuṉi āṭiṉōm
paḷiṅkik karittu ūṟṟiṉāṟ pōṉṟa nīriṭattu viruppuṭiyē māy ataṉiṭattē viḷiyāṭi namakku viruppantarum pāṭalkaḷip pāṭiṉōm. poṉṉilē aḻuntiṉa nīlam pōṉṟu mutukilē toṅkum kūntaliṉ nīrip piḻintōm. eṅkaḷ kaṇkaḷ civantiruntaṉa.
pūkkaḷip paṟittu pāṟiyil kuvittōm
piṉṉar ammalic cāraliṭattuḷḷa civanta pūkkaḷiyum mali erukku, civanta kōṭal, āmpal, aṉiccam, ceṅkaḻunīr, kuṟiñci, veṭci, ceṅkoṭuvēri, tēmā, cemmaṇi, perumūṅkil, eṟuḻ, marāmaram, kūviram, vaṭavaṉam, veḷḷiya voṭṭappāli, pañcāyk kōri, veṇkāk kavaṇam, karuviḷam, payiṉi, vāṉi, kuravam, paccili, makiḻ, kāyā, āviri, ciṟu mūṅkil, cūri, ciṟupūḷi, kuṉṟi, murukkili, marutu, kōṅru, mañcāṭi, pātiri, cerunti, puṉali, karanti, kāṭṭumalliki, mā, tilli, pāli, mulli, kañcā, piṭavam, ceṅkaruṅkāli, vāḻi, vaḷḷi, neytal, teṅkiṉ pāḷi, centāmari, ñāḻal, muval, kokuṭi, pavaḻakkāṉ malliki, cāti karuntāmarikkoṭi, veṇkōṭal, tāḻi, curapuṉṉi, kāñci, karuṅku vaḷi, ōmi, māvam, iṇṭam, ilavam, koṉṟi, aṭumpu, ātti, avari, pakaṉṟi, palācam, acōkam, vañci, picci, karunocci, tumpi, tiruttuḻāy, tōṉṟi, nantiyāvaṭṭam, naṟi, puṉṉi, parutti, pīrkku, kurukkatti, cantaṉam, akil, nārattam, nākam, iruvāṭci, kuntam, vēṅki, oṇ ceṅkāntaḷ mutaliyavaṟṟiṉ virumpappaṭum pūkkaḷi ellām paṟittu maḻiyiṉāṟ kaḻuvappaṭṭa pāṟimītu kuvittōm; āyō eṉṟu iṭiyiṭiyē olittuk kiḷikaḷi ōṭṭiṉōm; taḻikaḷik koytu ariyilē taḻi uṭiyi uṭuttōm; melliya malarkaḷi mirutuvāṉa kariya kūntalil aḻakupeṟac cāttiṉōm.
aḻakiya tōṉṟal avviṭattē vantāṉ
piṉṉar neruppup pōṉṟa niṟattuṭaṉ viḷaṅkum aḻakiya taḷiruṭiya acōkiṉ pūntātu utirkiṉṟa kuḷirnta niḻalil pōyirun tōm. appoḻutu ōr aḻakiya tōṉṟal vantāṉ. avaṉ mayiriṭattu mayirc cāntip pūci ulartti, akiṟpuki ūṭṭi, akil ney taṭavi muṭinta nīlamaṇi pōṉṟa koṇṭi uṭiyaṉ. avaṉ, maliyiṭattum nilattum marakkiḷikaḷiṭattum cuṉikaḷiṭattum pūtta paṉṉiṟa malarkaḷāl toṭutta mālikaḷiyum tāḻampūviyum muṭiyil aṇintu murukaṉ pōl viḷaṅkiṉāṉ; cantaṉam pūciya avaṉatu mārpiṭattuppala āparaṇaṅkaḷ viḷaṅkiṉa; ceṉṉiyilē cuṟṟiya piccimāliyum kātiṟ cerukiya acōkantaḷirum aḻaku ceytaṉa. avaṉ āṭiyiṉmēl tulaṅkumpaṭi kaccuk kaṭṭiyiruntāṉ. pūṇ iṟukap peṟṟa kiyil villiyum ampi yum ēnti niṉṟāṉ. avaṉ, taṉṉoṭetirtta ēṟukaḷi ellām veṉṟu putiya āvikkāṇum ēṟupōla nammuṉ tōṉṟiṉāṉ. mūṅkil muki pōṉṟa veḷḷiya paṟkaḷuṭiya nāykaḷ imi koṭṭātu eṅkaḷip pārttu vaḷintu varalāyiṉa. yām mikunta accam aṭintēmāy vēṟu iṭattē cellā niṉṟōm. appōtu apperuntakiyām veruvutaṟku añci iṉiya moḻikaḷic colli ematu aḻakip pukaḻnturittāṉ. piṉṉar, “iḷiyīr” eṉa emmi viḷittu, “ivviṭattē kaṇikkut tappi vanta yāṉiyip pārttatuṇṭō?” eṉṟu kēṭṭāṉ. ataṟku yām patil etuvum kūṟātu niṉṟōm. atukaṇṭu apperuntaki “melliyalīr tappi ōṭivanta vilaṅkik kāṭṭittārīrāyiṉum eṉṉuṭaṉ oru vārtti pēcutal nuṅkaṭkup paḻiyākumō” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ; taḻi paranta pūkka ḷuṭiya kompari muṟittup parikkōli matiyāta matayāṉipōla ōccik kalleṉṉum ōcipaṭak kurikkum vēṭṭi nāykaḷiṉ ōciyi māṟṟi yāṅkaḷ vārtti colvatōr kālatti etirpārttu niṉṟāṉ.
avaṉ nammi yāṉiyiṉiṉṟum kāttāṉ
iḵtu ivvāṟākak kuṟiya kālkaḷiyum tiṉi vikkōliṉāl vēynta kūriyiyum uṭiya kuṭiciyilirukkum kuṟamakaṉ piṇi pōṉṟa nōkkiyuṭiya maṉivi tāṉē camitta kaḷḷi eṭuttuk koṭukka ataṉi uṇṭu tiṉip puṉattik kāval ceyya maṟantāṉ. ākavē yāṉi tiṉippuṉatti aḻittatu. ataṉikkaṇṭu varuttam mikuntavaṉākiya kuṟavaṉ pāmpiṉ vaṭiviṉatāki villi vaḷittu ampi eytu, taṭṭiyit taṭṭi, vāyi maṭittuc cīḻkki aṭittu yāṉiyip puṉattiṉiṉṟum veruṭṭiṉāṉ. kōpam mikunta yāṉi maraṅkaḷi muṟittu iṭiyēṟupōl āravārañceytu kiyi nilattil aṭittu emaṉip pōla eṅkaḷmītu neruṅki vantatu. nām ataṉikkaṇṭu naṭukkamuṟṟu uyir piḻikkum ōriṭattip peṟavēṇṭi nāṇik kiviṭṭu vaḷikaḷ olippa akkuricilic cārntu teyvamēṟiṉa mayilpōla naṭuṅki niṉṟōm. akkuricil villi naṉṟāka vaḷintu yāṉiyiṉ mukattil ampic celuttiṉāṉ. puḷḷiyiṉiyum pukariṉiyumuṭiya mattakattil ampupaṭṭu uruviṉa puṇṇiliruntu, murukaṉāl varuttamuṟṟa makaḷirkku veṟiyāṭu kaḷattil kuruti kutikkumāṟu irattam perukiṟṟu. avvēḻam avviṭattil niṟṟal āṟṟātu puṟaṅkoṭuttu ōṭiṟṟu.
āṟṟōṭṭam eṅkaḷi iḻuttuc ceṉṟatu
ataṉpiṉ nām, murukavēḷukku maliyiluṟiyum teyvamakaḷir kikōttāṭuvatu pōṉṟu kaṭampiṉ ariyiṉi neruṅkac cūḻnta mālipōl ki kōttukkoṇṭu āṟṟiṉ perukkilē kutittu viḷiyāṭiṉōm. iṭikariyiṉiṉṟa vāḻi maratti iḻuppatu pōla āṟṟōṭṭam eṅkaḷi iḻuttuc ceṉṟatu. atu kaṇṭu aḻakiya māli aṇinta āṇṭaki virintu vantu eṅkaḷi eṭuttut taliviyip pārttu, “niṉatu aḻakiya nalatti nukarvēṉ, niṉṉi nīṅkuvēṉeṉac ciṟitu mañcātē” eṉṟu coṉṉāṉ; avaḷiṉ oḷi poruntiya neṟṟiyit tuṭittu neṭunāḷ ikkaḷavoḻukkam nikaḻavēṇṭumeṉa niṉittu eṉ mukatti nōkkic cirittāṉ. aṅṅaṉam avaṉ aṇukiya pōtu avaḷukku iyalpākiya nāṇamum accamum avviṭattu vantu tōṉṟukiyiṉālē viriya avaṉiṭattiṉiṉṟum nīṅka muyaṉṟāḷ; avaṉ viṭātavaṉāy aṅṅaṉam niṉṟa niliyilē kiyālē aṇittu ivaḷ mārpu taṉ mārpilē oṭuṅkumpaṭi taḻuviṉāṉ.
mayilkaḷ kayiṟṟil ēṟi āṭumakaḷ pōl taḷarum mali nāṭu
miḷaku paḻam cintikkiṭakkum nīṇṭa pāṟiyiṭattu māmpaḻaṅkaḷum palāppaḻaṅkaḷum utirntu kiṭakkum. ivaṟṟiṉ cāṟṟiṉāl muṟṟiya kaṭṭeḷivi nīreṉak karuti mayilkaḷ uṇṇum; uṇṭu ūrkaḷiṉ naṭuvē koṇṭāṭum viḻākkaḷil vāttiyaṅkaḷ olippak kayiṟṟilē ēṟi āṭukiṉṟa makaḷir tāḷattiṉāl taḷarumāṟu pōlat taḷarum. ivviyalpiṉa tākiya mali nāṭṭiyuṭiyavaṉ tāṉ muyaṅkutalāl ivaḷ uḷḷattu virumpuvatu mēl varintu koṇṭu illaṟam naṭattuvatāyirukku meṉṟu uṭkoṇṭāṉ; palarum vantu uṇṇumpaṭi akaṉṟa katavu tiṟantu kiṭakkiṉṟa vāyiliyuṭiya celvamikka nakariṭattu miṭākkaḷil varuvārk kellām iṭumpaṭi cōṟu camikkappeṟum. ivvāṟu celvamikka maṉi polivu peṟumpaṭi uyarnta ematu cuṟṟattiṉar viruntuṇṭu eñciya niṇamoḻukum neymikka cōṟṟi nī iṭa nāṉuṇṇal uyarntatākum” eṉṟu kūṟiṉāṉ.
mālik kālam
avaṉ maliyiṭattuḷḷa murukaṉik ki toḻutu piriyēṉ eṉakkūṟi avviṭattuḷḷa teḷinta aruvi nīrik kuṭittuc cūḷuṟavu ceytāṉ. tēvarkaḷum virumpum kukikaḷiluḷḷa pūñcōliyilē kaḷiṟu kūṭṭiṉa kūṭṭatti aṉṟip pakaṟ poḻutellām pōkkiṉāṉ. ñāyiṟu ēḻu kutiri pūṇṭi tēri yēṟip pakaṟ kālam kaḻiyumpaṭi mēṟku maliyil maṟintatu. māṉ kūṭṭam marattaṭiyiṟ tiraṇṭatu. pacukkūṭṭaṅkaḷ kaṉṟukaḷi aḻikkum kuraluṭiyaṉavāy maṉṟukaḷ niṟiyumpaṭi pukuntaṉa. kompu pōṉṟa vaḷinta vāyiyuṭiya aṉṟil uyarnta periya paṉiyiṉ uḷ maṭalilēyiruntu pēṭṭi aḻittatu. pāmpu tāṉ mēytal kāraṇamākat taṉṉiṭattuḷḷa māṇikkatti īṉṟatu. iṭiyar pala iṭaṅkaḷiṉiṉṟum āmpal eṉṉum paṇṇiṉiyuṭiya aḻakiya iṉiya kuḻaliṭattut teḷinta ōciyip palakālum parappiṉar. āmpaliṉ aḻakiya itaḻkaḷ muṟukkaviḻntaṉa. pārppārantik kālattuc ceyyum toḻilkaḷic ceytaṉar. celvamuṭiya maṉikaḷil vaḷikaḷi aṇinta makaḷir viḷakki ēṟṟi antikkālak kaṭamikaḷic ceytaṉar. kāṭṭil vāḻvār viṇṇittīṇṭum paraṇiliruntu tīkkaṭi kōlālē neruppip piṟappittu erittaṉar. mēkam periya maliyiṭattic cūḻntu iruṇṭatu. kāṭṭiluḷḷa vilaṅkukaḷellām kalleṉṉum ōci yuṭiyavāy oṉṟiyoṉṟu māṟik kūppiṭṭaṉa. paṟavikaḷ kāṭukaḷi liruntu olittaṉa. ivvāṟu kōpitta vēntaṉuṭiya pōrip pōla iruṇṭa mālik kālam vantatu.
talivi varintu pōvati virumpiṉāḷ
“viḷaṅkukiṉṟa pūṇiṉiyuṭiyāy! uṉatu cuṟṟattār uṉatu muṉkiyip piṭittu namakkuttara nāṭṭiluḷḷār ellōmum aṟiyum nalla kaliyāṇattip piṉpu nikaḻttuvōm. kaḷavoḻukkattāṟ peṟum pēriṉpam peṟutaṟku iṅṅaṉam cila nāḷ oḻukiṉēm eṉṟu niṉintu neñcu kalaṅkā tiruppāy” eṉa avaḷ neñcil varuttan tīrumpaṭi coṉṉāṉ; collip pacuvōṭu niṟkum kaṉṟupōla viṭāmal emmōṭu kūṭa vantu nammūr vāyililuḷḷa nīruṇṇum tuṟiyil emmiviṭṭu mīṇṭāṉ. accērkki toṭaṅkiya an nāṭṭōṉṟiya viruppattōṭē ennāḷum avaṉ iravuk kuṟiyiṭattē varuvāṉ. aṅṅaṉam varum pōtellām ūr kāvalar kaṭukik kāttārāyiṉum, kōpamuṭiya nāy kurittatāyiṉum, nī tuyiluṇarntā yāyiṉum, nilavu veḷippaṭṭu eṟittatāyiṉum, mūṅkili otta avaḷ melliya tōḷil peṟum iṉiya tuyilip peṟātu pōvāṉ. nām ceyyāta kuṟiyik kuṟiyeṉak karutit taliviyi etirppaṭātu ceṉṟālum avaṉ maṟupaṭiyum namatu maṉikku varuvataṟku veṟuppaṭiya māṭṭāṉ. avaṉ iḷamip paruvattik kaṭantavaṉum allaṉ; naṟkuṇaṅkaḷ māṟutaṟkuriya celvac cerukkāl taṉkulattukkuriya naṟkuṇaṅkaḷil ennāḷum nīṅkiyavaṉumallaṉ. piṟar kūṟum paḻimoḻi mutaliyavaṟṟāl iravuk kuṟiyilē kūṭutaṟku varutal oḻukka maṉṟeṉṟu niṉittu avaṉ varintu koṇṭu illaṟam naṭattutalē nalloḻukkameṉṟu talivi tuṇintāḷ. ataṉāl ivaḷuṭiya matartta kaṇkaḷ kalaṅkiṉa. piṉṉar ivaḷ, avar varukiṉṟa maliyiṭam puli, yāḷi mutaliyaṉa tuṉpam ceyvaṉavāyirukku meṉṟu niṉittāḷ. appōtu avaḷ valiyilakappaṭṭa mayilip pōlat taṉatu nalam pōmpaṭi melintu, kaṇkaḷil vīḻkiṉṟa nīr nāḷ tōṟum mārpilē tuḷippak kalaṅkā niṉṟāḷ. itukāṇ nalviṉi nikaḻnta vaṇṇam. (ivvāṟu tōḻi naṟṟāykku aṟattoṭu niṉṟāḷ.)
9. 1paṭṭiṉappāli
kāviri āṟu vaṟṟāta vaḷamuṭiyatu
kuṟṟamillāta pukaḻiyuṭiya kāviriyāṟu mēṟkut toṭarcci maliyiṟ ṟōṉṟik kaṭaliṭattē pāyum. veḷḷi eṉṉum viṇmītu tāṉ niṟṟaṟkuriya vaṭaticiyil nillātu teṉ ticikkaṇ cēriṉum taṉṉip pāṭukiṉṟatum tuḷiyākiya uṇavi uṭiyatumākiya vāṉampāṭi maḻit tuḷiyip peṟātu pularumpaṭi vāṉam poyppiṉum poṉkoḻikkum kāviriyiṭattil nīr vaṟaḷātu. ivvakic ciṟappaminta āṟṟiṉ kari yiṭattē cōḻa vaḷanāṭuḷḷatu.
maruta nilam
maruta nilattē oṉṟaṟkoṉṟu aṇmiyiluḷḷa ūrkaḷuṇṭu. vayalkaḷ mikka viḷivik koṭukkum. āṅkāṅku iṭappaṭṭuḷḷa karup pañcāṟu kāyccum koṭṭilkaḷil eḻunta tīmpuki paṭutalāl vayaliṭattē yuḷḷa neytaṟpū vāṭum. cenneṟ katiri uṇṭu vayiṟu parutta erumikaḷ āṅkāṅku kiṭakkum neṟkutirkaḷiṉ niḻalil uṟaṅkum. viḷivu nīṅkāta akaṉṟa kaḻaṉikaḷil kulitteṅkum, kuli vāḻiyum, kāykkamukum, maṇanāṟum mañcaḷum, pala iṉa maraṅkaḷum, kulikaḷiyuṭiya paṉiyum, paranta aṭiyiṉiyuṭiya cēmpum, muḷiyiṉiyuṭiya iñciyum neruṅki vaḷarum.
muṟṟattē ciṟuvar tēruruṭṭi viḷiyāṭuvar
cīṟūrkaḷiṭattē maṉikaḷiṉ muṟṟattu ularavitta nellik kōḻi tiṉṉātapaṭi oḷḷiya nutalum aḻakumuṭiya ciṟu peṇkaḷ kāvalāka iruppar. avarkaḷ nellit tiṉṉum kōḻikaḷit tamatu poṉṉāṟ ceyta makarak kuḻikaḷāl eṟintu ōṭṭuvar. kāliṭattē taṇṭi aṇinta ciṟār kutiri pūṭṭāmaṟ kiyāluruṭṭum mūṉṟu uruḷikaḷuṭiya ciṟu tērkaḷi muṟṟattē uruṭṭi viḷiyāṭuvar. ammarak kuḻikaḷ attēruruḷkaḷi uruḷātapaṭi taṭukkum.
